```rust
        assert_eq!((o.kind, o.answer_path, o.ready, o.reusable_key), (RequestKind::Permission, AnswerPath::Hook, true, true));
        assert_eq!(o.title, "Bash");
        assert_eq!(o.detail, "touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt");
        let ids: Vec<_> = o.options.iter().map(|x| (x.id.as_str(), x.effect)).collect();
        assert_eq!(ids, vec![("allow", Effect::Allow), ("allow_always", Effect::AllowAlways), ("deny", Effect::Deny)]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn allow_always_shows_the_exact_rule_it_will_write() {
        let p = payload("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
        let o = claude_permission("a", k(), None, &p);
        let always = o.options.iter().find(|x| x.effect == Effect::AllowAlways).unwrap();
        let d = always.description.as_deref().unwrap();
        let rule = crate::hooks::wire::always_rule(&p).unwrap();
        assert!(d.contains(&rule_text(&rule)), "{d}");
        assert_eq!(rule_text(&rule), "Bash(touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt)");
        assert!(d.contains(".claude/settings.local.json") && d.contains("outside"), "{d}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_rule_that_cannot_be_shown_whole_is_not_offered() {
        for cmd in ["x".repeat(5000), "echo \u{1b}[31m".to_string(), "a\nb".to_string(), "rm \u{202E}x".to_string()] {
            let mut p = payload("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
            p.tool_input["command"] = cmd.clone().into();
            let o = claude_permission("a", k(), None, &p);
            assert!(o.options.iter().all(|x| x.effect != Effect::AllowAlways), "{cmd:?}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn claude_keys_after_the_hold_drop_allow_always_and_use_the_no_option() {
        let dialog = crate::herdr::screen::find_dialog(&screen("claude-permission-bash.txt")).unwrap();
        let keys = claude_permission_keys(&dialog);
        assert_eq!(keys.get("allow").map(String::as_str), Some("1"));
        assert_eq!(keys.get("deny").map(String::as_str), Some("4"), "option 4 is \"No\" in the fixture");
        assert_eq!(keys.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(claude_keys_options().len(), 2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn single_question_is_answerable_only_when_bound() {
        let p = payload("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");
        let bound = claude_question("a", k(), None, &p, true).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((bound.kind, bound.answer_path, bound.ready), (RequestKind::Question, AnswerPath::Keys, false));
        assert_eq!(bound.options.iter().map(|o| o.label.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec!["Red", "Blue"]);
        assert_eq!(bound.options[0].id, "o1");
        assert_eq!(bound.questions.as_ref().unwrap()[0].text, "Pick a color");
        assert!(!bound.free_text, "Type something and Chat about this are never options");
        let unbound = claude_question("a", k(), None, &p, false).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(unbound.answer_path, AnswerPath::None);
        assert_eq!(question_keys(2), [("o1".to_string(), "1".to_string()), ("o2".to_string(), "2".to_string())].into());
    }

    #[test]
    fn malformed_questions_are_read_only() {
        for broken in [
            serde_json::json!([]),
            serde_json::json!([{"question": "Q", "header": "H", "options": []}]),
            serde_json::json!([{"question": "Q", "header": "H", "options": [{"label": 5}]}]),
            serde_json::json!([{"question": "Q", "options": [{"label": "A"}]}]),
            serde_json::json!([{"question": "", "header": "H", "options": [{"label": "A"}]}]),
            serde_json::json!([{"question": "Q", "header": "H", "multiSelect": "no", "options": [{"label": "A"}]}]),
            serde_json::json!([{"question": "Q", "header": "H", "options": [{"label": "A"}]}, 7]),
        ] {
            let mut p = payload("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");
            p.tool_input["questions"] = broken.clone();
            assert!(question_specs(&p.tool_input).is_err(), "{broken}");
            let o = claude_question("a", k(), None, &p, true).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(o.answer_path, AnswerPath::None, "{broken}");
            assert!(!o.options.iter().any(|_| true), "no options from a malformed input");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn multi_select_or_many_questions_stay_read_only() {
        let mut p = payload("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");
        p.tool_input["questions"][0]["multiSelect"] = true.into();
        assert_eq!(claude_question("a", k(), None, &p, true).unwrap().answer_path, AnswerPath::None);
        let mut p = payload("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");
        let q = p.tool_input["questions"][0].clone();
        p.tool_input["questions"].as_array_mut().unwrap().push(q);
        assert_eq!(claude_question("a", k(), None, &p, true).unwrap().answer_path, AnswerPath::None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn codex_permission_uses_keys_and_shows_the_command_then_the_reason() {
        let o = codex_permission("a", k(), None, &payload("codex/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), false);
        assert_eq!((o.answer_path, o.ready, o.reusable_key), (AnswerPath::Keys, false, true));
        assert!(o.detail.starts_with("touch ~/acp0_test_1.txt\n"));
        assert!(o.detail.contains("outside the writable workspace"));
        assert_eq!(o.options.iter().map(|x| x.id.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec!["allow", "allow_always", "deny"]);
        let collapsed = codex_permission("a", k(), None, &payload("codex/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), true);
        assert!(collapsed.detail.ends_with("Enlarge the pane to answer here."));
    }

    #[test]
    fn hostile_text_is_cleaned_and_capped() {
        let mut p = payload("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
        p.tool_name = Some("Ba\u{1b}[31msh\u{202E}".into());
        p.tool_input["command"] = format!("rm -rf /\u{7}{}", "x".repeat(5000)).into();
        let o = claude_permission("a", k(), None, &p);
        assert_eq!(o.title, "Ba[31msh");
        assert!(o.detail.chars().count() <= DETAIL_CHARS && !o.detail.contains('\u{7}'));
    }

    #[test]
    fn logged_payloads_are_redacted() {
        let s = payload_for_log(br#"{"tool_input":{"command":"GITHUB_TOKEN=ghp_secret make"}}"#);
        assert!(!s.contains("ghp_secret"), "{s}");
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Hook payloads, names and replies (spec §6.1 to §6.3). Payloads are read
//! field by field: a field of the wrong type is absent, never fatal, so a
//! future Claude or Codex version fails open instead of breaking acd.
use acd_core::CorrelationKey;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};

pub const MAX_ID_BYTES: usize = 128;
pub const MAX_HINT_BYTES: usize = 1024;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub enum HookAgent {
    Claude,
    Codex,
}

impl HookAgent {
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Option<HookAgent> {
        match s {
            "claude" => Some(HookAgent::Claude),
            "codex" => Some(HookAgent::Codex),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum HookName {
    SessionStart,
    UserPromptSubmit,
    PermissionRequest,
    PreToolUse,
    PostToolUse,
    Stop,
    SessionEnd,
    /// Notification and anything newer: logged and ignored.
    Other,
}

impl HookName {
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> HookName {
        match s {
            "SessionStart" => HookName::SessionStart,
            "UserPromptSubmit" => HookName::UserPromptSubmit,
            "PermissionRequest" => HookName::PermissionRequest,
            "PreToolUse" => HookName::PreToolUse,
            "PostToolUse" => HookName::PostToolUse,
            "Stop" => HookName::Stop,
            "SessionEnd" => HookName::SessionEnd,
            _ => HookName::Other,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
pub struct Payload {
    pub session_id: String,
    pub prompt_id: Option<String>,
    pub turn_id: Option<String>,
    pub cwd: Option<String>,
    pub tool_name: Option<String>,
    pub tool_input: Value,
    pub tool_response: Value,
    pub permission_suggestions: Vec<Value>,
    pub last_assistant_message: Option<String>,
    /// Present on subagent payloads, which are always dropped (spec §6.2).
    pub agent_id: Option<String>,
}

/// 1 to 128 bytes, no control characters.
pub fn valid_id(s: &str) -> bool {
    !s.is_empty() && s.len() <= MAX_ID_BYTES && !s.chars().any(char::is_control)
}

impl Payload {
    pub fn from_value(v: &Value) -> Option<Payload> {
        let o = v.as_object()?;
        let s = |k: &str| o.get(k).and_then(Value::as_str).map(str::to_owned);
        let id = |k: &str| s(k).filter(|x| valid_id(x));
        Some(Payload {
            session_id: id("session_id")?,
            prompt_id: id("prompt_id"),
            turn_id: id("turn_id"),
            cwd: s("cwd"),
            tool_name: s("tool_name"),
            tool_input: o.get("tool_input").cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null),
            tool_response: o.get("tool_response").cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null),
            permission_suggestions: o
                .get("permission_suggestions")
                .and_then(Value::as_array)
                .cloned()
                .unwrap_or_default(),
            last_assistant_message: s("last_assistant_message"),
            agent_id: s("agent_id"),
        })
    }

    pub fn is_subagent(&self) -> bool {
        self.agent_id.as_deref().is_some_and(|a| !a.is_empty())
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Hints {
    pub herdr_pane: Option<String>,
    pub herdr_socket: Option<String>,
    pub entrypoint: Option<String>,
}

/// A header hint, or `None` when empty, too long or holding control chars.
pub fn hint(v: Option<&str>) -> Option<String> {
    v.filter(|s| !s.is_empty() && s.len() <= MAX_HINT_BYTES && !s.chars().any(char::is_control))
        .map(str::to_owned)
}

impl Hints {
    pub fn from_headers(h: &axum::http::HeaderMap) -> Hints {
        let get = |name: &str| hint(h.get(name).and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok()));
        Hints {
            herdr_pane: get("x-herdr-pane"),
            herdr_socket: get("x-herdr-socket"),
            entrypoint: get("x-claude-entrypoint"),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct HookEvent {
    pub agent: HookAgent,
    pub name: HookName,
    pub payload: Payload,
    pub hints: Hints,
    pub at: tokio::time::Instant,
}

/// Compact JSON with object keys sorted at every level.
pub fn canonical_json(v: &Value) -> String {
    fn write(v: &Value, out: &mut String) {
        match v {
            Value::Object(m) => {
                let mut keys: Vec<&String> = m.keys().collect();
                keys.sort();
                out.push('{');
                for (i, k) in keys.iter().enumerate() {
                    if i > 0 {
                        out.push(',');
                    }
                    out.push_str(&Value::String((*k).clone()).to_string());
                    out.push(':');
                    write(&m[*k], out);
                }
                out.push('}');
            }
            Value::Array(a) => {
                out.push('[');
                for (i, x) in a.iter().enumerate() {
                    if i > 0 {
                        out.push(',');
                    }
                    write(x, out);
                }
                out.push(']');
            }
            other => out.push_str(&other.to_string()),
        }
    }
    let mut s = String::new();
    write(v, &mut s);
    s
}

/// Lowercase hex `sha256(tool_name ‖ 0x00 ‖ canonical_json(tool_input))`.
pub fn tool_digest(tool_name: &str, input: &Value) -> String {
    let mut h = Sha256::new();
    h.update(tool_name.as_bytes());
    h.update([0u8]);
    h.update(canonical_json(input).as_bytes());
    h.finalize().iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect()
}

pub fn command_of(input: &Value) -> Option<&str> {
    input.get("command").and_then(Value::as_str)
}

/// `claude:<session>:<prompt_id>:<digest>` (D2).
pub fn claude_key(p: &Payload) -> Option<CorrelationKey> {
    Some(CorrelationKey(format!(
        "claude:{}:{}:{}",
        p.session_id,
        p.prompt_id.as_deref()?,
        tool_digest(p.tool_name.as_deref()?, &p.tool_input)
    )))
}

/// `codex:<session>:<turn_id>:<digest>` (spec §5.1).
pub fn codex_key(p: &Payload) -> Option<CorrelationKey> {
    Some(CorrelationKey(format!(
        "codex:{}:{}:{}",
        p.session_id,
        p.turn_id.as_deref()?,
        tool_digest(p.tool_name.as_deref()?, &p.tool_input)
    )))
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct AlwaysRule {
    pub tool_name: String,
    pub rule_content: String,
}

/// The single rule construction used for display and delivery (ruling Q2):
/// a Bash request's exact command, else the first `addRules` suggestion.
pub fn always_rule(p: &Payload) -> Option<AlwaysRule> {
    if p.tool_name.as_deref() == Some("Bash")
        && let Some(c) = command_of(&p.tool_input)
    {
        return Some(AlwaysRule { tool_name: "Bash".into(), rule_content: c.to_owned() });
    }
    p.permission_suggestions.iter().find_map(|s| {
        if s.get("type")?.as_str()? != "addRules" {
            return None;
        }
        let r = s.get("rules")?.as_array()?.first()?;
        Some(AlwaysRule {
            tool_name: r.get("toolName")?.as_str()?.to_owned(),
            rule_content: r.get("ruleContent")?.as_str()?.to_owned(),
        })
    })
}

fn envelope(decision: Value) -> String {
    json!({"hookSpecificOutput": {"hookEventName": "PermissionRequest", "decision": decision}}).to_string()
}

pub fn reply_allow() -> String {
    envelope(json!({"behavior": "allow"}))
}

pub fn reply_deny() -> String {
    envelope(json!({"behavior": "deny", "message": "Denied from agent inbox"}))
}

/// Allow, plus the suggestion's rule persisted to `localSettings`, the only
/// destination P0 and gate 2e saw persist.
pub fn reply_allow_always(r: &AlwaysRule) -> String {
    envelope(json!({
        "behavior": "allow",
        "updatedPermissions": [{
            "type": "addRules",
            "rules": [{"toolName": r.tool_name, "ruleContent": r.rule_content}],
            "behavior": "allow",
            "destination": "localSettings"
        }]
    }))
}
```

```rust
//! Hook payloads to registry observations (spec §6.2, §6.3).
use std::collections::HashMap;

use acd_core::{CorrelationKey, RequestObs};
use acd_protocol::{AnswerPath, Effect, Question, RequestKind, RequestOption};
use serde_json::Value;

use super::wire::{AlwaysRule, HookAgent, HookName, Payload, always_rule, canonical_json, command_of};
use crate::herdr::screen::Dialog;
use crate::text::{TITLE_CHARS, cap_chars, clean_display, clean_screen, redact_log};

pub const DETAIL_CHARS: usize = 4000;
const DESCRIPTION_CHARS: usize = 400;
pub const CODEX_KEYS: [(&str, &str); 3] = [("allow", "1"), ("allow_always", "2"), ("deny", "esc")];

fn opt(id: &str, label: &str, effect: Effect, description: Option<String>) -> RequestOption {
    RequestOption { id: id.into(), label: label.into(), description, effect }
}

fn detail(raw: &str) -> String {
    cap_chars(clean_screen(raw).trim(), DETAIL_CHARS)
}

/// The command, the file path, or compact JSON (spec §6.2 step 1).
pub fn detail_of(input: &Value) -> String {
    if let Some(c) = command_of(input) {
        return detail(c);
    }
    if let Some(f) = input.get("file_path").and_then(Value::as_str) {
        return detail(f);
    }
    detail(&canonical_json(input))
}

pub fn claude_permission(
    agent_id: &str,
    key: CorrelationKey,
    upgrade_from: Option<CorrelationKey>,
    p: &Payload,
) -> RequestObs {
    let mut options = vec![opt("allow", "Yes", Effect::Allow, None), opt("deny", "No", Effect::Deny, None)];
    // The same `AlwaysRule` is stored with the hold and written by
    // `deliver` (Task 14), so what the client shows is what persists.
    if let Some(rule) = always_rule(p)
        && rule_displayable(&rule_text(&rule))
    {
        options.insert(
            1,
            opt(
                "allow_always",
                "Yes, always",
                Effect::AllowAlways,
                Some(format!(
                    "Adds the rule {} to .claude/settings.local.json in this project. Paths outside this directory may still prompt.",
                    rule_text(&rule)
                )),
            ),
        );
    }
    RequestObs {
        agent_id: agent_id.into(),
        key,
        upgrade_from,
        kind: RequestKind::Permission,
        answer_path: AnswerPath::Hook,
        title: clean_display(p.tool_name.as_deref().unwrap_or("Permission"), TITLE_CHARS),
        detail: detail_of(&p.tool_input),
        options,
        questions: None,
        free_text: false,
        raw_screen: None,
        ready: true,
        reusable_key: true,
    }
}

/// Options of a Claude permission request on the keys path (D9).
pub fn claude_keys_options() -> Vec<RequestOption> {
    vec![opt("allow", "Yes", Effect::Allow, None), opt("deny", "No", Effect::Deny, None)]
}

/// `allow` is option 1; `deny` is the option labelled `No`, else `esc`.
pub fn claude_permission_keys(dialog: &Dialog) -> HashMap<String, String> {
    let no = dialog
        .options
        .iter()
        .find(|o| o.label.trim() == "No")
        .map(|o| o.number.to_string())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "esc".into());
    [("allow".to_string(), "1".to_string()), ("deny".to_string(), no)].into()
}

pub fn rule_text(r: &AlwaysRule) -> String {
    format!("{}({})", r.tool_name, r.rule_content)
}

/// Shown whole or not offered at all: no control, bidi or separator
/// characters (they would be stripped or reorder the text), at most 4000
/// chars (the detail cap).
pub fn rule_displayable(text: &str) -> bool {
    text.chars().count() <= DETAIL_CHARS && clean_display(text, usize::MAX) == text
}

/// One question exactly as Claude sent it (raw strings, not cleaned).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct QuestionSpec {
    pub header: String,
    pub question: String,
    pub multi_select: bool,
    /// `(label, description)` in order.
    pub options: Vec<(String, Option<String>)>,
}

/// Full structural validation of `tool_input.questions` (D25): a
/// non-empty array of objects, each with a non-empty string `question`,
/// a string `header`, an optional boolean `multiSelect`, and a non-empty
/// `options` array of objects with a non-empty string `label` and an
/// optional string `description`. Any violation rejects the whole input.
pub fn question_specs(input: &Value) -> Result<Vec<QuestionSpec>, &'static str> {
    let qs = input.get("questions").and_then(Value::as_array).ok_or("questions is not an array")?;
    if qs.is_empty() {
        return Err("no questions");
    }
    qs.iter()
        .map(|q| {
            let q = q.as_object().ok_or("a question is not an object")?;
            let question = q.get("question").and_then(Value::as_str).filter(|s| !s.is_empty()).ok_or("question text")?;
            let header = q.get("header").and_then(Value::as_str).ok_or("header")?;
            let multi_select = match q.get("multiSelect") {
                None => false,
                Some(v) => v.as_bool().ok_or("multiSelect")?,
            };
            let opts = q.get("options").and_then(Value::as_array).filter(|a| !a.is_empty()).ok_or("options")?;
            let options = opts
                .iter()
                .map(|o| {
                    let o = o.as_object().ok_or("an option is not an object")?;
                    let label = o.get("label").and_then(Value::as_str).filter(|s| !s.is_empty()).ok_or("label")?;
                    let description = match o.get("description") {
                        None => None,
                        Some(d) => Some(d.as_str().ok_or("description")?.to_owned()),
                    };
                    Ok((label.to_owned(), description))
                })
                .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, &'static str>>()?;
            Ok(QuestionSpec { header: header.to_owned(), question: question.to_owned(), multi_select, options })
        })
        .collect()
}

pub fn question_keys(n: usize) -> HashMap<String, String> {
    (1..=n.min(9)).map(|i| (format!("o{i}"), i.to_string())).collect()
}

/// `bound`: the session is bound to a herdr pane. Only a single,
/// single-select question with 1 to 9 options on a bound session is
/// answerable (spec §6.2 Questions); its path is `keys`, not ready until
/// the dialog is captured.
pub fn claude_question(
    agent_id: &str,
    key: CorrelationKey,
    upgrade_from: Option<CorrelationKey>,
    p: &Payload,
    bound: bool,
) -> Option<RequestObs> {
    p.tool_input.get("questions")?;
    let Ok(specs) = question_specs(&p.tool_input) else {
        // D25: shown, never answerable, no options derived from bad input.
        return Some(RequestObs {
            agent_id: agent_id.into(),
            key,
            upgrade_from,
            kind: RequestKind::Question,
            answer_path: AnswerPath::None,
            title: "Question".into(),
            detail: "The question could not be read. Answer it in the terminal.".into(),
            options: vec![],
            questions: None,
            free_text: false,
            raw_screen: None,
            ready: false,
            reusable_key: true,
        });
    };
    let questions: Vec<Question> = specs
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .map(|(qi, q)| Question {
            id: format!("q{}", qi + 1),
            header: Some(clean_display(&q.header, TITLE_CHARS)),
            text: detail(&q.question),
            multi_select: q.multi_select,
            options: q
                .options
                .iter()
                .enumerate()
                .map(|(i, (label, d))| {
                    opt(&format!("o{}", i + 1), &clean_display(label, TITLE_CHARS), Effect::Choose,
                        d.as_deref().map(|d| clean_display(d, DESCRIPTION_CHARS)))
                })
                .collect(),
        })
        .collect();
    let first = questions.first()?.clone();
    let answerable = bound && questions.len() == 1 && !first.multi_select && (1..=9).contains(&first.options.len());
    Some(RequestObs {
        agent_id: agent_id.into(),
        key,
        upgrade_from,
        kind: RequestKind::Question,
        answer_path: if answerable { AnswerPath::Keys } else { AnswerPath::None },
        title: first.header.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "Question".into()),
        detail: first.text.clone(),
        options: first.options.clone(),
        questions: Some(questions),
        free_text: false,
        raw_screen: None,
        ready: false,
        reusable_key: true,
    })
}

/// spec §6.3 `PermissionRequest`. `collapsed`: the pane is too short and
/// the dialog hides the command, so it can never be matched.
pub fn codex_permission(
    agent_id: &str,
    key: CorrelationKey,
    upgrade_from: Option<CorrelationKey>,
    p: &Payload,
    collapsed: bool,
) -> RequestObs {
    let mut text = command_of(&p.tool_input).unwrap_or_default().to_owned();
    if let Some(d) = p.tool_input.get("description").and_then(Value::as_str) {
        text.push('\n');
        text.push_str(d);
    }
    let mut detail = detail(&text);
    if collapsed {
        detail.push_str("\n\nEnlarge the pane to answer here.");
    }
    RequestObs {
        agent_id: agent_id.into(),
        key,
        upgrade_from,
        kind: RequestKind::Permission,
        answer_path: AnswerPath::Keys,
        title: clean_display(p.tool_name.as_deref().unwrap_or("Permission"), TITLE_CHARS),
        detail,
        options: vec![
            opt("allow", "Yes", Effect::Allow, None),
            opt("allow_always", "Yes, don't ask again", Effect::AllowAlways, None),
            opt("deny", "No, and tell Codex what to do differently", Effect::Deny, None),
        ],
        questions: None,
        free_text: false,
        raw_screen: None,
        ready: false,
        reusable_key: true,
    }
}

pub fn payload_for_log(raw: &[u8]) -> String {
    redact_log(&String::from_utf8_lossy(raw))
}

/// Payloads only at debug, redacted and truncated (spec §7.1 Logs).
pub fn log_payload(agent: HookAgent, name: &HookName, raw: &[u8]) {
    if tracing::enabled!(tracing::Level::DEBUG) {
        tracing::debug!(?agent, ?name, payload = %payload_for_log(raw), "hook payload");
    }
}
```

### Task 8: Hook endpoint: auth, generations, bounds, holds, close detection

GATE 2: an empty header value counts as absent, because an unlisted
variable in a Claude header interpolates to `""` (2a). The
close-detection test relies on Claude closing the held connection on a
terminal deny or Esc (2b); the endpoint only reports the close, Task 15
decides what it means.

Sol I8 shaped the bounds: the lifecycle queue is bounded in events and
bytes and fails open when full; holds, closes and expiries travel on a
control channel bounded by the hold cap and are never dropped; the
listener caps open connections; the rejection table is capped and logs
only an opaque id. Each accepted request carries the credential
generation it matched (Hook token store T2).

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/endpoint.rs`
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/testing.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/mod.rs` (module list)
- Test: `crates/acd-adapters/tests/hooks_endpoint.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7 (`HookAgent`, `HookName`, `Payload`, `Hints`,
  `HookEvent`, `log_payload`).
- Produces:
  - `endpoint::Credential { token: String, generation: u64 }`.
  - `endpoint::HookAuth::new(current: Credential) -> HookAuth`,
    `set(&self, current: Credential, previous: Option<Credential>)`,
    `check(&self, presented: &str) -> Option<u64>` (the matched
    generation), `note_rejected(&self, hint: Option<&str>)`,
    `rejected_since(&self, since: Instant) -> usize`,
    `rejected_entries(&self) -> usize`.
  - `endpoint::remote_id(hint: Option<&str>) -> String` (8 hex digits of
    `sha256`, or `"none"`).
  - `endpoint::HoldId = u64`.
  - `endpoint::Inbound { Event { event, generation, bytes }, Held { event, hold, reply: oneshot::Sender<String>, generation }, HoldClosed { hold }, HoldExpired { hold } }`.
  - `endpoint::EndpointOptions { permission_hold, max_holds, body_timeout, max_body, max_events, max_event_bytes, max_connections }`
    with `Default` (3600 s, 64, 5 s, 1 MiB, 512, 16 MiB, 128).
  - `endpoint::mailbox(opts: &EndpointOptions) -> (MailboxTx, Mailbox)`;
    `Mailbox { pub events: mpsc::Receiver<Inbound>, pub control: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<Inbound> }`
    with `Mailbox::release(&self, bytes: usize)` (the reconciler calls it
    for every `Event` it takes) and `Mailbox::dropped(&self) -> u64`.
  - `endpoint::router(auth: Arc<HookAuth>, tx: MailboxTx, opts: EndpointOptions) -> axum::Router`.
  - `endpoint::capped(listener: tokio::net::TcpListener, max: usize) -> CappedListener`
    (an `axum::serve::Listener`); Task 28 serves the router through it.
  - `testing::post`, `testing::send`, `testing::read_response` (feature
    `testing`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test client**

`hooks/testing.rs` (behind `#[cfg(any(test, feature = "testing"))]` in

```rust
//! A raw HTTP/1.1 client for hook tests: no client library, and it can
//! close a connection mid-hold the way Claude does.
use tokio::io::{AsyncReadExt, AsyncWriteExt};
use tokio::net::TcpStream;

pub async fn send(port: u16, path: &str, token: &str, headers: &[(&str, &str)], body: &str) -> TcpStream {
    let mut s = TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", port)).await.unwrap();
    let mut req = format!(
        "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n",
        body.len()
    );
    if !token.is_empty() {
        req.push_str(&format!("X-Acd-Token: {token}\r\n"));
    }
    for (k, v) in headers {
        req.push_str(&format!("{k}: {v}\r\n"));
    }
    req.push_str("\r\n");
    req.push_str(body);
    s.write_all(req.as_bytes()).await.unwrap();
    s
}

pub async fn read_response(mut s: TcpStream) -> (u16, String) {
    let mut buf = Vec::new();
    // A reset after the reply (unread request bytes) keeps what was read.
    let _ = s.read_to_end(&mut buf).await;
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&buf).into_owned();
    let status = text.split_whitespace().nth(1).and_then(|c| c.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
    let body = text.split_once("\r\n\r\n").map(|(_, b)| b.to_owned()).unwrap_or_default();
    (status, body)
}

pub async fn post(port: u16, path: &str, token: &str, headers: &[(&str, &str)], body: &str) -> (u16, String) {
    read_response(send(port, path, token, headers, body).await).await
}
```

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_adapters::hooks::endpoint::*;
use acd_adapters::hooks::testing::{post, read_response, send};
use acd_adapters::hooks::wire::HookName;
use tokio::time::{Instant, timeout};

const TOKEN: &str = "tok-current-0123456789";

fn cred(token: &str, generation: u64) -> Credential {
    Credential { token: token.into(), generation }
}

async fn serve(opts: EndpointOptions) -> (u16, Arc<HookAuth>, Mailbox) {
    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.unwrap();
    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let (tx, mb) = mailbox(&opts);
    let auth = Arc::new(HookAuth::new(cred(TOKEN, 1)));
    let app = router(auth.clone(), tx, opts.clone());
    tokio::spawn(async move { axum::serve(capped(listener, opts.max_connections), app).await.unwrap() });
    (port, auth, mb)
}

fn fx(rel: &str) -> String {
    std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/hooks/{rel}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
}

async fn next_event(mb: &mut Mailbox) -> Inbound {
    let m = timeout(Duration::from_secs(2), mb.events.recv()).await.expect("event in time").unwrap();
    if let Inbound::Event { bytes, .. } = &m {
        mb.release(*bytes);
    }
    m
}

async fn next_control(mb: &mut Mailbox) -> Inbound {
    timeout(Duration::from_secs(2), mb.control.recv()).await.expect("control in time").unwrap()
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn rejects_bad_tokens_methods_paths_and_agents() {
    let (port, _auth, _mb) = serve(EndpointOptions::default()).await;
    let body = fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json");
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/UserPromptSubmit", "", &[], &body).await.0, 401);
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/UserPromptSubmit", "wrong", &[], &body).await.0, 401);
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/aider/UserPromptSubmit", TOKEN, &[], &body).await.0, 401);
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/elsewhere", TOKEN, &[], &body).await.0, 401);
    let mut s = tokio::net::TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", port)).await.unwrap();
    tokio::io::AsyncWriteExt::write_all(&mut s,
        format!("GET /hook/claude/Stop HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: x\r\nX-Acd-Token: {TOKEN}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n").as_bytes()).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(read_response(s).await.0, 401);
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/UserPromptSubmit?marker=acd-managed:v1", TOKEN, &[], &body).await.0, 200, "the query is ignored");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn each_request_carries_the_generation_it_matched() {
    let (port, auth, mut mb) = serve(EndpointOptions::default()).await;
    auth.set(cred("tok-new-0123456789", 2), Some(cred(TOKEN, 1)));
    let body = fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json");
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/UserPromptSubmit", TOKEN, &[], &body).await.0, 200);
    assert!(matches!(next_event(&mut mb).await, Inbound::Event { generation: 1, .. }));
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/UserPromptSubmit", "tok-new-0123456789", &[], &body).await.0, 200);
    assert!(matches!(next_event(&mut mb).await, Inbound::Event { generation: 2, .. }));
    auth.set(cred("tok-new-0123456789", 2), None);
    let before = Instant::now();
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/UserPromptSubmit", TOKEN, &[("X-Herdr-Pane", "w1:p1")], &body).await.0, 401);
    assert_eq!(auth.rejected_since(before), 1);
}

#[test]
fn rejections_are_logged_by_an_opaque_id_and_the_table_is_capped() {
    let id = remote_id(Some("w1:p1 secret-looking value"));
    assert_eq!(id.len(), 8);
    assert!(id.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()));
    assert!(!id.contains("w1"));
    assert_eq!(remote_id(None), "none");
    let auth = HookAuth::new(cred(TOKEN, 1));
    for i in 0..300 {
        assert_eq!(auth.check(&format!("bad-{i}")), None);
        auth.note_rejected(Some(&format!("pane-{i}")));
    }
    assert!(auth.rejected_entries() <= 256);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn lifecycle_hooks_reply_at_once_without_a_consumer() {
    let (port, _auth, _mb) = serve(EndpointOptions::default()).await; // never drained
    let t = Instant::now();
    let (status, body) = post(port, "/hook/claude/Stop", TOKEN, &[], &fx("claude/payload-Stop.json")).await;
    assert_eq!((status, body.as_str()), (200, ""));
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_millis(200));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_full_event_queue_fails_open_but_never_drops_control() {
    let (port, _auth, mut mb) = serve(EndpointOptions { max_events: 2, ..Default::default() }).await;
    let stop = fx("claude/payload-Stop.json");
    for _ in 0..3 {
        assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/Stop", TOKEN, &[], &stop).await, (200, String::new()));
    }
    assert_eq!(mb.dropped(), 1, "the third event was dropped and counted");
    // Holds still get through while the event queue is full.
    let s = send(port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN, &[], &fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json")).await;
    let Inbound::Held { hold, .. } = next_control(&mut mb).await else { panic!("hold on the control channel") };
    drop(s);
    assert!(matches!(next_control(&mut mb).await, Inbound::HoldClosed { hold: h } if h == hold));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_byte_budget_bounds_queued_bodies() {
    let (port, _auth, mb) = serve(EndpointOptions { max_event_bytes: 1500, ..Default::default() }).await;
    let stop = fx("claude/payload-Stop.json"); // about 900 bytes
    post(port, "/hook/claude/Stop", TOKEN, &[], &stop).await;
    post(port, "/hook/claude/Stop", TOKEN, &[], &stop).await;
    assert_eq!(mb.dropped(), 1);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn connections_beyond_the_cap_wait() {
    let (port, _auth, mut mb) = serve(EndpointOptions { max_connections: 1, ..Default::default() }).await;
    let idle = tokio::net::TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", port)).await.unwrap();
    let waiting = tokio::spawn(async move {
        post(port, "/hook/claude/Stop", TOKEN, &[], &fx("claude/payload-Stop.json")).await
    });
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
    assert!(!waiting.is_finished(), "the second connection waits for a slot");
    drop(idle);
    assert_eq!(waiting.await.unwrap().0, 200);
    let _ = next_event(&mut mb).await;
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_held_permission_returns_the_adapter_reply() {
    let (port, _auth, mut mb) = serve(EndpointOptions::default()).await;
    let s = send(port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN,
        &[("X-Herdr-Pane", "w1:p2"), ("X-Herdr-Socket", ""), ("X-Claude-Entrypoint", "cli")],
        &fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json")).await;
    let Inbound::Held { event, reply, generation, .. } = next_control(&mut mb).await else { panic!("not held") };
    assert_eq!((event.name.clone(), generation), (HookName::PermissionRequest, 1));
    assert_eq!(event.hints.herdr_pane.as_deref(), Some("w1:p2"));
    assert_eq!(event.hints.herdr_socket, None, "an empty interpolation is absent");
    reply.send(acd_adapters::hooks::wire::reply_allow()).unwrap();
    let (status, body) = read_response(s).await;
    assert_eq!((status, body), (200, acd_adapters::hooks::wire::reply_allow()));

    let Inbound::Held { reply, .. } = next_control(&mut mb).await else { panic!() };

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_client_close_is_seen_as_hold_closed() {
    let (port, _auth, mut mb) = serve(EndpointOptions::default()).await;
    let s = send(port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN, &[], &fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json")).await;
    let Inbound::Held { hold, reply, .. } = next_control(&mut mb).await else { panic!() };
    drop(s); // Claude closes the connection on a terminal deny or Esc (gate 2b)
    let t = Instant::now();
    match next_control(&mut mb).await {
        Inbound::HoldClosed { hold: h } => assert_eq!(h, hold),
        other => panic!("{other:?}"),
    }
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(1), "close seen within 1 s");
    assert!(reply.is_closed(), "a reply after the close can never be delivered");
}

    let s = send(port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN, &[], &fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json")).await;
    let Inbound::Held { hold, reply: _keep, .. } = next_control(&mut mb).await else { panic!() };
    assert_eq!(read_response(s).await, (200, String::new()));
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the endpoint**

`hooks/mod.rs` gains `pub mod endpoint;` and
`#[cfg(any(test, feature = "testing"))] pub mod testing;`.

`hooks/endpoint.rs`:

```rust
//! The hook endpoint (spec §6.1): `POST /hook/{agent}/{event}` on
//! 127.0.0.1. Lifecycle hooks get their reply before any processing. A
//! Claude tool `PermissionRequest` is parked as a hold until the adapter
//! replies, releases it, or `permission_hold` passes.
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, AtomicUsize, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex, RwLock};
use std::task::{Context, Poll};
use std::pin::Pin;
use std::time::Duration;

use axum::Router;
use axum::extract::{Path, Request, State};
use axum::http::{Method, StatusCode, header};
use axum::response::{IntoResponse, Response};
use axum::routing::any;
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use subtle::ConstantTimeEq;
use tokio::io::{AsyncRead, AsyncWrite, ReadBuf};
use tokio::net::{TcpListener, TcpStream};
use tokio::sync::{OwnedSemaphorePermit, Semaphore, mpsc, oneshot};
use tokio::time::Instant;

use super::mapping::log_payload;
use super::wire::{HookAgent, HookEvent, HookName, Hints, Payload};

const MAX_REJECTED: usize = 256;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Credential {
    pub token: String,
    pub generation: u64,
}

struct Creds {
    current: Credential,
    previous: Option<Credential>,
}

pub struct HookAuth {
    creds: RwLock<Creds>,
    /// Opaque remote id -> last rejection. At most `MAX_REJECTED` entries.
    rejected: Mutex<HashMap<String, Instant>>,
}

/// 8 hex digits of `sha256(hint)`: logs and tables never hold a header value.
pub fn remote_id(hint: Option<&str>) -> String {
    match hint {
        None => "none".into(),
        Some(h) => Sha256::digest(h.as_bytes()).iter().take(4).map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect(),
    }
}

impl HookAuth {
    pub fn new(current: Credential) -> HookAuth {
        HookAuth { creds: RwLock::new(Creds { current, previous: None }), rejected: Mutex::default() }
    }

    pub fn set(&self, current: Credential, previous: Option<Credential>) {
        *self.creds.write().expect("hook auth") = Creds { current, previous };
    }

    /// Constant-time comparison; returns the matched generation (T2).
    pub fn check(&self, presented: &str) -> Option<u64> {
        let c = self.creds.read().expect("hook auth");
        let eq = |stored: &Credential| {
            !stored.token.is_empty() && bool::from(stored.token.as_bytes().ct_eq(presented.as_bytes()))
        };
        if eq(&c.current) {
            Some(c.current.generation)
        } else {
            c.previous.as_ref().filter(|p| eq(p)).map(|p| p.generation)
        }
    }

    /// Logs at most once a minute per remote, by opaque id only.
    pub fn note_rejected(&self, hint: Option<&str>) {
        let id = remote_id(hint);
        let now = Instant::now();
        let mut r = self.rejected.lock().expect("hook auth");
        let quiet = r.get(&id).is_some_and(|t| now.duration_since(*t) < Duration::from_secs(60));
        r.insert(id.clone(), now);
        while r.len() > MAX_REJECTED {
            let oldest = r.iter().min_by_key(|(_, t)| **t).map(|(k, _)| k.clone()).expect("non-empty");
            r.remove(&oldest);
        }
        drop(r);
        if !quiet {
            tracing::warn!(remote = %id, "hook request with a missing or retired token (restart that Claude session or run /clear in it)");
        }
    }

    pub fn rejected_since(&self, since: Instant) -> usize {
        self.rejected.lock().expect("hook auth").values().filter(|t| **t >= since).count()
    }

    pub fn rejected_entries(&self) -> usize {
        self.rejected.lock().expect("hook auth").len()
    }
}

pub type HoldId = u64;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum Inbound {
    /// A lifecycle event (or an unheld permission). `bytes` returns to the
    /// budget through `Mailbox::release`.
    Event { event: HookEvent, generation: u64, bytes: usize },
    Held { event: HookEvent, hold: HoldId, reply: oneshot::Sender<String>, generation: u64 },
    /// The client closed the connection before acd replied.
    HoldClosed { hold: HoldId },
    /// `permission_hold` passed; the endpoint already replied empty.
    HoldExpired { hold: HoldId },
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct EndpointOptions {
    pub permission_hold: Duration,
    pub max_holds: usize,
    pub body_timeout: Duration,
    pub max_body: usize,
    pub max_events: usize,
    pub max_event_bytes: usize,
    pub max_connections: usize,
}

impl Default for EndpointOptions {
    fn default() -> Self {
        EndpointOptions {
            permission_hold: Duration::from_secs(3600),
            max_holds: 64,
            body_timeout: Duration::from_secs(5),
            max_body: 1 << 20,
            max_events: 512,
            max_event_bytes: 16 << 20,
            max_connections: 128,
        }
    }
}

struct Budget {
    bytes: AtomicUsize,
    max: usize,
    dropped: AtomicU64,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct MailboxTx {
    events: mpsc::Sender<Inbound>,
    /// Holds, closes and expiries: bounded in practice by `max_holds`
    /// (each hold produces at most one close or expiry). Never dropped.
    control: mpsc::UnboundedSender<Inbound>,
    budget: Arc<Budget>,
}

pub struct Mailbox {
    pub events: mpsc::Receiver<Inbound>,
    pub control: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<Inbound>,
    budget: Arc<Budget>,
}

impl Mailbox {
    pub fn release(&self, bytes: usize) {
        self.budget.bytes.fetch_sub(bytes, Ordering::SeqCst);
    }
    pub fn dropped(&self) -> u64 {
        self.budget.dropped.load(Ordering::SeqCst)
    }
}

pub fn mailbox(opts: &EndpointOptions) -> (MailboxTx, Mailbox) {
    let (etx, erx) = mpsc::channel(opts.max_events);
    let (ctx, crx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
    let budget = Arc::new(Budget { bytes: AtomicUsize::new(0), max: opts.max_event_bytes, dropped: AtomicU64::new(0) });
    (
        MailboxTx { events: etx, control: ctx, budget: budget.clone() },
        Mailbox { events: erx, control: crx, budget },
    )
}

impl MailboxTx {
    /// Queues a lifecycle event, or drops and counts it when the queue or
    /// the byte budget is full (fail open: the hook already got its reply).
    fn event(&self, event: HookEvent, generation: u64, bytes: usize) {
        let b = &self.budget;
        if b.bytes.fetch_add(bytes, Ordering::SeqCst) + bytes > b.max {
            b.bytes.fetch_sub(bytes, Ordering::SeqCst);
            b.dropped.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
            return;
        }
        if self.events.try_send(Inbound::Event { event, generation, bytes }).is_err() {
            b.bytes.fetch_sub(bytes, Ordering::SeqCst);
            b.dropped.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone)]
struct Ctx {
    auth: Arc<HookAuth>,
    tx: MailboxTx,
    opts: EndpointOptions,
    holds: Arc<AtomicUsize>,
    next: Arc<AtomicU64>,
}

pub fn router(auth: Arc<HookAuth>, tx: MailboxTx, opts: EndpointOptions) -> Router {
    let ctx = Ctx { auth, tx, opts, holds: Arc::default(), next: Arc::default() };
    Router::new()
        .route("/hook/{agent}/{event}", any(handle))
        .fallback(|| async { StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED })
        .with_state(ctx)
}

fn empty() -> Response {
    StatusCode::OK.into_response()
}

async fn handle(State(ctx): State<Ctx>, Path((agent, event)): Path<(String, String)>, req: Request) -> Response {
    if req.method() != Method::POST {
        return StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED.into_response();
    }
    let Some(agent) = HookAgent::parse(&agent) else {
        return StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED.into_response();
    };
    let hints = Hints::from_headers(req.headers());
    let presented = req.headers().get("x-acd-token").and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok()).unwrap_or("");
    let Some(generation) = ctx.auth.check(presented) else {
        ctx.auth.note_rejected(hints.herdr_pane.as_deref());
        return StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED.into_response();
    };
    let body = match tokio::time::timeout(ctx.opts.body_timeout, axum::body::to_bytes(req.into_body(), ctx.opts.max_body)).await {
        Ok(Ok(b)) => b,
        Ok(Err(_)) => return StatusCode::PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE.into_response(),
        Err(_) => return StatusCode::REQUEST_TIMEOUT.into_response(),
    };
    let name = HookName::parse(&event);
    log_payload(agent, &name, &body);
    let Some(payload) = serde_json::from_slice(&body).ok().and_then(|v| Payload::from_value(&v)) else {
        return empty();
    };
    let holdable = agent == HookAgent::Claude
        && name == HookName::PermissionRequest
        && payload.tool_name.as_deref() != Some("AskUserQuestion");
    let event = HookEvent { agent, name, payload, hints, at: Instant::now() };
    if !holdable {
        ctx.tx.event(event, generation, body.len());
        return empty();
    }
    if ctx.holds.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst) >= ctx.opts.max_holds {
        ctx.holds.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
        tracing::warn!("too many held permission prompts; this one is answered in the terminal");
        ctx.tx.event(event, generation, body.len());
        return empty();
    }
    hold(ctx, event, generation).await
}

/// Reports a close when dropped before a reply was taken. hyper drops the
/// handler future when the client closes the connection, so a guard drop
/// without `done` is the "answered in the terminal or interrupted" signal.
struct HoldGuard {
    tx: mpsc::UnboundedSender<Inbound>,
    hold: HoldId,
    holds: Arc<AtomicUsize>,
    done: bool,
}

impl Drop for HoldGuard {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.holds.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
        if !self.done {
            let _ = self.tx.send(Inbound::HoldClosed { hold: self.hold });
        }
    }
}

async fn hold(ctx: Ctx, event: HookEvent, generation: u64) -> Response {
    let id = ctx.next.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    let (reply_tx, reply_rx) = oneshot::channel::<String>();
    let mut guard = HoldGuard { tx: ctx.tx.control.clone(), hold: id, holds: ctx.holds.clone(), done: false };
    if ctx.tx.control.send(Inbound::Held { event, hold: id, reply: reply_tx, generation }).is_err() {
        guard.done = true;
        return empty();
    }
    let out = tokio::time::timeout(ctx.opts.permission_hold, reply_rx).await;
    guard.done = true;
    match out {
        Ok(Ok(body)) if !body.is_empty() => ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/json")], body).into_response(),
        Ok(_) => empty(),
        Err(_) => {
            let _ = ctx.tx.control.send(Inbound::HoldExpired { hold: id });
            empty()
        }
    }
}

/// A listener that keeps at most `max` connections open; `accept` waits
/// for a slot. The permit lives in the connection's IO and is returned
/// when hyper drops it.
pub struct CappedListener {
    inner: TcpListener,
    slots: Arc<Semaphore>,
}

pub fn capped(listener: TcpListener, max: usize) -> CappedListener {
    CappedListener { inner: listener, slots: Arc::new(Semaphore::new(max)) }
}

pub struct CappedIo {
    io: TcpStream,
    _slot: OwnedSemaphorePermit,
}

impl AsyncRead for CappedIo {
    fn poll_read(mut self: Pin<&mut Self>, cx: &mut Context<'_>, buf: &mut ReadBuf<'_>) -> Poll<std::io::Result<()>> {
        Pin::new(&mut self.io).poll_read(cx, buf)
    }
}

impl AsyncWrite for CappedIo {
    fn poll_write(mut self: Pin<&mut Self>, cx: &mut Context<'_>, buf: &[u8]) -> Poll<std::io::Result<usize>> {
        Pin::new(&mut self.io).poll_write(cx, buf)
    }
    fn poll_flush(mut self: Pin<&mut Self>, cx: &mut Context<'_>) -> Poll<std::io::Result<()>> {
        Pin::new(&mut self.io).poll_flush(cx)
    }
    fn poll_shutdown(mut self: Pin<&mut Self>, cx: &mut Context<'_>) -> Poll<std::io::Result<()>> {
        Pin::new(&mut self.io).poll_shutdown(cx)
    }
}

impl axum::serve::Listener for CappedListener {
    type Io = CappedIo;
    type Addr = std::net::SocketAddr;

    async fn accept(&mut self) -> (CappedIo, std::net::SocketAddr) {
        loop {
            let slot = self.slots.clone().acquire_owned().await.expect("never closed");
            match self.inner.accept().await {
                Ok((io, addr)) => return (CappedIo { io, _slot: slot }, addr),
                Err(e) => {
                    tracing::debug!(error = %e, "hook listener accept failed");
                    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fn local_addr(&self) -> std::io::Result<std::net::SocketAddr> {
        self.inner.local_addr()
    }
}
```

(`axum::serve::Listener::accept` is declared as returning `impl Future`;
an `async fn` in the impl satisfies it on Rust 1.90. If the trait in the
locked axum version differs, follow its signature; the behaviour stays
the same.)

**If `a_client_close_is_seen_as_hold_closed` fails** (hyper does not
drop the handler future on a client EOF in this version), do not weaken
the test. Make `CappedIo` also watch for EOF: keep a `Notify` per
connection, and serve each connection with

loop that aborts the connection task when a duplicated socket
(`TcpStream::into_std` + `try_clone`) reads zero bytes on `peek`. Expose
it as `pub async fn serve(listener: CappedListener, router: Router)` from

```rust
//! `~/.claude/sessions/<pid>.json` discovery (spec §6.2, gate 5). Read
//! only `<digits>.json`; never open the `.key` files. Best effort: an
//! entry that cannot be read or verified is skipped and logged once.
use std::collections::HashSet;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;

use acd_protocol::AgentState;
use serde_json::Value;

use super::wire::valid_id;

const MAX_ENTRY_BYTES: u64 = 64 * 1024;
/// Owner-only writes: a group- or world-writable entry is not trusted.
const MAX_ENTRY_MODE: u32 = 0o755;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct RegistryEntry {
    pub pid: i32,
    pub session_id: String,
    pub cwd: Option<String>,
    pub started_at_ms: Option<i64>,
    pub proc_start: Option<String>,
    pub entrypoint: Option<String>,
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub status: Option<String>,
    pub waiting_for: Option<String>,
}

impl RegistryEntry {
    pub fn from_value(v: &Value) -> Option<RegistryEntry> {
        let o = v.as_object()?;
        let s = |k: &str| o.get(k).and_then(Value::as_str).map(str::to_owned);
        let pid = o.get("pid")?.as_i64().filter(|p| *p > 0 && *p <= i32::MAX as i64)? as i32;
        Some(RegistryEntry {
            pid,
            session_id: s("sessionId").filter(|x| valid_id(x))?,
            cwd: s("cwd"),
            started_at_ms: o.get("startedAt").and_then(Value::as_i64),
            proc_start: s("procStart"),
            entrypoint: s("entrypoint"),
            name: s("name"),
            status: s("status"),
            waiting_for: s("waitingFor"),
        })
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ProcVerdict {
    Live,
    Gone,
    Unverifiable,
}

pub trait ProcCheck: Send + Sync {
    fn check(&self, pid: i32, proc_start: &str) -> ProcVerdict;
}

/// `ps -o lstart=` in UTC (gate 5): `Fri Oct  9 07:29:36 2026`.
pub fn parse_lstart_utc(s: &str) -> Option<i64> {
    let t: Vec<&str> = s.split_whitespace().collect();
    let [_, mon, day, hms, year] = t.as_slice() else { return None };
    const MONTHS: [&str; 12] = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];
    let month = MONTHS.iter().position(|m| m == mon)? as u32 + 1;
    let mut hms = hms.split(':').map(|x| x.parse::<u32>().ok());
    let (h, m, sec) = (hms.next()??, hms.next()??, hms.next()??);
    let date = chrono::NaiveDate::from_ymd_opt(year.parse().ok()?, month, day.parse().ok()?)?;
    Some(date.and_hms_opt(h, m, sec)?.and_utc().timestamp())
}

pub struct SystemProc;

impl ProcCheck for SystemProc {
    fn check(&self, pid: i32, proc_start: &str) -> ProcVerdict {
        let Some(claimed) = parse_lstart_utc(proc_start) else {
            return ProcVerdict::Unverifiable;
        };
        match process_start_secs(pid) {
            Ok(Some(actual)) if (actual - claimed).abs() <= 1 => ProcVerdict::Live,
            Ok(_) => ProcVerdict::Gone,
            Err(()) => ProcVerdict::Unverifiable,
        }
    }
}

fn pid_exists(pid: i32) -> Option<bool> {
    // SAFETY: signal 0 only checks for existence and permission.
    if unsafe { libc::kill(pid, 0) } == 0 {
        return Some(true);
    }
    match std::io::Error::last_os_error().raw_os_error() {
        Some(libc::ESRCH) => Some(false),
        Some(libc::EPERM) => Some(true),
        _ => None,
    }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
fn process_start_secs(pid: i32) -> Result<Option<i64>, ()> {
    match pid_exists(pid) {
        Some(false) => return Ok(None),
        None => return Err(()),
        Some(true) => {}
    }
    let mut info: libc::proc_bsdinfo = unsafe { std::mem::zeroed() };
    let size = std::mem::size_of::<libc::proc_bsdinfo>() as libc::c_int;
    // SAFETY: `info` is a properly sized, writable proc_bsdinfo.
    let n = unsafe {
        libc::proc_pidinfo(pid, libc::PROC_PIDTBSDINFO, 0, &mut info as *mut _ as *mut libc::c_void, size)
    };
    if n == size { Ok(Some(info.pbi_start_tvsec as i64)) } else { Err(()) }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
fn process_start_secs(pid: i32) -> Result<Option<i64>, ()> {
    let stat = match std::fs::read_to_string(format!("/proc/{pid}/stat")) {
        Ok(s) => s,
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(None),
        Err(_) => return Err(()),
    };
    // Field 22 (starttime, clock ticks after boot); fields after the ")"
    // that ends the command name start at field 3.
    let after = stat.rsplit_once(')').ok_or(())?.1;
    let ticks: i64 = after.split_whitespace().nth(19).ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;
    let btime: i64 = std::fs::read_to_string("/proc/stat")
        .map_err(|_| ())?
        .lines()
        .find_map(|l| l.strip_prefix("btime "))
        .ok_or(())?
        .trim()
        .parse()
        .map_err(|_| ())?;
    // SAFETY: sysconf has no preconditions.
    let hz = unsafe { libc::sysconf(libc::_SC_CLK_TCK) };
    if hz <= 0 {
        return Err(());
    }
    Ok(Some(btime + ticks / hz as i64))
}

#[cfg(not(any(target_os = "macos", target_os = "linux")))]
fn process_start_secs(_pid: i32) -> Result<Option<i64>, ()> {
    Err(())
}

pub struct Scan {
    pub live: Vec<RegistryEntry>,
    /// False when any root or any entry could not be read or verified. An
    /// incomplete scan never removes an agent (spec §6.2) and never
    /// retires a hook token (T4).
    pub complete: bool,
    pub scanned_at: tokio::time::Instant,
}

pub struct Scanner {
    dirs: Vec<PathBuf>,
    proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>,
    logged: HashSet<String>,
}

impl Scanner {
    pub fn new(dirs: Vec<PathBuf>, proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>) -> Scanner {
        Scanner { dirs, proc, logged: HashSet::new() }
    }

    pub fn set_dirs(&mut self, dirs: Vec<PathBuf>) {
        self.dirs = dirs;
    }

    pub fn scan(&mut self) -> Scan {
        let mut live: Vec<RegistryEntry> = Vec::new();
        let mut complete = true;
        for dir in self.dirs.clone() {
            let (found, ok) = self.scan_dir(&dir);
            complete &= ok;
            for e in found {
                if !live.iter().any(|l| l.session_id == e.session_id) {
                    live.push(e);
                }
            }
        }
        Scan { live, complete, scanned_at: tokio::time::Instant::now() }
    }

    fn log_once(&mut self, what: &str, why: &str) {
        if self.logged.insert(format!("{what}:{why}")) {
            tracing::info!(entry = %what, %why, "skipping a Claude session registry entry");
        }
    }

    fn scan_dir(&mut self, dir: &Path) -> (Vec<RegistryEntry>, bool) {
        let entries = match std::fs::read_dir(dir) {
            Ok(e) => e,
            Err(_) => {
                self.log_once(&dir.display().to_string(), "unreadable");
                return (vec![], false);
            }
        };
        let mut live = Vec::new();
        let mut complete = true;
        for e in entries.flatten() {
            let name = e.file_name();
            let Some(name) = name.to_str() else { continue };
            let Some(stem) = name.strip_suffix(".json") else { continue };
            if stem.is_empty() || !stem.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()) {
                continue;
            }
            let entry = acd_securefs::read_private(&dir.join(name), MAX_ENTRY_MODE, MAX_ENTRY_BYTES)
                .ok()
                .and_then(|b| serde_json::from_slice::<Value>(&b).ok())
                .and_then(|v| RegistryEntry::from_value(&v))
                .filter(|e| e.pid.to_string() == stem);
            let Some(entry) = entry else {
                complete = false;
                self.log_once(name, "unreadable");
                continue;
            };
            match entry.proc_start.as_deref().map(|p| self.proc.check(entry.pid, p)) {
                Some(ProcVerdict::Live) => live.push(entry),
                Some(ProcVerdict::Gone) => {}
                Some(ProcVerdict::Unverifiable) | None => {
                    complete = false;
                    self.log_once(name, "process metadata unverifiable");
                }
            }
        }
        (live, complete)
    }
}

pub fn map_status(status: Option<&str>) -> AgentState {
    match status {
        Some("idle") => AgentState::Idle,
        Some("busy") => AgentState::Working,
        Some("waiting") => AgentState::NeedsYou,
        _ => AgentState::Unknown,
    }
}

pub fn sessions_dir(home: &Path, claude_config_dir: Option<&Path>) -> PathBuf {
    claude_config_dir.map(Path::to_path_buf).unwrap_or_else(|| home.join(".claude")).join("sessions")
}
```

### Task 10: Ownership and binding

GATE 4 (round-2 ruling B4): `codex_bind` never uses the hook env. It
binds by the **one** Codex pane reporting the session (two or more is
ambiguous), else by the cwd only when exactly one Codex pane is in that
cwd across all panes, whatever sessions they report (gate 4: reported
sessions come from the same stale env, so they cannot rule a pane out).
Fixtures: `agent-info-codex-with-agent-session.json`,
`agent-info-codex-shared-daemon-no-agent-session.json`,
`agent-list-codex-fresh-app-server-misbound.json`. Task 17 then requires
the bound pane's own screen to match (M2) and no other Codex pane to show
the same command. GATE 2: Claude header binding relies on header
interpolation (2a).

- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/owner.rs`

```rust
//! Who owns a session (spec §6.2 Ownership) and which herdr pane it is
//! bound to (§6.2 Binding; §6.3 Binding as amended by gate 4).
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use acd_protocol::AgentKind;

use super::wire::{Hints, valid_id};
use crate::herdr::map::socket_hash;
use crate::herdr::reconciler::PaneMap;
use crate::t3::sessions::SessionMap;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PaneFact {
    pub agent_id: String,
    pub socket: PathBuf,
    pub pane_id: String,
    pub kind: AgentKind,
    pub session: Option<String>,
    pub cwd: Option<String>,
    /// G1: tickets and targets compare it.
    pub incarnation: u64,
}

pub fn pane_facts(panes: &PaneMap) -> Vec<PaneFact> {
    panes
        .read()
        .expect("pane map")
        .iter()
        .map(|(id, p)| PaneFact {
            agent_id: id.clone(),
            socket: p.socket.clone(),
            pane_id: p.pane_id.clone(),
            kind: p.kind,
            session: p.session.clone(),
            cwd: p.cwd.clone(),
            incarnation: p.incarnation,
        })
        .collect()
}

pub trait SessionOwners: Send + Sync {
    fn owner(&self, session_id: &str) -> Option<String>;
}

impl SessionOwners for SessionMap {
    fn owner(&self, session_id: &str) -> Option<String> {
        SessionMap::owner(self, session_id)
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Owner {
    T3(String),
    Herdr(String),
    Hooks,
    Unknown,
}

/// The pane named by the Claude headers. The socket header is only
/// compared through `socket_hash` with sockets herdr discovery already
/// knows; acd never opens a path taken from a header. A pane that reports
/// a different session is not this session's pane.
pub fn header_pane<'a>(hints: &Hints, session_id: &str, panes: &'a [PaneFact]) -> Option<&'a PaneFact> {
    let pane = hints.herdr_pane.as_deref().filter(|p| valid_id(p))?;
    let hash = socket_hash(Path::new(hints.herdr_socket.as_deref()?));
    panes.iter().find(|f| {
        f.pane_id == pane
            && f.kind == AgentKind::Claude
            && socket_hash(&f.socket) == hash
            && f.session.as_deref().is_none_or(|s| s == session_id)
    })
}

/// Spec §6.2 Ownership, rules 1 to 4, in order.
pub fn claude_owner(
    session_id: &str,
    hints: &Hints,
    entrypoint: Option<&str>,
    t3: Option<&dyn SessionOwners>,
    panes: &[PaneFact],
    ignored: &[String],
) -> Owner {
    if let Some(agent) = t3.and_then(|m| m.owner(session_id)) {
        return Owner::T3(agent);
    }
    if let Some(p) = header_pane(hints, session_id, panes) {
        return Owner::Herdr(p.agent_id.clone());
    }
    if let Some(p) = panes.iter().find(|p| p.kind == AgentKind::Claude && p.session.as_deref() == Some(session_id)) {
        return Owner::Herdr(p.agent_id.clone());
    }
    match entrypoint {
        Some(e) if ignored.iter().any(|i| i == e) => Owner::Unknown,
        _ => Owner::Hooks,
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum CodexBind {
    Session(PaneFact),
    OnlyInCwd(PaneFact),
    Ambiguous,
    Unbound,
}

/// Gate 4 and ruling B4: the hook env is never used. Exactly one Codex
/// pane reporting this session binds; two or more are ambiguous.
/// Otherwise the cwd binds only when exactly one Codex pane is in it
/// across all panes, whatever sessions they report. The caller still
/// requires that pane's own screen to match (M2) and no competing pane
/// to show the same command (Task 17).
pub fn codex_bind(session_id: &str, cwd: Option<&str>, panes: &[PaneFact]) -> CodexBind {
    let codex: Vec<&PaneFact> = panes.iter().filter(|p| p.kind == AgentKind::Codex).collect();
    let by_session: Vec<&&PaneFact> = codex.iter().filter(|p| p.session.as_deref() == Some(session_id)).collect();
    match by_session.as_slice() {
        [one] => return CodexBind::Session((**one).clone()),
        [_, _, ..] => return CodexBind::Ambiguous,
        [] => {}
    }
    let Some(cwd) = cwd else { return CodexBind::Unbound };
    let same: Vec<&&PaneFact> = codex
        .iter()
        .filter(|p| p.cwd.as_deref().is_some_and(|c| same_dir(c, cwd)))
        .collect();
    match same.as_slice() {
        [] => CodexBind::Unbound,
        [one] => CodexBind::OnlyInCwd((**one).clone()),
        _ => CodexBind::Ambiguous,
    }
}

/// herdr reports `/tmp/...`, Codex `/private/tmp/...` (D5).
pub fn same_dir(a: &str, b: &str) -> bool {
    let canon = |s: &str| std::fs::canonicalize(s).unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from(s.trim_end_matches('/')));
    canon(a) == canon(b)
}
```

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/mod.rs` (`HooksAdapter`, `HooksConfig`)
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/state.rs`
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/tests/common/mod.rs` (the shared hooks test rig)
- Test: `crates/acd-adapters/tests/hooks_claude.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 2, 7, 8, 9, 10; `HerdrAdapter::panes()` and
  `agent_locks()`; `SessionMap`.
- Produces:
  - `hooks::HooksConfig` (fields below) and
    `HooksConfig::new(permission_hold: Duration, ignored_entrypoints: Vec<String>, sessions_dirs: SessionRoots) -> HooksConfig`.
  - `hooks::SessionRoots`: `SessionRoots::fixed(dirs: Vec<PathBuf>)`,
    `SessionRoots::dynamic(f: impl Fn() -> Vec<PathBuf> + Send + Sync + 'static)`,
    `SessionRoots::none()`. The scan asks it for the roots before every
    scan, so the daemon (Task 28) can follow the install records (D24).
  - `hooks::HooksAdapter::new(cfg: HooksConfig, herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>, t3: Option<Arc<SessionMap>>, proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>) -> Arc<HooksAdapter>`.
  - `HooksAdapter::router(&self, auth: Arc<HookAuth>) -> axum::Router`.
  - `HooksAdapter::start(self: &Arc<Self>, core: Arc<Core>)`.
  - `HooksAdapter::last_complete_scan(&self) -> Option<(tokio::time::Instant, Vec<RegistryEntry>)>`
    (when the last **complete** scan finished, and its live sessions; T4
    requires it to be fresh).
  - `HooksAdapter::sessions_on_generation(&self, generation: u64) -> HashSet<String>`
    (session ids seen authenticating with that credential generation;
    the last four generations are kept).
  - `Adapter` impl: name `"hooks"`, `answer_paths() == [AnswerPath::Hook]`,
    `sources() == [Source::Hooks]`. `send` returns
    `NotDelivered(NotSupported)`. `deliver` is added in Task 14 (this
    task returns `NotDelivered(RequestGone)`).
  - `state::Shared { held: Mutex<HashMap<String, HeldReq>>, last_scan: Mutex<Option<(Instant, Vec<RegistryEntry>)>>, by_generation: Mutex<BTreeMap<u64, HashSet<String>>> }` (crate-private).
  - Every session carries `bind_gen` (G2), bumped by `apply_owner` on
    every owner change. The reconciler loop serves control, internal
    results, the tick, T3 changes and then events, in that priority.
    Task 18 adds request revocation to the transitions that need it.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test rig and failing tests**

The rig lives in `crates/acd-adapters/tests/common/mod.rs`, so

```rust
#![allow(dead_code)]
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_adapters::herdr::{HerdrAdapter, HerdrConfig, fake::FakeHerdr, keys::KeysTiming, reconciler::Timing};
use acd_adapters::hooks::claude_sessions::{ProcCheck, ProcVerdict};
use acd_adapters::hooks::endpoint::{Credential, HookAuth};
use acd_adapters::hooks::testing::{post, send};
use acd_adapters::hooks::{HooksAdapter, HooksConfig};
use acd_adapters::t3::sessions::SessionMap;
use acd_core::{Core, testing::test_machine};
use acd_protocol::Agent;
use serde_json::Value;

pub const TOKEN: &str = "tok-hooks-test-0123456789";

pub struct Live;
impl ProcCheck for Live {
    fn check(&self, _: i32, _: &str) -> ProcVerdict { ProcVerdict::Live }
}

pub struct Rig {
    pub core: Arc<Core>,
    pub hooks: Arc<HooksAdapter>,
    pub herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
    pub fake: Option<FakeHerdr>,
    pub t3: Arc<SessionMap>,
    pub port: u16,
    pub home: tempfile::TempDir,
}

pub fn fast(cfg: &mut HooksConfig) {
    cfg.tick = Duration::from_millis(20);
    cfg.scan_interval = Duration::from_millis(100);
    cfg.evidence_window = Duration::from_millis(400);
    cfg.capture_window = Duration::from_millis(600);
    cfg.capture_poll = Duration::from_millis(50);
    cfg.codex_window = Duration::from_millis(600);
    cfg.held_dialog_poll = Duration::from_millis(100);
    cfg.end_grace = Duration::from_millis(200);
}

/// `panes`: herdr agents to fake; empty means no herdr at all.
pub async fn rig(panes: &[Value], tweak: impl FnOnce(&mut HooksConfig)) -> Rig {
    let home = tempfile::Builder::new().prefix("acd").tempdir_in("/tmp").unwrap();
    let (herdr, fake) = if panes.is_empty() {
        (None, None)
    } else {
        let sock = home.path().join(".config/herdr/herdr.sock");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(sock.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
        let fake = FakeHerdr::start_at(&sock).await;
        for p in panes { fake.set_agent(p.clone()); }
        let cfg = HerdrConfig {
            enabled: true, home: home.path().into(), env_socket: None, extra_sockets: vec![],
            scan_interval: Duration::from_millis(100),
            timing: Timing { list_idle: Duration::from_millis(300), list_blocked: Duration::from_millis(100),
                             backoff_max: Duration::from_millis(200), fallback_after: Duration::from_millis(200),
                             poll: Duration::from_millis(50) },
            keys: KeysTiming { evidence_timeout: Duration::from_millis(800), ..KeysTiming::default() },
        };
        (Some(HerdrAdapter::new(cfg)), Some(fake))
    };
    let sessions = home.path().join(".claude/sessions");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&sessions).unwrap();
    let mut cfg = HooksConfig::new(Duration::from_secs(3600), vec!["sdk-ts".into()],
        acd_adapters::hooks::SessionRoots::fixed(vec![sessions]));
    fast(&mut cfg);
    tweak(&mut cfg);
    let t3 = Arc::new(SessionMap::default());
    let hooks = HooksAdapter::new(cfg, herdr.clone(), Some(t3.clone()), Arc::new(Live));
    let mut adapters: Vec<Arc<dyn acd_core::Adapter>> = vec![hooks.clone()];
    if let Some(h) = &herdr { adapters.push(h.clone()); }
    let core = Core::new(test_machine(), adapters);
    if let Some(h) = &herdr {
        // h.set_evidence(hooks.evidence()); // Task 16 adds HooksAdapter::evidence and restores this line
        h.start(core.clone());
    }
    hooks.start(core.clone());
    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.unwrap();
    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let auth = Arc::new(HookAuth::new(Credential { token: TOKEN.into(), generation: 1 }));
    let app = hooks.router(auth);
    tokio::spawn(async move { axum::serve(acd_adapters::hooks::endpoint::capped(listener, 128), app).await.unwrap() });
    if let Some(h) = &herdr {
        let n = panes.len();
        until("herdr panes", || (h.panes().read().unwrap().len() == n).then_some(())).await;
    }
    Rig { core, hooks, herdr, fake, t3, port, home }
}

pub async fn until<T>(what: &str, mut f: impl FnMut() -> Option<T>) -> T {
    for _ in 0..250 {
        if let Some(v) = f() { return v; }
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20)).await;
    }
    panic!("timed out waiting for {what}");
}

pub fn fx(rel: &str) -> Value {
    let p = format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/hooks/{rel}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"));
    serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(p).unwrap()).unwrap()
}

pub const SID: &str = "4e3885ee-7454-410d-a2e2-86f4dc2533c0";

impl Rig {
    pub fn sock(&self) -> String { self.fake.as_ref().unwrap().path().to_string_lossy().into_owned() }
    pub async fn hook(&self, agent: &str, event: &str, body: &Value, headers: &[(&str, &str)]) -> (u16, String) {
        post(self.port, &format!("/hook/{agent}/{event}"), TOKEN, headers, &body.to_string()).await
    }
    pub async fn hold(&self, body: &Value, headers: &[(&str, &str)]) -> tokio::net::TcpStream {
        send(self.port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN, headers, &body.to_string()).await
    }
    pub fn agent(&self, id: &str) -> Option<Agent> { self.core.agent(id) }
}

pub const CLI: [(&str, &str); 1] = [("X-Claude-Entrypoint", "cli")];
```

```rust
mod common;
use common::*;

use std::time::Duration;

use acd_adapters::herdr::fake::fake_agent;
use acd_adapters::hooks::testing::read_response;
use acd_protocol::*;
use serde_json::{Value, json};

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_hooks_only_session_appears_on_its_first_hook() {
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json"), &CLI).await;
    let a = until("agent", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}"))).await;
    assert_eq!((a.source, a.kind, a.state, a.can_send), (Source::Hooks, AgentKind::Claude, AgentState::Working, false));
    assert_eq!(a.project.as_deref(), Some("claude1"));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn stop_sets_done_and_caps_the_last_message() {
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    let mut stop = fx("claude/payload-Stop.json");
    stop["last_assistant_message"] = "x".repeat(900).into();
    r.hook("claude", "Stop", &stop, &CLI).await;
    let a = until("done", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).filter(|a| a.state == AgentState::Done)).await;
    assert_eq!(a.last_message.unwrap().chars().count(), 500);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn an_ignored_entrypoint_without_a_binding_is_not_shown() {
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json"), &[("X-Claude-Entrypoint", "sdk-ts")]).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
    assert!(r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).is_none());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn subagent_payloads_are_dropped() {
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    let mut p = fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json");
    p["agent_id"] = "sub-1".into();
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &p, &CLI).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
    assert!(r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).is_none());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_t3_owned_session_is_dropped_and_comes_back_when_t3_lets_go() {
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json"), &CLI).await;
    until("agent", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}"))).await;
    r.t3.set_for_tests("t3:thread-1", Some(SID));
    until("dropped", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).is_none().then_some(())).await;
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json"), &CLI).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
    assert!(r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).is_none(), "T3 still owns it");
    r.t3.set_for_tests("t3:thread-1", None);
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json"), &CLI).await;
    until("back", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}"))).await;
}

fn registry_entry(r: &Rig, status: &str) {

    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;

    until("done", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).filter(|a| a.state == AgentState::Done)).await;

#[tokio::test]
async fn session_end_removes_the_agent_after_the_grace() {
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json"), &CLI).await;
    until("agent", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}"))).await;
    let mut end = fx("claude/payload-Stop.json");
    end["hook_event_name"] = "SessionEnd".into();
    r.hook("claude", "SessionEnd", &end, &CLI).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100)).await;
    assert!(r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).is_some(), "kept during the grace");
    until("removed", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).is_none().then_some(())).await;
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_binding_that_appears_later_migrates_the_agent() {
    let mut pane = fake_agent("w1:p1", "claude", "idle");
    let r = rig(&[pane.clone()], |_| {}).await;
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json"), &CLI).await;
    until("hooks-only", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}"))).await;

    r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().push_event(json!({"event": "pane_updated", "data": {"pane_id": "w1:p1"}}));
    until("pane session", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().values().find_map(|p| p.session.clone())).await;
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json"), &CLI).await;
    until("migrated", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).is_none().then_some(())).await;
}
```
