```rust
//! Claude and Codex hooks (spec §6.1 to §6.3).
pub mod claude_sessions;
pub mod endpoint;
pub mod mapping;
pub mod owner;
mod state;
#[cfg(any(test, feature = "testing"))]
pub mod testing;
pub mod wire;

use std::collections::HashSet;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_core::{Adapter, Answer, Core, DeliverError};
use acd_protocol::{Agent, AnswerPath, ErrorCode, Request, Source};
use async_trait::async_trait;

use self::claude_sessions::{ProcCheck, RegistryEntry};
use self::endpoint::{EndpointOptions, HookAuth, Mailbox, MailboxTx};
use crate::herdr::HerdrAdapter;
use crate::t3::sessions::SessionMap;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct HooksConfig {
    /// `claude.permission_hold` (spec §9); holds end here with an empty reply.
    pub permission_hold: Duration,
    pub ignored_entrypoints: Vec<String>,
    /// Registry roots (`<claude config dir>/sessions`), asked before every
    /// scan; empty disables the scan.
    pub sessions_dirs: SessionRoots,
    pub scan_interval: Duration,
    pub tick: Duration,
    /// Correlated outcome evidence window (spec §5.1, §6.2: 30 s).
    pub evidence_window: Duration,
    /// Fingerprint capture after a request opens (spec §6.2 step 6: 2 s).
    pub capture_window: Duration,
    pub capture_poll: Duration,
    /// Codex dialog wait (spec §6.3: 1 s).
    pub codex_window: Duration,
    /// Presence checks of a held request's captured dialog.
    pub held_dialog_poll: Duration,
    /// Removal after `SessionEnd` or the registry entry disappearing (10 min).
    pub end_grace: Duration,
    /// Removal after no event at all (24 h).
    pub idle_expiry: Duration,
    pub max_holds: usize,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct SessionRoots(Arc<dyn Fn() -> Vec<PathBuf> + Send + Sync>);

impl SessionRoots {
    pub fn fixed(dirs: Vec<PathBuf>) -> SessionRoots {
        SessionRoots(Arc::new(move || dirs.clone()))
    }
    pub fn dynamic(f: impl Fn() -> Vec<PathBuf> + Send + Sync + 'static) -> SessionRoots {
        SessionRoots(Arc::new(f))
    }
    pub fn none() -> SessionRoots {
        SessionRoots::fixed(vec![])
    }
    pub fn get(&self) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
        (self.0)()
    }
}

impl std::fmt::Debug for SessionRoots {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str("SessionRoots")
    }
}

impl HooksConfig {
    pub fn new(permission_hold: Duration, ignored_entrypoints: Vec<String>, sessions_dirs: SessionRoots) -> HooksConfig {
        HooksConfig {
            permission_hold,
            ignored_entrypoints,
            sessions_dirs,
            scan_interval: Duration::from_secs(30),
            tick: Duration::from_millis(250),
            evidence_window: Duration::from_secs(30),
            capture_window: Duration::from_secs(2),
            capture_poll: Duration::from_millis(200),
            codex_window: Duration::from_secs(1),
            held_dialog_poll: Duration::from_secs(1),
            end_grace: Duration::from_secs(600),
            idle_expiry: Duration::from_secs(24 * 3600),
            max_holds: 64,
        }
    }
}

pub struct HooksAdapter {
    cfg: HooksConfig,
    herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
    t3: Option<Arc<SessionMap>>,
    proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>,
    shared: Arc<state::Shared>,
    opts: EndpointOptions,
    tx: MailboxTx,
    rx: Mutex<Option<Mailbox>>,
}

impl HooksAdapter {
    pub fn new(
        cfg: HooksConfig,
        herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
        t3: Option<Arc<SessionMap>>,
        proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>,
    ) -> Arc<HooksAdapter> {
        let opts = EndpointOptions { permission_hold: cfg.permission_hold, max_holds: cfg.max_holds, ..Default::default() };
        let (tx, rx) = endpoint::mailbox(&opts);
        Arc::new(HooksAdapter { cfg, herdr, t3, proc, shared: Arc::default(), opts, tx, rx: Mutex::new(Some(rx)) })
    }

    pub fn router(&self, auth: Arc<HookAuth>) -> axum::Router {
        endpoint::router(auth, self.tx.clone(), self.opts.clone())
    }

    /// Spawns the reconciler. Call once.
    pub fn start(self: &Arc<Self>, core: Arc<Core>) {
        let rx = self.rx.lock().expect("rx").take().expect("HooksAdapter::start called twice");
        let r = state::Reconciler::new(
            self.cfg.clone(), core, self.herdr.clone(), self.t3.clone(), self.proc.clone(), self.shared.clone(), rx,
        );
        tokio::spawn(r.run());
    }

    pub fn last_complete_scan(&self) -> Option<(tokio::time::Instant, Vec<RegistryEntry>)> {
        self.shared.last_scan.lock().expect("shared").clone()
    }

    pub fn sessions_on_generation(&self, generation: u64) -> HashSet<String> {
        self.shared.by_generation.lock().expect("shared").get(&generation).cloned().unwrap_or_default()
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl Adapter for HooksAdapter {
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
        "hooks"
    }
    fn answer_paths(&self) -> &[AnswerPath] {
        &[AnswerPath::Hook]
    }
    fn sources(&self) -> &[Source] {
        &[Source::Hooks]
    }
    async fn deliver(&self, _request: &Request, _answer: &Answer) -> Result<(), DeliverError> {
        Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::RequestGone)) // Task 14
    }
    /// Hooks-only agents have `can_send: false`; the core refuses first.
    async fn send(&self, _agent: &Agent, _text: &str) -> Result<(), DeliverError> {
        Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::NotSupported))
    }
}
```

`hooks/state.rs`:

```rust
//! The hooks reconciler: the only code that changes hook-derived state.
//! It drains the endpoint's mailbox in arrival order and its own internal
//! channel (results of spawned screen reads and scans). It never awaits a
//! herdr call itself.
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, HashMap, HashSet};
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

use acd_core::{AgentObs, Core, Observation};
use acd_protocol::{AgentKind, AgentState, Effect, Source};
use tokio::sync::{mpsc, oneshot, watch};
use tokio::time::Instant;

use super::HooksConfig;
use super::claude_sessions::{ProcCheck, RegistryEntry, Scan, Scanner, map_status};
use super::endpoint::{HoldId, Inbound, Mailbox};
use super::owner::{Owner, PaneFact, claude_owner, pane_facts};
use super::wire::{AlwaysRule, HookAgent, HookEvent, HookName};
use crate::herdr::HerdrAdapter;
use crate::t3::sessions::SessionMap;
use crate::text::{LAST_MESSAGE_CHARS, TITLE_CHARS, cap_chars, clean_display};

/// State `deliver` and the reconciler share. `deliver` only takes a reply
/// sender and stamps `delivered`; no lock is held across an await.
#[derive(Default)]
pub(crate) struct Shared {
    pub held: Mutex<HashMap<String, HeldReq>>,
    /// When the last complete scan finished, and what it found.
    pub last_scan: Mutex<Option<(Instant, Vec<RegistryEntry>)>>,
    /// Credential generation -> sessions seen using it (T4).
    pub by_generation: Mutex<BTreeMap<u64, HashSet<String>>>,
}

pub(crate) struct HeldReq {
    pub hold: HoldId,
    pub reply: Option<oneshot::Sender<String>>,
    pub rule: Option<AlwaysRule>,
    pub delivered: Option<(Effect, Instant)>,
}

pub(crate) enum Internal {
    Scanned(Scan),
}

struct Session {
    owner: Owner,
    /// G2: bumped on every owner or binding change and every revocation.
    bind_gen: u64,
    /// The registry agent this session shows as, if any.
    agent_id: Option<String>,
    hooks_only: bool,
    cwd: Option<String>,
    name: Option<String>,
    entrypoint: Option<String>,
    state: AgentState,
    last_message: Option<String>,
    last_hook: Option<Instant>,
    last_event: Instant,
    in_registry: bool,
    ended: Option<Instant>,
}

pub(crate) struct Reconciler {
    cfg: HooksConfig,
    core: Arc<Core>,
    herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
    t3: Option<Arc<SessionMap>>,
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
    mailbox: Mailbox,
    internal_tx: mpsc::UnboundedSender<Internal>,
    internal_rx: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<Internal>,
    scanner: Option<Arc<Mutex<Scanner>>>,
    sessions: HashMap<String, Session>,
}

impl Reconciler {
    pub(crate) fn new(
        cfg: HooksConfig,
        core: Arc<Core>,
        herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
        t3: Option<Arc<SessionMap>>,
        proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>,
        shared: Arc<Shared>,
        mailbox: Mailbox,
    ) -> Reconciler {
        let (internal_tx, internal_rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
        let scanner = Some(Arc::new(Mutex::new(Scanner::new(cfg.sessions_dirs.get(), proc))));
        Reconciler { cfg, core, herdr, t3, shared, mailbox, internal_tx, internal_rx, scanner, sessions: HashMap::new() }
    }

    /// Priority (Global Constraints, Sol I8): control (holds, closes,
    /// expiries), internal read results, the tick, T3 ownership changes,
    /// then lifecycle events. A flood of events cannot starve closes,
    /// timers or takeovers; events wait in the bounded mailbox.
    pub(crate) async fn run(mut self) {
        let mut tick = tokio::time::interval(self.cfg.tick);
        tick.set_missed_tick_behavior(tokio::time::MissedTickBehavior::Delay);
        let mut t3_changes = self.t3.as_ref().map(|m| m.changes());
        let mut next_scan = Instant::now();
        loop {
            tokio::select! {
                biased;
                Some(m) = self.mailbox.control.recv() => self.on_inbound(m).await,
                Some(m) = self.internal_rx.recv() => self.on_internal(m).await,
                _ = tick.tick() => {
                    let now = Instant::now();
                    if now >= next_scan {
                        next_scan = now + self.cfg.scan_interval;
                        self.spawn_scan();
                    }
                    self.on_tick().await;
                }
                _ = changed(&mut t3_changes) => self.reevaluate_all().await,
                m = self.mailbox.events.recv() => match m {
                    Some(m) => {
                        if let Inbound::Event { bytes, .. } = &m {
                            self.mailbox.release(*bytes);
                        }
                        self.on_inbound(m).await
                    }
                    None => return,
                },
            }
        }
    }

    fn spawn_scan(&self) {
        let Some(scanner) = self.scanner.clone() else { return };
        let dirs = self.cfg.sessions_dirs.get();
        if dirs.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let tx = self.internal_tx.clone();
        tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
            let mut s = scanner.lock().expect("scanner");
            s.set_dirs(dirs);
            let scan = s.scan();
            let _ = tx.send(Internal::Scanned(scan));
        });
    }

    fn panes(&self) -> Vec<PaneFact> {
        self.herdr.as_ref().map(|h| pane_facts(&h.panes())).unwrap_or_default()
    }

    fn owner_of(&self, session_id: &str, hints: &super::wire::Hints, entrypoint: Option<&str>) -> Owner {
        let t3 = self.t3.as_deref().map(|m| m as &dyn super::owner::SessionOwners);
        claude_owner(session_id, hints, entrypoint, t3, &self.panes(), &self.cfg.ignored_entrypoints)
    }

    async fn on_inbound(&mut self, m: Inbound) {
        match m {
            Inbound::Event { event, generation, .. } => self.on_event(event, generation, None).await,
            Inbound::Held { event, hold, reply, generation } => self.on_event(event, generation, Some((hold, reply))).await,
            Inbound::HoldClosed { hold } => self.on_hold_ended(hold, false).await,
            Inbound::HoldExpired { hold } => self.on_hold_ended(hold, true).await,
        }
    }

    async fn on_event(&mut self, event: HookEvent, generation: u64, hold: Option<(HoldId, oneshot::Sender<String>)>) {
        {
            // T4: remember which credential generation each session used.
            let mut g = self.shared.by_generation.lock().expect("shared");
            g.entry(generation).or_default().insert(event.payload.session_id.clone());
            while g.len() > 4 {
                let oldest = *g.keys().next().expect("non-empty");
                g.remove(&oldest);
            }
        }
        if event.payload.is_subagent() {
            return; // `hold` drops here: the endpoint replies empty
        }
        match event.agent {
            HookAgent::Claude => self.on_claude(event, hold).await,
            HookAgent::Codex => self.on_codex(event).await,
        }
    }

    async fn on_claude(&mut self, event: HookEvent, hold: Option<(HoldId, oneshot::Sender<String>)>) {
        let sid = event.payload.session_id.clone();
        let entrypoint = event.hints.entrypoint.clone();
        let owner = self.owner_of(&sid, &event.hints, entrypoint.as_deref());
        let now = Instant::now();
        let s = self.sessions.entry(sid.clone()).or_insert_with(|| Session {
            owner: Owner::Unknown, bind_gen: 0, agent_id: None, hooks_only: false, cwd: None, name: None, entrypoint: None,
            state: AgentState::Idle, last_message: None, last_hook: None, last_event: now, in_registry: false, ended: None,
        });
        s.last_event = now;
        s.last_hook = Some(now);
        if event.payload.cwd.is_some() {
            s.cwd = event.payload.cwd.clone();
        }
        if entrypoint.is_some() {
            s.entrypoint = entrypoint;
        }
        match event.name {
            HookName::UserPromptSubmit => s.state = AgentState::Working,
            HookName::Stop => {
                s.state = AgentState::Done;
                s.last_message = event
                    .payload
                    .last_assistant_message
                    .as_deref()
                    .map(|m| cap_chars(&clean_display(m, usize::MAX), LAST_MESSAGE_CHARS));
            }
            HookName::SessionEnd => s.ended = Some(now),
            _ => {}
        }
        self.apply_owner(&sid, owner).await;
        let _ = hold; // permission requests: Task 14
    }

    async fn on_codex(&mut self, _event: HookEvent) {} // Task 17

    async fn on_hold_ended(&mut self, _hold: HoldId, _expired: bool) {} // Task 15

    async fn on_internal(&mut self, m: Internal) {
        match m {
            Internal::Scanned(scan) => self.on_scan(scan).await,
        }
    }

    /// Moves a session to `owner`, publishing whatever that changes.
    async fn apply_owner(&mut self, sid: &str, owner: Owner) {
        let hooks_id = format!("claude:{sid}");
        let Some(s) = self.sessions.get_mut(sid) else { return };
        let was_hooks_only = s.hooks_only;
        if s.owner != owner {
            s.bind_gen += 1; // G2: outstanding tickets for this session fail
        }
        s.owner = owner.clone();
        match owner {
            Owner::T3(_) | Owner::Unknown => {
                s.agent_id = None;
                s.hooks_only = false;
                if was_hooks_only {
                    self.core.observe(Observation::AgentGone { agent_id: hooks_id });
                }
            }
            Owner::Herdr(agent) => {
                s.agent_id = Some(agent.clone());
                s.hooks_only = false;
                if was_hooks_only && self.core.agent(&agent).is_some() {
                    let lock = self.herdr.as_ref().map(|h| h.agent_locks());
                    let _g = match &lock {
                        Some(l) => Some(l.lock(&agent).await),
                        None => None,
                    };
                    self.core.observe(Observation::AgentMigrated { from: hooks_id, to: agent });
                }
            }
            Owner::Hooks => {
                s.agent_id = Some(hooks_id.clone());
                s.hooks_only = true;
                let obs = hooks_only_obs(&hooks_id, s);
                self.core.observe(Observation::AgentUpsert(obs));
            }
        }
    }

    async fn reevaluate_all(&mut self) {
        let ids: Vec<(String, Option<String>)> =
            self.sessions.iter().map(|(k, s)| (k.clone(), s.entrypoint.clone())).collect();
        for (sid, entrypoint) in ids {
            let owner = self.owner_of(&sid, &Default::default(), entrypoint.as_deref());
            let changed = self.sessions.get(&sid).is_some_and(|s| s.owner != owner);
            if changed {
                self.apply_owner(&sid, owner).await;
            }
        }
    }

    async fn on_scan(&mut self, scan: Scan) {
        let now = Instant::now();
        let live: HashSet<String> = scan.live.iter().map(|e| e.session_id.clone()).collect();
        for e in &scan.live {
            let s = self.sessions.entry(e.session_id.clone()).or_insert_with(|| Session {
                owner: Owner::Unknown, bind_gen: 0, agent_id: None, hooks_only: false, cwd: None, name: None, entrypoint: None,
                state: AgentState::Unknown, last_message: None, last_hook: None, last_event: now, in_registry: true, ended: None,
            });
            s.in_registry = true;
            s.last_event = now;
            s.cwd = s.cwd.clone().or_else(|| e.cwd.clone());
            s.name = e.name.clone().or(s.name.take());
            if s.entrypoint.is_none() {
                s.entrypoint = e.entrypoint.clone();
            }
            // D11: hooks are fresher than the scan, and done stays done.
            let fresh_hook = s.last_hook.is_some_and(|t| now.duration_since(t) < self.cfg.scan_interval);
            let scanned = map_status(e.status.as_deref());
            if !fresh_hook && !(s.state == AgentState::Done && scanned == AgentState::Idle) {
                s.state = scanned;
            }
        }
        if scan.complete {
            for (sid, s) in self.sessions.iter_mut() {
                if s.in_registry && !live.contains(sid) && s.ended.is_none() {
                    s.ended = Some(now);
                }
            }
            *self.shared.last_scan.lock().expect("shared") = Some((scan.scanned_at, scan.live.clone()));
        }
        for e in &scan.live {
            let owner = self.owner_of(&e.session_id, &Default::default(), e.entrypoint.as_deref());
            self.apply_owner(&e.session_id, owner).await;
        }
    }

    async fn on_tick(&mut self) {
        let now = Instant::now();
        let gone: Vec<String> = self
            .sessions
            .iter()
            .filter(|(_, s)| {
                s.ended.is_some_and(|t| now.duration_since(t) >= self.cfg.end_grace)
                    || now.duration_since(s.last_event) >= self.cfg.idle_expiry
            })
            .map(|(k, _)| k.clone())
            .collect();
        for sid in gone {
            if let Some(s) = self.sessions.remove(&sid)
                && s.hooks_only
            {
                self.core.observe(Observation::AgentGone { agent_id: format!("claude:{sid}") });
            }
        }
    }
}

fn hooks_only_obs(id: &str, s: &Session) -> AgentObs {
    let project = s
        .cwd
        .as_deref()
        .and_then(|c| Path::new(c).file_name())
        .map(|n| clean_display(&n.to_string_lossy(), TITLE_CHARS))
        .filter(|p| !p.is_empty());
    let title = s
        .name
        .as_deref()
        .map(|n| clean_display(n, TITLE_CHARS))
        .filter(|t| !t.is_empty())
        .or_else(|| project.clone())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "Claude".into());
    AgentObs {
        id: id.into(),
        source: Source::Hooks,
        kind: AgentKind::Claude,
        title,
        cwd: s.cwd.as_deref().map(|c| clean_display(c, usize::MAX)),
        project,
        state: s.state,
        failed: false,
        last_message: s.last_message.clone(),
        can_send: false,
    }
}

async fn changed(rx: &mut Option<watch::Receiver<u64>>) {
    match rx {
        Some(r) if r.changed().await.is_ok() => {}
        _ => std::future::pending().await,
    }
}
```

Note: `apply_owner` re-upserts a hooks-only agent on every event, which
is how state changes (`working`, `done`) reach the registry; the
registry drops identical upserts.

### Task 12: Outcome rules

Pure rules used by Tasks 14 to 17: what a later hook event says about a
request (spec §5.1 Attribution, §6.2 step 3, §6.3 Completion; Occurrence
contract O5). No state, so this task runs in parallel with Task 11.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/evidence.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/mod.rs` (module list)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7 (`HookEvent`, `HookName`, `tool_digest`, `command_of`),
  herdr `keys::Verdict`.
- Produces:
  - `evidence::Expect { ClaudeTool { session_id, tool_name, digest }, ClaudeQuestion { session_id, question: String, labels: HashMap<String, String> }, CodexTool { session_id, tool_name, command } }`.
  - `evidence::Seen { ToolRan, TurnEnded, Answered(String) }`,
    `evidence::Sent { Allow, Deny, Choose(String) }`,
    `evidence::observe(expect: &Expect, ev: &HookEvent) -> Option<Seen>`,
    `evidence::verdict(sent: &Sent, seen: &Seen) -> Verdict` (herdr's `keys::Verdict`),
    `evidence::sent_for_effect(Effect) -> Option<Sent>`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `evidence.rs`**

```rust
//! Correlated outcome evidence (spec §5.1 Attribution, §6.2 step 3, §6.3
//! Completion). Pure rules here; `EvidenceHub` (Task 16) feeds the keys
//! engine with them.
use std::collections::HashMap;

use acd_protocol::Effect;
use serde_json::Value;

use super::wire::{HookEvent, HookName, command_of, tool_digest};
pub use crate::herdr::keys::Verdict;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Expect {
    ClaudeTool { session_id: String, tool_name: String, digest: String },
    ClaudeQuestion { session_id: String, question: String, labels: HashMap<String, String> },
    CodexTool { session_id: String, tool_name: String, command: String },
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Seen {
    ToolRan,
    TurnEnded,
    Answered(String),
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Sent {
    Allow,
    Deny,
    Choose(String),
}

pub fn sent_for_effect(e: Effect) -> Option<Sent> {
    match e {
        Effect::Allow | Effect::AllowAlways => Some(Sent::Allow),
        Effect::Deny => Some(Sent::Deny),
        Effect::Choose | Effect::Unknown => None,
    }
}

fn session_of(e: &Expect) -> &str {
    match e {
        Expect::ClaudeTool { session_id, .. }
        | Expect::ClaudeQuestion { session_id, .. }
        | Expect::CodexTool { session_id, .. } => session_id,
    }
}

/// What `ev` says about the expected request, if anything.
pub fn observe(expect: &Expect, ev: &HookEvent) -> Option<Seen> {
    if ev.payload.session_id != session_of(expect) {
        return None;
    }
    let turn_end = matches!(ev.name, HookName::Stop | HookName::UserPromptSubmit);
    let tool = ev.payload.tool_name.as_deref();
    match expect {
        Expect::ClaudeTool { tool_name, digest, .. } => {
            if ev.name == HookName::PostToolUse
                && tool == Some(tool_name.as_str())
                && tool_digest(tool_name, &ev.payload.tool_input) == *digest
            {
                Some(Seen::ToolRan)
            } else {
                turn_end.then_some(Seen::TurnEnded)
            }
        }
        Expect::ClaudeQuestion { question, .. } => {
            if ev.name == HookName::PostToolUse && tool == Some("AskUserQuestion") {
                let answer = ev.payload.tool_response.get("answers").or_else(|| ev.payload.tool_input.get("answers"))
                    .and_then(|a| a.get(question.as_str()))
                    .and_then(Value::as_str)
                    .unwrap_or_default();
                Some(Seen::Answered(answer.to_owned()))
            } else {
                turn_end.then_some(Seen::TurnEnded)
            }
        }
        Expect::CodexTool { tool_name, command, .. } => {
            if ev.name == HookName::PostToolUse
                && tool == Some(tool_name.as_str())
                && command_of(&ev.payload.tool_input) == Some(command.as_str())
            {
                Some(Seen::ToolRan)
            } else {
                turn_end.then_some(Seen::TurnEnded)
            }
        }
    }
}

pub fn verdict(sent: &Sent, seen: &Seen) -> Verdict {
    match (sent, seen) {
        (Sent::Allow, Seen::ToolRan) | (Sent::Deny, Seen::TurnEnded) => Verdict::Consistent,
        (Sent::Choose(want), Seen::Answered(got)) if want == got => Verdict::Consistent,
        _ => Verdict::Contradicts,
    }
}
```

`mod.rs` adds `pub mod evidence;`.

### Task 13: Exact dialog matching

The Dialog match contract (M1 to M5), and its adversarial tests (Sol
B1). This replaces the whitespace-stripping `contains` match of
revision 1, which let a hook for `touch /tmp/x` match a dialog for
`touch /tmp/x; rm -rf ~`.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/herdr/screen.rs` (`Dialog::region`, `Dialog::option_rows`)
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/dialogs.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/mod.rs` (module list)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7 (`QuestionSpec`), herdr `screen::find_dialog`.
- Produces:
  - `screen::Dialog` gains `pub region: Vec<String>` (the raw rows of
    the located region, first row to footer, as read) and
    `pub option_rows: Vec<usize>` (indexes into `region`). For P3 hook
    matching only; the guard never reads them.
  - `dialogs::DialogMatch { ClaudeBash { command: String }, ClaudeQuestion { spec: QuestionSpec }, CodexCommand { command: String } }`,
    `DialogMatch::matches(&self, d: &Dialog) -> bool`,
    `DialogMatch::find(&self, screen: &str) -> Option<Dialog>`,
    `DialogMatch::collapsed_codex(screen: &str) -> bool`,
    `dialogs::single_row(s: &str) -> bool` (M4).

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn screen(name: &str) -> String {
        std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
    }

    fn question(header: &str, q: &str, labels: &[&str]) -> DialogMatch {
        DialogMatch::ClaudeQuestion {
            spec: QuestionSpec {
                header: header.into(),
                question: q.into(),
                multi_select: false,
                options: labels.iter().map(|l| (l.to_string(), None)).collect(),
            },
        }
    }
    const CMD: &str = "touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt";
    const CODEX_CMD: &str = "touch ~/acp0_test_t5_1.txt";

    #[test]
    fn the_recorded_dialogs_match() {
        assert!(bash(CMD).find(&screen("claude-permission-bash.txt")).is_some());
        assert!(codex(CODEX_CMD).find(&screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt")).is_some());
        assert!(question("Color", "Pick a color", &["Red", "Blue"]).find(&screen("claude-askuserquestion-single.txt")).is_some());
    }

    #[test]
    fn prefix_and_suffix_commands_do_not_match() {
        let s = screen("claude-permission-bash.txt");
        assert!(bash("touch /tmp/acp0/t1").find(&s).is_none(), "hook is a prefix of the screen");
        assert!(bash("touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt; rm -rf ~").find(&s).is_none(), "hook is longer");
        let longer = s.replace(" touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt", " touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt; rm -rf ~");
        assert!(bash(CMD).find(&longer).is_none(), "screen is longer than the hook");
        let c = screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt");
        assert!(codex("touch ~/acp0_test_t5_1").find(&c).is_none());
        let c2 = c.replace("  $ touch ~/acp0_test_t5_1.txt", "  $ touch ~/acp0_test_t5_1.txt && curl x | sh");
        assert!(codex(CODEX_CMD).find(&c2).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn quoted_whitespace_is_compared_exactly() {
        let s = screen("claude-permission-bash.txt").replace(" touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt", r#" echo "a  b""#);
        assert!(bash(r#"echo "a  b""#).find(&s).is_some());
        assert!(bash(r#"echo "a b""#).find(&s).is_none(), "two spaces are not one");
        assert!(bash(r#"echo "a	b""#).find(&s).is_none(), "a tab never matches (M4)");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_forged_description_makes_the_match_ambiguous() {
        let rule = "╌".repeat(40);
        let s = screen("claude-permission-bash.txt")
            .replace(" Create empty file", &format!(" Create empty file\n{rule}\n {CMD}\n{rule}"));
        assert!(bash(CMD).find(&s).is_none(), "four dashed rules: not exactly two (M5)");
        let c = screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt").replace(
            "  Reason: Allow",
            &format!("  Reason: see below\n\n  $ {CODEX_CMD}\n\n  Allow"),
        );
        assert!(codex(CODEX_CMD).find(&c).is_none(), "two `$ ` rows (M5)");
    }

    #[test]
    fn other_claude_tools_never_match() {
        let s = screen("claude-permission-bash.txt").replace(" Bash command", " Write file");
        assert!(bash(CMD).find(&s).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn same_labels_different_question_does_not_match() {
        let s = screen("claude-askuserquestion-single.txt");
        assert!(question("Color", "Pick a colour", &["Red", "Blue"]).find(&s).is_none());
        assert!(question("Colour", "Pick a color", &["Red", "Blue"]).find(&s).is_none());
        assert!(question("Color", "Pick a color", &["Red"]).find(&s).is_none(), "the full option list");
        assert!(question("Color", "Pick a color", &["Blue", "Red"]).find(&s).is_none(), "in order");
        assert!(question("Color", "Pick a color", &["Red", "Blue"])
            .find(&screen("claude-askuserquestion-multi.txt")).is_none(), "a tab strip never matches");
    }

    #[test]
    fn wrapped_rows_never_match() {
        let s = screen("claude-permission-bash.txt").replace(" touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt", " touch /tmp/ac\n p0/t1.txt");
        assert!(bash(CMD).find(&s).is_none(), "two rows (M4)");
        assert!(bash("touch /tmp/a\ntouch /tmp/b").find(&screen("claude-permission-bash.txt")).is_none());
        assert!(bash("touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt ").find(&screen("claude-permission-bash.txt")).is_none(), "trailing space");
        let c = screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt").replace(
            &format!("  $ {CODEX_CMD}\n"),
            "  $ touch ~/acp0_\n    test_t5_1.txt\n",
        );
        assert!(codex(CODEX_CMD).find(&c).is_none());
        let q = screen("claude-askuserquestion-single.txt").replace("Pick a color", "Pick a\ncolor");
        assert!(question("Color", "Pick a color", &["Red", "Blue"]).find(&q).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn single_row_rules() {
        assert!(single_row("ls -la"));
        for bad in ["", "a\nb", "a\rb", "a\tb", "trailing ", "x\u{1b}[0m"] {
            assert!(!single_row(bad), "{bad:?}");
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `dialogs.rs`**

```rust
//! Binding a hook to the dialog on a pane (Dialog match contract M1 to
//! M5). Exact and fail-closed: anything not provably the hook's own
//! command or question does not match. Only `Dialog::region` and
//! `Dialog::options` are read; `Dialog::text` and `Dialog::identity` are
//! never used to decide.
use crate::herdr::screen::{Dialog, DialogKind, find_dialog};
use super::mapping::QuestionSpec;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum DialogMatch {
    ClaudeBash { command: String },
    ClaudeQuestion { spec: QuestionSpec },
    CodexCommand { command: String },
}

/// M4: a value that can only render on one row and compares exactly.
pub fn single_row(s: &str) -> bool {
    !s.is_empty() && !s.chars().any(char::is_control) && s.trim_end() == s
}

fn blank(row: &str) -> bool {
    row.trim().is_empty()
}

fn rule(row: &str) -> bool {
    let t = row.trim();
    t.chars().count() >= 10 && t.chars().all(|c| c == '─' || c == '╌')
}

fn dashed_rule(row: &str) -> bool {
    let t = row.trim();
    t.chars().count() >= 10 && t.chars().all(|c| c == '╌')
}

/// The rows above the first option.
fn head(d: &Dialog) -> Option<&[String]> {
    let first = *d.option_rows.first()?;
    Some(&d.region[..first])
}

/// M1.
fn claude_bash(d: &Dialog, command: &str) -> bool {
    if d.kind != DialogKind::ClaudePermission || !single_row(command) {
        return false;
    }
    let Some(rows) = head(d) else { return false };
    let Some(title) = rows.iter().skip(1).find(|r| !blank(r)) else { return false };
    if title.trim() != "Bash command" {
        return false;
    }
    let dashed: Vec<usize> = rows.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_, r)| dashed_rule(r)).map(|(i, _)| i).collect();
    let [a, b] = dashed[..] else { return false };
    if b != a + 2 {
        return false; // zero rows or a wrapped (multi-row) command
    }
    rows[a + 1].strip_prefix(' ').is_some_and(|r| r.trim_end() == command)
}

/// M2.
fn codex_command(d: &Dialog, command: &str) -> bool {
    if d.kind != DialogKind::CodexPermission || !single_row(command) {
        return false;
    }
    let Some(rows) = head(d) else { return false };
    let cmd_rows: Vec<usize> = rows.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_, r)| r.starts_with("  $ ")).map(|(i, _)| i).collect();
    let [i] = cmd_rows[..] else { return false };
    let single = rows.get(i + 1).is_none_or(|r| blank(r));
    single && rows[i]["  $ ".len()..].trim_end() == command
}

/// M3.
fn claude_question(d: &Dialog, spec: &QuestionSpec) -> bool {
    if d.kind != (DialogKind::ClaudeQuestion { tabs: false })
        || spec.multi_select
        || !single_row(&spec.question)
        || !single_row(&spec.header)
        || !spec.options.iter().all(|(l, _)| single_row(l))
    {
        return false;
    }
    let Some(rows) = head(d) else { return false };
    let headers: Vec<usize> = rows.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_, r)| r.contains('☐')).map(|(i, _)| i).collect();
    let [h] = headers[..] else { return false };
    if rows[h].trim() != format!("☐ {}", spec.header) {
        return false;
    }
    let between: Vec<&String> = rows[h + 1..].iter().filter(|r| !blank(r) && !rule(r)).collect();
    if between.len() != 1 || between[0].trim() != spec.question {
        return false;
    }
    let n = spec.options.len();
    d.options.len() == n + 2
        && d.options.iter().enumerate().all(|(i, o)| usize::from(o.number) == i + 1)
        && spec.options.iter().zip(&d.options).all(|((label, _), o)| o.label == *label)
        && d.options[n].label == "Type something."
        && d.options[n + 1].label == "Chat about this"
}

impl DialogMatch {
    pub fn matches(&self, d: &Dialog) -> bool {
        match self {
            DialogMatch::ClaudeBash { command } => claude_bash(d, command),
            DialogMatch::ClaudeQuestion { spec } => claude_question(d, spec),
            DialogMatch::CodexCommand { command } => codex_command(d, command),
        }
    }

    pub fn find(&self, screen: &str) -> Option<Dialog> {
        find_dialog(screen).filter(|d| self.matches(d))
    }

    /// A collapsed Codex dialog: reported, never matched (M2).
    pub fn collapsed_codex(screen: &str) -> bool {
        find_dialog(screen).is_some_and(|d| d.kind == DialogKind::CodexCollapsed)
    }
}
```

`mod.rs` adds `pub mod dialogs;`.

```rust
async fn answer(r: &Rig, id: &str, rid: &str, option: &str) -> CommandResult {
    r.core.handle("c", ClientMessage::RequestAnswer {
        id: id.into(), request_id: rid.into(), revision: None, option_id: Some(option.into()), text: None,
    }).await
}

async fn resolution(rx: &mut tokio::sync::broadcast::Receiver<ServerMessage>, rid: &str) -> ResolvedBy {
    loop {
        if let ServerMessage::RequestResolved { request_id, by, .. } = rx.recv().await.unwrap()
            && request_id == rid
        {
            return by;
        }
    }
}

fn pending_id(r: &Rig, agent: &str) -> String {
    r.agent(agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id).expect("pending request")
}

    v["session_id"] = SID.into();
    v
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `deliver`**

In `hooks/mod.rs`, replace the Task 11 stub:

```rust
    /// Writes the reply into the request's hold. Nothing reached Claude if
    /// the hold is gone (closed, expired or released): `NotDelivered`, and
    /// the core puts the request back to `open`.
    async fn deliver(&self, request: &Request, answer: &Answer) -> Result<(), DeliverError> {
        use acd_protocol::Effect;
        let Answer::Option(option_id) = answer else {
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::NotSupported));
        };
        let Some(effect) = request.options.iter().find(|o| &o.id == option_id).map(|o| o.effect) else {
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::BadRequest));
        };
        let mut held = self.shared.held.lock().expect("held");
        let Some(h) = held.get_mut(&request.id) else {
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::RequestGone));
        };
        let body = match (effect, &h.rule) {
            (Effect::Allow, _) => wire::reply_allow(),
            (Effect::Deny, _) => wire::reply_deny(),
            (Effect::AllowAlways, Some(rule)) => wire::reply_allow_always(rule),
            _ => return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::BadRequest)),
        };
        let Some(reply) = h.reply.take() else {
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::RequestGone));
        };
        h.delivered = Some((effect, tokio::time::Instant::now()));
        if reply.send(body).is_err() {
            h.delivered = None;
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::RequestGone));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
```

```rust
use acd_core::CorrelationKey;
use acd_protocol::{AnswerPath, RequestKind, ResolvedBy};
use super::dialogs::DialogMatch;
use super::evidence::{self, Expect, Verdict};
use super::mapping;
use super::dialogs::single_row;
use super::wire::{Payload, always_rule, claude_key, command_of, tool_digest};

#[allow(dead_code)] // `HoldEnded` and `Keys` are constructed from Task 15
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum Phase {
    /// Hook path, hold open, nothing written.
    Held,
    /// `deliver` wrote the reply; awaiting evidence (O6). Derived by
    /// `phase()` from the shared `delivered` stamp, never stored.
    Delivered,
    /// Hook path, hold closed with no delivery; waiting for evidence.
    HoldEnded { since: Instant },
    /// Answer path `keys`: the herdr engine and target watch own it.
    Keys,
    /// Not answerable; ended by evidence or the dialog leaving the screen.
    ReadOnly,
}

#[allow(dead_code)] // `matcher`, `pane`, `capture`, `absent`, `next_check`, `read_seq` are read from Task 15
struct Track {
    agent_id: String,
    session_id: String,
    key: CorrelationKey,
    /// G3: unique per occurrence; tickets carry it.
    op: u64,
    /// G3: bumped for every spawned read; only the latest result applies.
    read_seq: u64,
    payload: Payload,
    expect: Expect,
    /// The exact screen match for this hook (M1), if it can have one.
    matcher: Option<DialogMatch>,
    pane: Option<PaneFact>,
    phase: Phase,
    opened_at: Instant,
    /// The detection snapshot that matched this request's dialog.
    capture: Option<String>,
    absent: u8,
    next_check: Instant,
}
```

The new methods:

```rust
    fn fallback_key(&self, agent_id: &str) -> Option<CorrelationKey> {
        self.core
            .pending_key(agent_id)
            .filter(|(_, _, kind)| *kind == RequestKind::BlockedUnparsed)
            .map(|(_, key, _)| key)
    }

    /// Observes under the per-agent delivery lock for herdr agents (D7).
    async fn observe_locked(&self, agent_id: &str, herdr_agent: bool, obs: Observation) {
        let locks = self.herdr.as_ref().filter(|_| herdr_agent).map(|h| h.agent_locks());
        let _g = match &locks {
            Some(l) => Some(l.lock(agent_id).await),
            None => None,
        };
        self.core.observe(obs);
    }

    fn bound_pane(&self, sid: &str) -> Option<PaneFact> {
        let Owner::Herdr(agent) = &self.sessions.get(sid)?.owner else { return None };
        self.panes().into_iter().find(|p| p.agent_id == *agent)
    }

    async fn open_claude_permission(&mut self, event: HookEvent, hold: Option<(HoldId, oneshot::Sender<String>)>) {
        let sid = event.payload.session_id.clone();
        let Some(agent_id) = self.sessions.get(&sid).and_then(|s| s.agent_id.clone()) else { return };
        let pane = self.bound_pane(&sid);
        let Some(key) = claude_key(&event.payload) else { return };
        // O1: a new dialog means the session's previous one ended.
        self.end_session_occurrences(&sid).await;
        let mut obs = mapping::claude_permission(&agent_id, key.clone(), self.fallback_key(&agent_id), &event.payload);
        if hold.is_none() {
            // Over the hold cap: no hook path. A bound pane can still be
            // answered with keys once its dialog is captured.
            if pane.is_none() {
                return;
            }
            obs.answer_path = AnswerPath::Keys;
            obs.options = mapping::claude_keys_options();
            obs.ready = false;
        }
        self.observe_locked(&agent_id, pane.is_some(), Observation::RequestSeen(obs)).await;
        let Some(rid) = self.core.request_id_for(&agent_id, &key) else { return };
        let tool_name = event.payload.tool_name.clone().unwrap_or_default();
        // M1: only a Bash command that renders on one row can ever match.
        let matcher = command_of(&event.payload.tool_input)
            .filter(|c| tool_name == "Bash" && single_row(c))
            .map(|c| DialogMatch::ClaudeBash { command: c.to_owned() });
        // The rule the client was shown (Task 7), never rebuilt here.
        let rule = always_rule(&event.payload).filter(|r| mapping::rule_displayable(&mapping::rule_text(r)));
        let phase = match hold {
            Some((hold_id, reply)) => {
                self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").insert(rid.clone(), HeldReq {
                    hold: hold_id,
                    reply: Some(reply),
                    rule,
                    delivered: None,
                });
                self.holds.insert(hold_id, rid.clone());
                Phase::Held
            }
            None => Phase::ReadOnly, // becomes Keys once captured
        };
        let now = Instant::now();
        self.next_op += 1;
        self.tracks.insert(rid.clone(), Track {
            agent_id,
            session_id: sid.clone(),
            key,
            op: self.next_op,
            read_seq: 0,
            expect: Expect::ClaudeTool {
                session_id: sid,
                digest: tool_digest(&tool_name, &event.payload.tool_input),
                tool_name,
            },
            matcher,
            payload: event.payload,
            pane: pane.clone(),
            phase,
            opened_at: now,
            capture: None,
            absent: 0,
            next_check: now + self.cfg.held_dialog_poll,
        });
        self.sweep();
    }


    fn delivered(&self, rid: &str) -> Option<(Effect, Instant)> {
        self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").get(rid).and_then(|h| h.delivered)
    }

    /// O6: `Held` becomes `Delivered` once `deliver` stamped the reply.
    fn phase(&self, rid: &str) -> Option<Phase> {
        let t = self.tracks.get(rid)?;
        Some(match t.phase {
            Phase::Held if self.delivered(rid).is_some() => Phase::Delivered,
            p => p,
        })
    }

    /// O1: before a new occurrence of `sid` opens, its open one ends.
    /// Held (nothing written) and every screen-bound phase resolve
    /// `terminal`; Delivered resolves `uncertain` (D10). Holds are released
    /// empty and targets retired (inside the delivery lock).
    async fn end_session_occurrences(&mut self, sid: &str) {
        let ids: Vec<String> = self.tracks.iter().filter(|(_, t)| t.session_id == sid).map(|(k, _)| k.clone()).collect();
        for rid in ids {
            let by = match self.phase(&rid) {
                Some(Phase::Delivered) => ResolvedBy::Uncertain,
                _ => ResolvedBy::Terminal,
            };
            let agent = self.tracks.get(&rid).map(|t| t.agent_id.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
            let locks = self.herdr.as_ref().map(|h| h.agent_locks());
            let _g = match &locks {
                Some(l) => Some(l.lock(&agent).await),
                None => None,
            };
            if let Some(h) = &self.herdr {
                h.keys_targets().remove(&rid);
            }
            if by == ResolvedBy::Uncertain {
                tracing::debug!(request_id = %rid, "superseded before its evidence arrived (D10)");
            }
            self.end(&rid, by);
        }
    }

    /// Ends a request and forgets it; dropping its reply sender releases a
    /// still-open hold with an empty reply.
    fn end(&mut self, rid: &str, by: ResolvedBy) {
        self.core.observe(Observation::RequestEndedById { request_id: rid.to_owned(), by });
        self.forget(rid);
    }

    /// Forgets a request. Its occurrence counts as confirmed gone whatever
    /// the outcome (spec §5.1: the hook source reported the end), so a
    /// repeat of the same reusable key opens a new request (D2).
    fn forget(&mut self, rid: &str) {
        if let Some(h) = self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").remove(rid) {
            self.holds.remove(&h.hold);
        }
        self.core.observe(Observation::DialogGoneById { request_id: rid.to_owned() });
        self.tracks.remove(rid);
    }

    /// Drops tracks whose request the registry already resolved
    /// (superseded, agent gone, migrated away with a conflict).
    fn sweep(&mut self) {
        let dead: Vec<String> = self.tracks.keys().filter(|rid| self.core.request(rid).is_none()).cloned().collect();
        for rid in dead {
            if self.delivered(&rid).is_some() {
                tracing::debug!(request_id = %rid, "request ended before its evidence arrived (D10)");
            }
            self.forget(&rid);
        }
    }

    /// Spec §5.1 and §6.2 step 3 for requests the hook path owns.
    fn apply_evidence(&mut self, ev: &HookEvent) {
        let ids: Vec<String> = self
            .tracks
            .iter()
            .filter(|(_, t)| t.session_id == ev.payload.session_id && t.phase != Phase::Keys && ev.at >= t.opened_at)
            .map(|(k, _)| k.clone())
            .collect();
        for rid in ids {
            let Some(seen) = self.tracks.get(&rid).and_then(|t| evidence::observe(&t.expect, ev)) else { continue };
            let by = match self.delivered(&rid) {
                Some((_, at)) if ev.at < at => continue,
                Some((effect, _)) => match evidence::sent_for_effect(effect).map(|s| evidence::verdict(&s, &seen)) {
                    Some(Verdict::Consistent) => ResolvedBy::Inbox,
                    _ => ResolvedBy::Terminal,
                },
                // The dialog ended without any acd delivery.
                None => ResolvedBy::Terminal,
            };
            self.end(&rid, by);
        }
    }

```

T3 takeover and agent removal reach holds through `sweep` (the request
is gone from the registry, so its reply sender drops). Call
`self.sweep()` at the end of `apply_owner` too, so a hold is released in
the same turn as the takeover.

```rust
/// Unheld permissions (hold cap 0) go straight to the keys path once
/// captured, so a stale capture would register a target: the sharpest
/// stale-read probe. The pane is `working`, so herdr's own fallback and
/// watch never read it and the gate catches P3's capture read.
async fn stale_rig() -> (Rig, String, Vec<(&'static str, String)>) {
    let r = rig(&[fake_agent("w1:p1", "claude", "working")], |c| c.max_holds = 0).await;
    let dialog = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/claude-permission-bash.txt", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap();
    r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &dialog);
    let agent = r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().keys().next().unwrap().clone();
    let h = bound_headers(&r);
    (r, agent, h)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Read tickets (`tickets.rs`)**

```rust
//! Read tickets (Read ticket contract G1 to G6, Sol B2). A spawned screen
//! read carries what must still hold when its result comes back; the
//! reconciler gathers `Now` inside the delivery lock and applies the
//! result only if `still_valid`.
use acd_protocol::{AnswerPath, RequestStatus};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct Ticket {
    pub agent_id: String,
    pub incarnation: u64,
    pub session_id: String,
    pub bind_gen: u64,
    /// `None` for a Codex binding watch, which runs before a request exists.
    pub request_id: Option<String>,
    pub op: u64,
    pub read_seq: u64,
}

/// What holds now, gathered inside the delivery lock of `agent_id`.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct Now {
    /// The pane map's incarnation for the agent, if it is still there.
    pub incarnation: Option<u64>,
    /// The session's current binding generation.
    pub bind_gen: Option<u64>,
    pub t3_owned: bool,
    /// `(op, read_seq)` of the ticket's track, if it still exists.
    pub track: Option<(u64, u64)>,
    /// The request's status and path, if it still exists.
    pub request: Option<(RequestStatus, AnswerPath)>,
}

/// G5. `paths`: the answer paths a result may act on.
pub(crate) fn still_valid(t: &Ticket, now: &Now, paths: &[AnswerPath]) -> bool {
    let common = now.incarnation == Some(t.incarnation) && now.bind_gen == Some(t.bind_gen) && !now.t3_owned;
    match &t.request_id {
        None => common,
        Some(_) => {
            common
                && now.track == Some((t.op, t.read_seq))
                && now.request.is_some_and(|(st, path)| st == RequestStatus::Open && paths.contains(&path))
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn ticket() -> Ticket {
        Ticket {
            agent_id: "herdr:h:w1:p1".into(), incarnation: 7, session_id: "s".into(), bind_gen: 3,
            request_id: Some("r1".into()), op: 11, read_seq: 2,
        }
    }
    fn now() -> Now {
        Now {
            incarnation: Some(7), bind_gen: Some(3), t3_owned: false, track: Some((11, 2)),
            request: Some((RequestStatus::Open, AnswerPath::Hook)),
        }
    }
    const P: &[AnswerPath] = &[AnswerPath::Hook];

    #[test]
    fn a_fresh_ticket_is_valid() {
        assert!(still_valid(&ticket(), &now(), P));
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_change_invalidates() {
        let t = ticket();
        for (why, n) in [
            ("pane replaced", Now { incarnation: Some(8), ..now() }),
            ("pane gone", Now { incarnation: None, ..now() }),
            ("rebound", Now { bind_gen: Some(4), ..now() }),
            ("T3 took over", Now { t3_owned: true, ..now() }),
            ("occurrence ended (Stop, new prompt)", Now { track: None, ..now() }),
            ("another occurrence", Now { track: Some((12, 0)), ..now() }),
            ("request answering", Now { request: Some((RequestStatus::Answering, AnswerPath::Hook)), ..now() }),
            ("request resolved", Now { request: None, ..now() }),
            ("path changed", Now { request: Some((RequestStatus::Open, AnswerPath::None)), ..now() }),
        ] {
            assert!(!still_valid(&t, &n, P), "{why}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn out_of_order_reads_keep_the_latest() {
        // Two reads were spawned (read_seq 1, then 2); 1 completes last.
        let older = Ticket { read_seq: 1, ..ticket() };
        assert!(!still_valid(&older, &now(), P), "only the latest read applies");
        assert!(still_valid(&ticket(), &now(), P));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_watch_without_a_request_checks_pane_binding_and_owner() {
        let w = Ticket { request_id: None, op: 0, read_seq: 1, ..ticket() };
        assert!(still_valid(&w, &Now { track: None, request: None, ..now() }, P));
        assert!(!still_valid(&w, &Now { bind_gen: Some(9), track: None, request: None, ..now() }, P));
    }
}
```

```rust
use acd_protocol::RequestStatus;
use tokio::sync::Semaphore;
use super::tickets::{Now, Ticket, still_valid};
use crate::herdr::client::CALL_TIMEOUT;
use crate::herdr::keys::KeysTarget;
use crate::herdr::reconciler::PaneRef;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum Purpose {
    /// Find this request's dialog within `capture_window`.
    Capture,
    /// One read: is the matched dialog still shown?
    Presence,
    /// One read after the hold closed (`expired: false`) or expired.
    HoldEnded { expired: bool },
}

/// What a read found. `Unavailable` (failed or truncated) is never taken
/// as proof that a dialog is gone (Sol minor 1).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum ReadResult {
    Matched(String),
    NoMatch,
    Unavailable,
}

// `Internal` gains:
//     Screen { ticket: Ticket, purpose: Purpose, result: ReadResult },
```

The read side:

```rust
    /// Issues a ticket for `rid`'s track (bumping its `read_seq`) and the
    /// pane it reads. `None` when the track, its pane or a matcher is gone.
    fn ticket_for(&mut self, rid: &str) -> Option<(Ticket, PaneRef, DialogMatch)> {
        let herdr = self.herdr.clone()?;
        let t = self.tracks.get_mut(rid)?;
        let pane = t.pane.clone()?;
        let matcher = t.matcher.clone()?;
        let pref = herdr.panes().read().expect("pane map").get(&pane.agent_id).cloned()?;
        if pref.incarnation != pane.incarnation {
            return None; // replaced since binding: never read on its behalf
        }
        t.read_seq += 1;
        let ticket = Ticket {
            agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(),
            incarnation: pane.incarnation,
            session_id: t.session_id.clone(),
            bind_gen: 0, // filled below
            request_id: Some(rid.to_owned()),
            op: t.op,
            read_seq: t.read_seq,
        };
        let bind_gen = self.bind_gen_of(&ticket.session_id)?;
        Some((Ticket { bind_gen, ..ticket }, pref, matcher))
    }

    /// G2 for Claude sessions; Task 17 extends it to Codex sessions.
    fn bind_gen_of(&self, sid: &str) -> Option<u64> {
        self.sessions.get(sid).map(|s| s.bind_gen)
    }

    /// Spawns one read. `Capture` polls until the matcher finds the dialog
    /// or `capture_window` passes; the others read once. Presence reads
    /// are skipped when all 8 read slots are busy (G6).
    fn spawn_read(&mut self, rid: &str, purpose: Purpose) {
        let Some((ticket, pref, matcher)) = self.ticket_for(rid) else { return };
        let permit = match purpose {
            Purpose::Presence => match self.reads.clone().try_acquire_owned() {
                Ok(p) => Some(p),
                Err(_) => return,
            },
            _ => None,
        };
        let reads = self.reads.clone();
        let tx = self.internal_tx.clone();
        let (window, poll) = (self.cfg.capture_window, self.cfg.capture_poll);
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let _permit = match permit {
                Some(p) => p,
                None => reads.acquire_owned().await.expect("never closed"),
            };
            let deadline = Instant::now() + window;
            loop {
                let read = pref
                    .herdr
                    .read_screen(&pref.pane_id, "detection", None, Instant::now() + CALL_TIMEOUT)
                    .await
                    .ok()
                    .filter(|r| !r.truncated);
                let result = match read {
                    None => ReadResult::Unavailable,
                    Some(r) if matcher.find(&r.text).is_some() => ReadResult::Matched(r.text),
                    Some(_) => ReadResult::NoMatch,
                };
                let retry = purpose == Purpose::Capture
                    && !matches!(result, ReadResult::Matched(_))
                    && Instant::now() < deadline;
                if !retry {
                    let _ = tx.send(Internal::Screen { ticket, purpose, result });
                    return;
                }
                tokio::time::sleep(poll).await;
            }
        });
    }

    /// G5's inputs, read inside the delivery lock of `t.agent_id`.
    fn now_for(&self, t: &Ticket) -> Now {
        let incarnation = self
            .herdr
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|h| h.panes().read().expect("pane map").get(&t.agent_id).map(|p| p.incarnation));
        let track = t.request_id.as_ref().and_then(|rid| self.tracks.get(rid)).map(|tr| (tr.op, tr.read_seq));
        let request = t.request_id.as_ref().and_then(|rid| self.core.request(rid)).map(|r| (r.status, r.answer_path));
        Now {
            incarnation,
            bind_gen: self.bind_gen_of(&t.session_id),
            t3_owned: self.t3.as_ref().is_some_and(|m| m.owner(&t.session_id).is_some()),
            track,
            request,
        }
    }
```

The result side, all inside the delivery lock (G5):

```rust
    async fn on_screen(&mut self, ticket: Ticket, purpose: Purpose, result: ReadResult) {
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let locks = herdr.agent_locks();
        let _g = locks.lock(&ticket.agent_id).await;
        let paths: &[AnswerPath] = match purpose {
            Purpose::Capture => &[AnswerPath::Hook, AnswerPath::Keys, AnswerPath::None],
            _ => &[AnswerPath::Hook, AnswerPath::None],
        };
        if !still_valid(&ticket, &self.now_for(&ticket), paths) {
            tracing::debug!(?purpose, "dropping a stale screen read");
            return;
        }
        let rid = ticket.request_id.clone().expect("track reads carry a request id");
        match (purpose, result) {
            (Purpose::Capture, ReadResult::Matched(screen)) => {
                if let Some(h) = self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").get_mut(&rid) {
                    h.bound_captured = true;
                }
                let unheld = self.tracks.get(&rid).is_some_and(|t| t.phase == Phase::ReadOnly);
                if let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid) {
                    t.capture = Some(screen.clone());
                }
                if unheld {
                    self.switch_to_keys_locked(&rid, screen, ticket.incarnation); // over the hold cap
                }
            }
            (Purpose::Capture, _) => {} // no match: hold-only (spec §6.2 step 6)
            (Purpose::Presence, ReadResult::Unavailable) => {}
            (Purpose::Presence, r) => {
                let gone = {
                    let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid) else { return };
                    t.absent = if matches!(r, ReadResult::Matched(_)) { 0 } else { t.absent + 1 };
                    t.absent >= 2
                };
                if gone && self.delivered(&rid).is_none() {
                    self.end(&rid, ResolvedBy::Terminal);
                }
            }
            (Purpose::HoldEnded { .. }, ReadResult::Matched(screen)) => {
                self.switch_to_keys_locked(&rid, screen, ticket.incarnation);
            }
            (Purpose::HoldEnded { .. }, ReadResult::NoMatch) => self.end(&rid, ResolvedBy::Terminal),
            (Purpose::HoldEnded { expired }, ReadResult::Unavailable) => {
                // Unavailable is not "gone" (Sol minor 1): retry, then uncertain.
                let tries = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid).map(|t| {
                    t.hold_end_tries += 1;
                    t.hold_end_tries
                });
                if tries.is_some_and(|n| n < 3) {
                    self.spawn_read(&rid, Purpose::HoldEnded { expired });
                } else {
                    self.end(&rid, ResolvedBy::Uncertain);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    async fn on_hold_ended(&mut self, hold: HoldId, expired: bool) {
        let Some(rid) = self.holds.remove(&hold) else { return };
        if let Some(h) = self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").get_mut(&rid) {
            h.reply = None; // a later deliver gets RequestGone or RequestNotReady
        }
        if self.delivered(&rid).is_some() {
            return; // evidence decides
        }
        let now = Instant::now();
        let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid) else { return };
        let captured = t.pane.is_some() && t.capture.is_some();
        t.phase = Phase::HoldEnded { since: now };
        if captured {
            self.spawn_read(&rid, Purpose::HoldEnded { expired });
        } else if expired {
            // Hooks-only, or bound without a matched dialog (spec §6.2 step 6).
            self.end(&rid, ResolvedBy::Lost);
        }
    }

    /// Spec §6.2 steps 4 and 5, inside the delivery lock the caller holds:
    /// rebuild for keys (D9), register a target bound to the pane
    /// incarnation, and only then mark it ready.
    fn switch_to_keys_locked(&mut self, rid: &str, screen: String, incarnation: u64) {
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid) else { return };
        let Some(dialog) = crate::herdr::screen::find_dialog(&screen) else { return };
        let mut obs = mapping::claude_permission(&t.agent_id, t.key.clone(), None, &t.payload);
        obs.answer_path = AnswerPath::Keys;
        obs.options = mapping::claude_keys_options();
        obs.ready = false;
        self.core.observe(Observation::RequestSeen(obs));
        let target = KeysTarget::from_screen(
            rid.to_owned(), t.agent_id.clone(), t.key.clone(), &screen, mapping::claude_permission_keys(&dialog),
        )
        .map(|k| k.for_incarnation(incarnation));
        let open_on_keys = self
            .core
            .request(rid)
            .is_some_and(|r| r.status == RequestStatus::Open && r.answer_path == AnswerPath::Keys);
        match target {
            Ok(target) if open_on_keys && herdr.keys_targets().set(target).is_ok() => {
                self.core.observe(Observation::RequestReady { agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(), key: t.key.clone(), ready: true });
                t.phase = Phase::Keys;
            }
            _ => {
                self.core.observe(Observation::RequestPath {
                    agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(),
                    key: t.key.clone(),
                    answer_path: AnswerPath::None,
                });
                t.phase = Phase::ReadOnly;
            }
        }
    }
```

A claim that lands after the hold closed but before the switch must not
say "answered elsewhere" while the dialog is still up. `HeldReq` (Task
11) gains `bound_captured: bool` (`false` at open; set by a matched
capture above). In `deliver` (Task 14), report `RequestNotReady` for
such a request, so the client retries for 3 s with the same command id
(spec §8) and lands on the keys path:

```rust
        let gone = |h: &HeldReq| {
            DeliverError::NotDelivered(if h.bound_captured { ErrorCode::RequestNotReady } else { ErrorCode::RequestGone })
        };
        // ...
        let Some(reply) = h.reply.take() else {
            return Err(gone(h));
        };
        // ...
        if reply.send(body).is_err() {
            h.delivered = None;
            return Err(gone(h));
        }
```

A migration moves the request to the herdr agent (Task 2), so the track
must follow it. In `apply_owner`'s `Owner::Herdr` branch, right after
observing `AgentMigrated` (still inside that agent's lock), re-point every
track of the session and start a capture on the new pane. `bind_gen`
already moved, so reads issued for the hooks-only phase are dropped:

```rust

                            t.pane = pane.clone();

                        if pane.is_some() {
                            self.spawn_read(&rid, Purpose::Capture);
```

```rust
    fn target(rid: &str) -> crate::herdr::keys::KeysTarget {
        let dialog = std::fs::read_to_string(format!(
            "{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/claude-permission-bash.txt", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap();
        crate::herdr::keys::KeysTarget::from_screen(
            rid.into(), "a".into(), acd_core::CorrelationKey("k".into()), &dialog,
            [("allow".to_string(), "1".to_string()), ("deny".to_string(), "4".to_string())].into()).unwrap()

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn the_hub_uses_only_evidence_after_the_key_and_for_this_request() {
        use crate::herdr::keys::Evidence;
        let hub = EvidenceHub::default();
        hub.expect("r1", bash("touch /tmp/acp0/t2.txt"));
        let mut post = ev("claude/payload-PostToolUse-Bash.json", HookName::PostToolUse);
        hub.publish(&post); // before the key: ignored
        let sent_at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        assert_eq!(
            tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_millis(100), hub.outcome(&target("r1"), "allow", sent_at)).await.ok(),
            None, "an event before sent_at is not evidence");
        post.at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        hub.publish(&post); // after the key: in the backlog
        assert_eq!(hub.outcome(&target("r1"), "allow", sent_at).await, Verdict::Consistent);
        assert_eq!(hub.outcome(&target("r1"), "deny", sent_at).await, Verdict::Contradicts);
        assert_eq!(hub.outcome(&target("r2"), "allow", sent_at).await, Verdict::NoEvidence, "never reused across ids");
        hub.forget("r1");
        assert_eq!(hub.outcome(&target("r1"), "allow", sent_at).await, Verdict::NoEvidence);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn the_hub_waits_for_live_evidence() {
        use crate::herdr::keys::Evidence;
        let hub = std::sync::Arc::new(EvidenceHub::default());
        hub.expect("r1", bash("touch /tmp/acp0/t2.txt"));
        let sent_at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        let h2 = hub.clone();
        let waiting = tokio::spawn(async move { h2.outcome(&target("r1"), "allow", sent_at).await });
        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
        let mut stop = ev("claude/payload-Stop.json", HookName::Stop);
        stop.at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        hub.publish(&stop);
        assert_eq!(waiting.await.unwrap(), Verdict::Contradicts, "allow, then the turn ended without the tool");
    }
```

```rust
fn herdr_screen(name: &str) -> String {
    std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
}

async fn question_rig() -> (Rig, String) {
    let r = rig(&[fake_agent("w1:p1", "claude", "blocked")], |_| {}).await;
    let q = herdr_screen("claude-askuserquestion-single.txt");
    let f = r.fake.as_ref().unwrap();
    f.set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &q);
    f.set_screen("w1:p1", "visible", &q);
    f.on_keys(|f, pane, _| {
        let idle = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/claude-idle-detection.txt", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap();
        let prompt = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/claude-idle-prompt.txt", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap();
        f.set_screen(pane, "detection", &idle);
        f.set_screen(pane, "visible", &prompt);
        f.set_status(pane, "working");
    });
    let agent = r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().keys().next().unwrap().clone();
    (r, agent)
}

fn post_answer(label: &str) -> Value {
    let mut p = fx("claude/payload-PostToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");
    p["tool_response"]["answers"]["Pick a color"] = label.into();
    p["tool_input"]["answers"]["Pick a color"] = label.into();
    p
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_single_question_on_a_bound_pane_is_answered_with_its_number_key() {
    let (r, agent) = question_rig().await;
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    let h = bound_headers(&r);
    let h: Vec<(&str, &str)> = h.iter().map(|(k, v)| (*k, v.as_str())).collect();
    r.hook("claude", "PreToolUse", &fx("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json"), &h).await;
    r.hook("claude", "PermissionRequest", &fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-AskUserQuestion.json"), &h).await;
    let rid = until("question", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).filter(|p| p.kind == RequestKind::Question).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    assert_eq!(r.core.request(&rid).unwrap().answer_path, AnswerPath::Keys);
    until("target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid)).await;
    assert!(answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "o1").await.is_ok());
    assert_eq!(r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().keys_sent().last().map(|k| k.1.clone()), Some(vec!["1".to_string()]));
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &post_answer("Red"), &h).await;
    assert_eq!(resolution(&mut rx, &rid).await, ResolvedBy::Inbox);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_question_answered_otherwise_is_terminal() {
    let (r, agent) = question_rig().await;
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    let h = bound_headers(&r);
    let h: Vec<(&str, &str)> = h.iter().map(|(k, v)| (*k, v.as_str())).collect();
    r.hook("claude", "PreToolUse", &fx("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json"), &h).await;
    let rid = until("question", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).filter(|p| p.kind == RequestKind::Question).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    until("target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid)).await;
    answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "o1").await;
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &post_answer("Blue"), &h).await;
    assert_eq!(resolution(&mut rx, &rid).await, ResolvedBy::Terminal);
}
```

Append to `evidence.rs`:

```rust
use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::sync::Mutex;

use async_trait::async_trait;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;
use tokio::time::Instant;

use crate::herdr::keys::{Evidence, KeysTarget};

const RECENT: usize = 256;

/// Expectations by request id, plus the recent and live hook events. The
/// keys engine asks it for a verdict after a key (P1 follow-ups: only
/// evidence for `target.request_id` that arrived after `sent_at`, never a
/// verdict reused across ids, bounded by the engine's `evidence_timeout`).
pub struct EvidenceHub {
    expects: Mutex<HashMap<String, Expect>>,
    recent: Mutex<VecDeque<HookEvent>>,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<HookEvent>,
}

impl Default for EvidenceHub {
    fn default() -> Self {
        EvidenceHub { expects: Mutex::default(), recent: Mutex::default(), tx: broadcast::channel(RECENT).0 }
    }
}

impl EvidenceHub {
    pub fn expect(&self, request_id: &str, e: Expect) {
        self.expects.lock().expect("evidence").insert(request_id.to_owned(), e);
    }

    pub fn forget(&self, request_id: &str) {
        self.expects.lock().expect("evidence").remove(request_id);
    }

    pub fn publish(&self, ev: &HookEvent) {
        {
            let mut r = self.recent.lock().expect("evidence");
            r.push_back(ev.clone());
            while r.len() > RECENT {
                r.pop_front();
            }
        }
        let _ = self.tx.send(ev.clone());
    }
}

pub fn sent_for_option(expect: &Expect, option_id: &str) -> Option<Sent> {
    match expect {
        Expect::ClaudeQuestion { labels, .. } => labels.get(option_id).cloned().map(Sent::Choose),
        _ => match option_id {
            "allow" | "allow_always" => Some(Sent::Allow),
            "deny" => Some(Sent::Deny),
            _ => None,
        },
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl Evidence for EvidenceHub {
    async fn outcome(&self, target: &KeysTarget, option_id: &str, sent_at: Instant) -> Verdict {
        let Some(expect) = self.expects.lock().expect("evidence").get(target.request_id()).cloned() else {
            return Verdict::NoEvidence;
        };
        let Some(sent) = sent_for_option(&expect, option_id) else {
            return Verdict::NoEvidence;
        };
        // Subscribe before reading the backlog so nothing falls between.
        let mut rx = self.tx.subscribe();
        let backlog: Vec<HookEvent> =
            self.recent.lock().expect("evidence").iter().filter(|e| e.at >= sent_at).cloned().collect();
        for ev in &backlog {
            if let Some(seen) = observe(&expect, ev) {
                return verdict(&sent, &seen);
            }
        }
        loop {
            match rx.recv().await {
                Ok(ev) if ev.at >= sent_at => {
                    if let Some(seen) = observe(&expect, &ev) {
                        return verdict(&sent, &seen);
                    }
                }
                Ok(_) | Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Lagged(_)) => {}
                Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Closed) => return Verdict::NoEvidence,
            }
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Questions and track kinds in the reconciler**

`Track` gains `kind: TrackKind`:

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum TrackKind {
    Permission,
    Question,
    Codex,
}
```

Set `kind: TrackKind::Permission` in `open_claude_permission`. At the top
of `on_event`, publish every event: `self.evidence.publish(&event);`.
In `forget`, also call `self.evidence.forget(rid)`. In
`switch_to_keys_locked`, after `t.phase = Phase::Keys`, call
`self.evidence.expect(rid, t.expect.clone())`.

In `on_claude`, add the question branch next to the permission one:

```rust
        let ask = matches!(event.name, HookName::PreToolUse | HookName::PermissionRequest)
            && tool == Some("AskUserQuestion");
        if ask {
            self.open_claude_question(event).await;
        } else if event.name == HookName::PermissionRequest {
            self.open_claude_permission(event, hold).await;
        }
```

The question path implements O2 (the `PreToolUse` opens the occurrence;
the matching `PermissionRequest` merges into it) and uses the validated
`QuestionSpec` from Task 7 for the request, the screen match (M3) and
the evidence labels, so all three describe the same question (Sol
question 2):

```rust
    async fn open_claude_question(&mut self, event: HookEvent) {
        let sid = event.payload.session_id.clone();
        let Some(agent_id) = self.sessions.get(&sid).and_then(|s| s.agent_id.clone()) else { return };
        let pane = self.bound_pane(&sid);
        let Some(key) = claude_key(&event.payload) else { return };
        // O2: a PermissionRequest for the question merges into the open
        // occurrence its PreToolUse opened; anything else is a new one (O1).
        let open_same = self
            .core
            .request_id_for(&agent_id, &key)
            .filter(|rid| self.tracks.get(rid).is_some_and(|t| t.kind == TrackKind::Question));
        if event.name == HookName::PermissionRequest && open_same.is_some() {
            return;
        }
        self.end_session_occurrences(&sid).await;
        let Some(obs) =
            mapping::claude_question(&agent_id, key.clone(), self.fallback_key(&agent_id), &event.payload, pane.is_some())
        else {
            return;
        };
        let answerable = obs.answer_path == AnswerPath::Keys;
        self.observe_locked(&agent_id, pane.is_some(), Observation::RequestSeen(obs)).await;
        let Some(rid) = self.core.request_id_for(&agent_id, &key) else { return };
        // D25: malformed input is read-only, with no matcher and no labels.
        let spec = mapping::question_specs(&event.payload.tool_input).ok().and_then(|v| v.into_iter().next());
        let labels = spec
            .as_ref()
            .map(|q| q.options.iter().enumerate().map(|(i, (l, _))| (format!("o{}", i + 1), l.clone())).collect())
            .unwrap_or_default();
        let now = Instant::now();
        self.next_op += 1;
        self.tracks.insert(rid.clone(), Track {
            agent_id,
            session_id: sid.clone(),
            key,
            op: self.next_op,
            read_seq: 0,
            expect: Expect::ClaudeQuestion {
                session_id: sid,
                question: spec.as_ref().map(|q| q.question.clone()).unwrap_or_default(),
                labels,
            },
            matcher: spec.filter(|_| answerable).map(|spec| DialogMatch::ClaudeQuestion { spec }),
            kind: TrackKind::Question,
            payload: event.payload,
            pane: pane.clone(),
            phase: Phase::ReadOnly,
            opened_at: now,
            capture: None,
            absent: 0,
            next_check: now + self.cfg.held_dialog_poll,
            hold_end_tries: 0,
        });
        if answerable && pane.is_some() {
            self.spawn_read(&rid, Purpose::Capture);
        }
        self.sweep();
    }

    /// A captured single question, inside the delivery lock the caller
    /// holds (G5): register its number keys bound to the pane incarnation,
    /// then mark it ready. `None` or any failure leaves it read-only.
    fn register_question_locked(&mut self, rid: &str, screen: Option<String>, incarnation: u64) {
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid) else { return };
        let Expect::ClaudeQuestion { labels, .. } = &t.expect else { return };
        let target = screen.and_then(|s| {
            KeysTarget::from_screen(rid.to_owned(), t.agent_id.clone(), t.key.clone(), &s, mapping::question_keys(labels.len()))
                .ok()
                .map(|k| k.for_incarnation(incarnation))
        });
        let open = self.core.request(rid).is_some_and(|r| r.status == RequestStatus::Open && r.answer_path == AnswerPath::Keys);
        match target {
            Some(target) if open && herdr.keys_targets().set(target).is_ok() => {
                self.core.observe(Observation::RequestReady { agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(), key: t.key.clone(), ready: true });
                t.phase = Phase::Keys;
                self.evidence.expect(rid, t.expect.clone());
            }
            _ => self.core.observe(Observation::RequestPath {
                agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(),
                key: t.key.clone(),
                answer_path: AnswerPath::None,
            }),
        }
    }
```

In `on_screen` (Task 15), replace the two `Purpose::Capture` arms with a
dispatch on the track kind (still inside the lock, after `still_valid`):

```rust
            (Purpose::Capture, result) => {
                let matched = match result {
                    ReadResult::Matched(s) => Some(s),
                    _ => None,
                };
                if matched.is_some()
                    && let Some(h) = self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").get_mut(&rid)
                {
                    h.bound_captured = true;
                }
                let Some((kind, phase)) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid).map(|t| {
                    t.capture = matched.clone();
                    (t.kind, t.phase)
                }) else {
                    return;
                };
                match (kind, matched) {
                    (TrackKind::Question, m) => self.register_question_locked(&rid, m, ticket.incarnation),
                    (TrackKind::Permission, Some(screen)) if phase == Phase::ReadOnly => {
                        self.switch_to_keys_locked(&rid, screen, ticket.incarnation); // over the hold cap
                    }
                    _ => {} // held permission keeps the capture; Codex: Task 17
                }
            }
```

### Task 17: Codex requests

Spec §6.3, Occurrence contract O3, Dialog match contract M2, Read ticket
contract (Sol B2) and ruling B4. A Codex `PermissionRequest` already got
its empty reply from the endpoint.

GATE 4: binding follows Task 10's `codex_bind`; a target is registered
only after the bound pane's own screen matches exactly (M2), no other
Codex pane shows the same command, and the binding read is still valid
inside the delivery lock. The tests encode gate 4: the env is ignored,
two Codex panes in one cwd stay read-only, and a session pane that never
shows the dialog (the misbinding gate 4 observed) opens nothing.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/state.rs`
- Test: `crates/acd-adapters/tests/hooks_codex.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 10, 12 to 16 (`codex_bind`, `DialogMatch`, tickets, tracks, hub);
  `mapping::codex_permission`, `CODEX_KEYS`.
- Produces: no new public names.
