```rust
mod common;
use common::*;

use std::time::Duration;

use acd_adapters::herdr::fake::fake_agent;
use acd_protocol::*;
use serde_json::{Value, json};

const CODEX_SID: &str = "01a116d3-e8a3-7ef0-9f57-79c5413ed009";

fn herdr_screen(name: &str) -> String {
    std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
}

/// The recorded payload, re-pointed at the command the recorded dialog shows.
fn permission(cwd: &str) -> Value {
    let mut p = fx("codex/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
    p["tool_input"]["command"] = "touch ~/acp0_test_t5_1.txt".into();
    p["cwd"] = cwd.into();
    p
}

fn codex_pane(id: &str, cwd: &str, session: Option<&str>) -> Value {
    let mut a = fake_agent(id, "codex", "working");
    a["cwd"] = cwd.into();
    a["foreground_cwd"] = cwd.into();
    if let Some(s) = session {
        a["agent_session"] = json!({"source": "herdr:codex", "agent": "codex", "kind": "id", "value": s});
    }
    a
}

async fn answer(r: &Rig, id: &str, rid: &str, option: &str) -> CommandResult {
    r.core.handle("c", ClientMessage::RequestAnswer {
        id: id.into(), request_id: rid.into(), revision: None, option_id: Some(option.into()), text: None,
    }).await
}

fn agent_of(r: &Rig, pane: &str) -> String {
    r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().iter().find(|(_, p)| p.pane_id == pane).unwrap().0.clone()
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_session_bound_codex_approval_is_answerable_within_a_second() {

    let r = rig(&[codex_pane("w1:p1", &cwd, Some(CODEX_SID))], |_| {}).await;
    r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &herdr_screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt"));
    let agent = agent_of(&r, "w1:p1");
    let t = tokio::time::Instant::now();
    assert_eq!(r.hook("codex", "PermissionRequest", &permission(&cwd), &[]).await, (200, String::new()));
    let rid = until("request", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    until("ready target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid)).await;
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(3), "spec §14 item 2");
    let req = r.core.request(&rid).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((req.answer_path, req.options.len()), (AnswerPath::Keys, 3));
    assert!(answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "deny").await.is_ok());
    assert_eq!(r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().keys_sent().len(), 1);
}
```

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_repeated_dialog_opens_a_new_occurrence_with_a_fresh_target() {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cwd = tmp.path().to_str().unwrap().to_owned();
    let r = rig(&[codex_pane("w1:p1", &cwd, Some(CODEX_SID))], |_| {}).await;
    r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &herdr_screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt"));
    let agent = agent_of(&r, "w1:p1");
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    r.hook("codex", "PermissionRequest", &permission(&cwd), &[]).await;
    let r1 = until("first", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    until("first target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&r1)).await;
    // Answered in the terminal and asked again before the watch saw it go.
    r.hook("codex", "PermissionRequest", &permission(&cwd), &[]).await;
    loop {
        if let ServerMessage::RequestResolved { request_id, by, .. } = rx.recv().await.unwrap()
            && request_id == r1
        {
            assert_eq!(by, ResolvedBy::Terminal);
            break;
        }
    }
    let r2 = until("second", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id).filter(|id| *id != r1)).await;
    until("second target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&r2)).await;
    assert!(answer(&r, "a1", &r2, "allow").await.is_ok());
}
```

```rust
    for p in ["w1:p1", "w1:p2"] {

        assert!(pending.is_none_or(|q| q.kind == RequestKind::BlockedUnparsed), "{p}: ruling B4");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Codex sessions get their own binding generation (G2). The `Reconciler`
gains `codex: HashMap<String, CodexSession>`:

```rust
struct CodexSession {
    /// Bumped by every Codex event of the session, every T3 ownership
    /// change and every revocation: a watch spawned before any of those
    /// is dropped (G5).
    bind_gen: u64,
    last_event: Instant,
}
```

Extend `bind_gen_of` (Task 15) to fall back to `self.codex`:

```rust
    fn bind_gen_of(&self, sid: &str) -> Option<u64> {
        self.sessions.get(sid).map(|s| s.bind_gen).or_else(|| self.codex.get(sid).map(|c| c.bind_gen))
    }
```

and, in `on_tick`, drop Codex sessions with no event for `idle_expiry`.

```rust
use super::owner::{CodexBind, codex_bind};
use super::wire::codex_key;

pub(crate) enum CodexSeen {
    /// The detection snapshot of the bound pane that matches (M2).
    Full(String),
    /// A collapsed Codex dialog (pane too short): it cannot be matched.
    Collapsed,
    /// No match on the bound pane, or another Codex pane shows the same
    /// command (ruling B4): open nothing.
    Nothing,
}

// `Internal` gains:
//     Codex { ticket: Ticket, event: HookEvent, pane: PaneFact, key: CorrelationKey, command: String, seen: CodexSeen },
```

```rust
    async fn on_codex(&mut self, event: HookEvent) {
        let sid = event.payload.session_id.clone();
        let now = Instant::now();
        let c = self.codex.entry(sid.clone()).or_insert(CodexSession { bind_gen: 0, last_event: now });
        c.bind_gen += 1; // any later event supersedes an earlier watch
        c.last_event = now;
        self.apply_evidence(&event);
        if event.name != HookName::PermissionRequest {
            return;
        }
        if self.t3.as_ref().is_some_and(|m| m.owner(&sid).is_some()) {
            return; // T3 shows it (ownership rule 1 holds for Codex sessions too)
        }
        if event.hints.herdr_pane.is_some() {
            tracing::debug!("Codex HERDR_* hints are not used for binding (gate 4)");
        }
        let Some(key) = codex_key(&event.payload) else { return };
        // M2/M4: only a command that renders on one row can ever match.
        let Some(command) = command_of(&event.payload.tool_input).filter(|c| single_row(c)).map(str::to_owned) else {
            self.end_session_occurrences(&sid).await; // O3 still applies
            return;
        };
        self.end_session_occurrences(&sid).await; // O3
        let panes = self.panes();
        let pane = match codex_bind(&sid, event.payload.cwd.as_deref(), &panes) {
            CodexBind::Session(p) | CodexBind::OnlyInCwd(p) => p,
            CodexBind::Ambiguous | CodexBind::Unbound => {
                tracing::debug!("Codex request not bound; the herdr fallback shows it read-only");
                return;
            }
        };
        let rivals: Vec<PaneFact> = panes
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|p| p.kind == acd_protocol::AgentKind::Codex && p.agent_id != pane.agent_id)
            .collect();
        let ticket = Ticket {
            agent_id: pane.agent_id.clone(),
            incarnation: pane.incarnation,
            session_id: sid.clone(),
            bind_gen: self.codex[&sid].bind_gen,
            request_id: None,
            op: 0,
            read_seq: 0,
        };
        self.spawn_codex_watch(ticket, event, pane, rivals, key, command);
    }

    /// Polls the bound pane for up to `codex_window` (spec §6.3: 1 s), then
    /// reads every other Codex pane once: if any of them also shows this
    /// command, the binding is ambiguous (ruling B4).
    fn spawn_codex_watch(
        &self, ticket: Ticket, event: HookEvent, pane: PaneFact, rivals: Vec<PaneFact>, key: CorrelationKey, command: String,
    ) {
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let Some(pref) = herdr.panes().read().expect("pane map").get(&pane.agent_id).cloned() else { return };
        let rival_refs: Vec<PaneRef> = {
            let map = herdr.panes();
            let map = map.read().expect("pane map");
            rivals.iter().filter_map(|r| map.get(&r.agent_id).cloned()).collect()
        };
        let reads = self.reads.clone();
        let tx = self.internal_tx.clone();
        let (window, poll) = (self.cfg.codex_window, self.cfg.capture_poll);
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let _permit = reads.acquire_owned().await.expect("never closed");
            let matcher = DialogMatch::CodexCommand { command: command.clone() };
            let read = |p: PaneRef| async move {
                p.herdr.read_screen(&p.pane_id, "detection", None, Instant::now() + CALL_TIMEOUT).await.ok().filter(|r| !r.truncated)
            };
            let deadline = Instant::now() + window;
            let mut collapsed = false;
            let mut seen = loop {
                if let Some(r) = read(pref.clone()).await {
                    if matcher.find(&r.text).is_some() {
                        break CodexSeen::Full(r.text);
                    }
                    collapsed |= DialogMatch::collapsed_codex(&r.text);
                }
                if Instant::now() >= deadline {
                    break if collapsed { CodexSeen::Collapsed } else { CodexSeen::Nothing };
                }
                tokio::time::sleep(poll).await;
            };
            if matches!(seen, CodexSeen::Full(_)) {
                for rival in rival_refs {
                    if read(rival).await.is_some_and(|r| matcher.find(&r.text).is_some()) {
                        seen = CodexSeen::Nothing; // two panes show this command
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
            let _ = tx.send(Internal::Codex { ticket, event, pane, key, command, seen });
        });
    }

    /// Inside the delivery lock (G5): the pane incarnation, the session's
    /// generation (no later Codex event) and T3 ownership must be
    /// unchanged, and `codex_bind` must still pick the same pane.
    async fn on_codex_seen(
        &mut self, ticket: Ticket, event: HookEvent, pane: PaneFact, key: CorrelationKey, command: String, seen: CodexSeen,
    ) {
        let collapsed = match &seen {
            CodexSeen::Nothing => return, // misbound, gone or ambiguous: open nothing
            CodexSeen::Collapsed => true,
            CodexSeen::Full(_) => false,
        };
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let locks = herdr.agent_locks();
        let _g = locks.lock(&ticket.agent_id).await;
        let still_bound = matches!(
            codex_bind(&ticket.session_id, event.payload.cwd.as_deref(), &self.panes()),
            CodexBind::Session(p) | CodexBind::OnlyInCwd(p) if p.agent_id == pane.agent_id
        );
        if !still_bound || !still_valid(&ticket, &self.now_for(&ticket), &[]) {
            tracing::debug!("dropping a stale Codex binding read");
            return;
        }
        let agent_id = pane.agent_id.clone();
        let obs = mapping::codex_permission(&agent_id, key.clone(), self.fallback_key(&agent_id), &event.payload, collapsed);
        self.core.observe(Observation::RequestSeen(obs));
        let Some(rid) = self.core.request_id_for(&agent_id, &key) else { return };
        let now = Instant::now();
        let sid = event.payload.session_id.clone();
        self.next_op += 1;
        let op = self.next_op;
        self.tracks.entry(rid.clone()).or_insert_with(|| Track {
            agent_id: agent_id.clone(),
            session_id: sid.clone(),
            key: key.clone(),
            op,
            read_seq: 0,
            expect: Expect::CodexTool {
                session_id: sid,
                tool_name: event.payload.tool_name.clone().unwrap_or_default(),
                command: command.clone(),
            },
            matcher: Some(DialogMatch::CodexCommand { command }),
            kind: TrackKind::Codex,
            payload: event.payload.clone(),
            pane: Some(pane),
            phase: Phase::ReadOnly,
            opened_at: now,
            capture: None,
            absent: 0,
            next_check: now + self.cfg.held_dialog_poll,
            hold_end_tries: 0,
        });
        if let CodexSeen::Full(screen) = seen {
            self.register_codex_locked(&rid, screen, ticket.incarnation);
        }
        self.sweep();
    }

    /// Inside the delivery lock the caller holds.
    fn register_codex_locked(&mut self, rid: &str, screen: String, incarnation: u64) {
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid) else { return };
        let keys = mapping::CODEX_KEYS.iter().map(|(o, k)| (o.to_string(), k.to_string())).collect();
        let target = KeysTarget::from_screen(rid.to_owned(), t.agent_id.clone(), t.key.clone(), &screen, keys)
            .map(|k| k.for_incarnation(incarnation));
        // Drop the "Enlarge the pane" note now that the command is visible.
        let obs = mapping::codex_permission(&t.agent_id, t.key.clone(), None, &t.payload, false);
        self.core.observe(Observation::RequestSeen(obs));
        let open = self.core.request(rid).is_some_and(|r| r.status == RequestStatus::Open && r.answer_path == AnswerPath::Keys);
        if let Ok(target) = target
            && open
            && herdr.keys_targets().set(target).is_ok()
        {
            self.core.observe(Observation::RequestReady { agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(), key: t.key.clone(), ready: true });
            t.phase = Phase::Keys;
            t.capture = Some(screen);
            self.evidence.expect(rid, t.expect.clone());
        }
    }
```

O3 replaces revision 1's `restart_occurrence`: every Codex
`PermissionRequest` first ends the session's open occurrence through
`end_session_occurrences` (Task 14), which retires its target inside the
lock, so a reappearing identical dialog gets a new request id and a fresh
target (D8 forbids replacing one).

Wire it up:

- `on_internal`: `Internal::Codex { ticket, event, pane, key, command, seen } => self.on_codex_seen(ticket, event, pane, key, command, seen).await`.
- `on_screen`'s capture dispatch (Task 16): `(TrackKind::Codex, Some(s)) => self.register_codex_locked(&rid, s, ticket.incarnation)`.
- `on_tick`: a Codex track in `Phase::ReadOnly` with no capture (a
  collapsed dialog) is re-read until its full dialog shows, at most once
  per `capture_window + held_dialog_poll`. Put this arm before the
  presence arm:

```rust
                (Phase::ReadOnly, None) if t.kind == TrackKind::Codex && t.capture.is_none() && now >= t.next_check => {
                    t.next_check = now + self.cfg.capture_window + self.cfg.held_dialog_poll;
                    captures.push(rid.clone());
                }
```

  with `let mut captures = Vec::new();` next to `reads`, and after the
  loop `for rid in captures { self.spawn_read(&rid, Purpose::Capture); }`.

A Codex request answered in the terminal ends through the herdr target
watch (dialog gone, `terminal`) once it has a target, and through
`apply_evidence` (`PostToolUse` or `Stop`, `terminal`) while collapsed.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```rust
    /// Ownership transitions: revoke every request of `sid`. Inside each
    /// track's delivery lock: retire the target, end the request
    /// (`uncertain` while answering, else `lost`), mark it gone, release
    /// the hold and drop the expectation. Never removes a herdr agent.
    /// Bumps the session's `bind_gen`, so every outstanding read fails G5.
    async fn revoke_session(&mut self, sid: &str) {
        if let Some(s) = self.sessions.get_mut(sid) {
            s.bind_gen += 1;
        }
        if let Some(c) = self.codex.get_mut(sid) {
            c.bind_gen += 1;
        }
        let ids: Vec<String> = self.tracks.iter().filter(|(_, t)| t.session_id == sid).map(|(k, _)| k.clone()).collect();
        for rid in ids {
            let agent = self.tracks.get(&rid).map(|t| t.agent_id.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
            let locks = self.herdr.as_ref().map(|h| h.agent_locks());
            let _g = match &locks {
                Some(l) => Some(l.lock(&agent).await),
                None => None,
            };
            if let Some(h) = &self.herdr {
                h.keys_targets().remove(&rid);
            }
            let by = match self.core.request(&rid).map(|r| r.status) {
                Some(RequestStatus::Answering) => ResolvedBy::Uncertain,
                _ => ResolvedBy::Lost,
            };
            self.end(&rid, by); // `end` also marks it gone and releases the hold
        }
    }
```

Rework `apply_owner` (Task 11) so it follows the table. Compute
`old = s.owner.clone()` first, and when `old != owner`:

```rust
        let revoke = match (&old, &owner) {
            (Owner::Herdr(a), Owner::Herdr(b)) => a != b,
            (Owner::Herdr(_), _) => true,                       // to T3, Unknown or Hooks
            (Owner::Hooks, Owner::T3(_) | Owner::Unknown) => true,
            _ => false,                                         // Hooks -> Herdr migrates instead
        };
        if revoke {
            self.revoke_session(sid).await;
        }
```

then the existing per-owner actions (migration for `Hooks` → `Herdr`,
`AgentGone(claude:<sid>)` for `Hooks` → `T3`/`Unknown`, upsert for
`→ Hooks`). Re-borrow `self.sessions` after the await.

Extend `reevaluate_all` to Codex sessions:

```rust
        let codex: Vec<String> = self.codex.keys().cloned().collect();
        for sid in codex {
            if self.t3.as_ref().is_some_and(|m| m.owner(&sid).is_some())
                && self.tracks.values().any(|t| t.session_id == sid)
            {
                self.revoke_session(&sid).await;
            }
        }
```

### Task 19: Secrets hardening and `init` repair

P1 follow-ups: `check_mode` must not follow symlinks and must check the
owner; `write_secret` must clean up its temp file on failure and fsync
the directory after the rename; `init` must check the mode of files it
keeps and reject an empty `machine-id`.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd-securefs/src/lib.rs` (`check_private_file`)
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/secrets.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/identity.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/main.rs` (`init`)
- Test: `crates/acd/src/secrets.rs` unit tests, `crates/acd/tests/cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `acd_securefs::check_private_file(path: &Path, max_mode: u32) -> Result<(), SecureFsError>`.
  - `secrets::check_mode(path, max_mode)`: same signature, now no-follow
    and owner-checked; the "Fix with: chmod" hint is kept for mode errors.
  - `secrets::read_secret(path)`: reads through `read_private` (0600, 4 KiB).
  - `secrets::write_secret(path, value)`: unique temp name ending in
    `.tmp`, removed on any failure, parent directory fsynced.
  - `secrets::repair_mode(path: &Path, mode: u32) -> Result<bool>`: for
    `init`; refuses symlinks and foreign owners, tightens a loose mode and
    returns whether it changed.
  - `identity::machine_id(dir)` errors on an empty id.

`secrets.rs` tests:

```rust
    #[test]
    fn check_mode_refuses_a_symlink_even_to_a_private_file() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let real = dir.path().join("real");
        write_secret(&real, "x").unwrap();
        let link = dir.path().join("link");
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&real, &link).unwrap();
        let err = check_mode(&link, 0o600).unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(err.contains("symlink"), "{err}");
        assert!(read_secret(&link).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn write_secret_leaves_no_temp_file_on_failure() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let target = dir.path().join("occupied");
        std::fs::create_dir(&target).unwrap(); // rename onto a directory fails
        assert!(write_secret(&target, "x").is_err());
        let leftovers: Vec<_> = std::fs::read_dir(dir.path()).unwrap()
            .filter_map(|e| e.ok()).filter(|e| e.file_name().to_string_lossy().ends_with(".tmp")).collect();
        assert!(leftovers.is_empty(), "{leftovers:?}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn repair_mode_tightens_but_refuses_symlinks() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let p = dir.path().join("t");
        write_secret(&p, "x").unwrap();
        fs::set_permissions(&p, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o644)).unwrap();
        assert!(repair_mode(&p, 0o600).unwrap());
        assert_eq!(fs::metadata(&p).unwrap().permissions().mode() & 0o777, 0o600);
        assert!(!repair_mode(&p, 0o600).unwrap(), "already private");
        let link = dir.path().join("l");
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&p, &link).unwrap();
        assert!(repair_mode(&link, 0o600).is_err());
    }
```

```rust
#[test]
fn init_repairs_loose_modes_of_files_it_keeps() {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let dir = common::init_dir(tmp.path());
    let before = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("client.token")).unwrap();
    for f in ["client.token", "hook.token", "machine-id", "config.toml"] {
        std::fs::set_permissions(dir.join(f), std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o644)).unwrap();
    }
    let out = acd(&dir).arg("init").output().unwrap();
    assert!(out.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
    for f in ["client.token", "hook.token", "machine-id", "config.toml"] {
        assert_eq!(mode(dir.join(f)), 0o600, "{f}");
    }
    assert_eq!(before, std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("client.token")).unwrap(), "kept, not replaced");
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).contains("repaired"));
}

#[test]
fn init_and_run_refuse_a_symlinked_token() {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let dir = common::init_dir(tmp.path());
    let elsewhere = tmp.path().join("elsewhere");
    std::fs::rename(dir.join("hook.token"), &elsewhere).unwrap();
    std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&elsewhere, dir.join("hook.token")).unwrap();
    let out = acd(&dir).arg("init").output().unwrap();
    assert!(!out.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("symlink"));
    let (status, err) = run_expecting_exit(&dir);
    assert!(!status.success() && err.contains("symlink"), "{err}");
}

#[test]
fn an_empty_machine_id_is_refused_by_run_and_regenerated_by_init() {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let dir = common::init_dir(tmp.path());
    std::fs::write(dir.join("machine-id"), "\n").unwrap();
    let (status, err) = run_expecting_exit(&dir);
    assert!(!status.success() && err.contains("machine-id is empty"), "{err}");
    assert!(acd(&dir).arg("init").status().unwrap().success());
    assert!(!std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("machine-id")).unwrap().trim().is_empty());
}
```

```rust
/// No-follow open, regular file, owned by the euid, mode within `max_mode`.
pub fn check_private_file(path: &Path, max_mode: u32) -> Result<(), SecureFsError> {
    let file = open_path(path, false)?;
    verify(&file, path, false, max_mode)
}
```

`secrets.rs`:

```rust
use acd_securefs::SecureFsError;

fn explain(e: SecureFsError) -> anyhow::Error {
    match e {
        SecureFsError::Mode { path, mode, want } => anyhow::anyhow!(
            "{} has mode {:o}, which lets other users read it. Fix with: chmod {:o} {}",
            path.display(), mode & 0o777, want, path.display()
        ),
        SecureFsError::Symlink(p) => anyhow::anyhow!("{} is a symlink; replace it with a regular file", p.display()),
        SecureFsError::Owner(p) => anyhow::anyhow!("{} is not owned by you", p.display()),
        other => anyhow::anyhow!("{other}"),
    }
}

/// Fails if `path` is a symlink, is not owned by the effective user, or
/// grants any permission bit outside `max_mode`.
pub fn check_mode(path: &Path, max_mode: u32) -> Result<()> {
    let meta = fs::symlink_metadata(path).with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display()))?;
    let r = if meta.is_dir() {
        acd_securefs::check_private_dir(path, max_mode)
    } else {
        acd_securefs::check_private_file(path, max_mode)
    };
    r.map_err(explain)
}

pub fn read_secret(path: &Path) -> Result<String> {
    let bytes = acd_securefs::read_private(path, 0o600, 4096).map_err(explain)?;
    Ok(String::from_utf8(bytes).context("secret is not UTF-8")?.trim().to_string())
}

/// Writes `value` atomically with mode 0600: a unique temp file in the
/// same directory, fsync, rename, fsync of the directory. The temp file is
/// removed on any failure.
pub fn write_secret(path: &Path, value: &str) -> Result<()> {
    let dir = path.parent().context("secret path has no parent")?;
    let mut rnd = [0u8; 8];
    getrandom::fill(&mut rnd).expect("OS randomness is available");
    let name = path.file_name().context("secret path has no file name")?.to_string_lossy();
    let tmp = dir.join(format!(
        "{name}.{}.{}.tmp",
        std::process::id(),
        rnd.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect::<String>()
    ));
    let result = (|| -> Result<()> {
        let mut f = OpenOptions::new().write(true).create_new(true).mode(0o600).open(&tmp)
            .with_context(|| format!("creating {}", tmp.display()))?;
        f.write_all(value.as_bytes())?;
        f.write_all(b"\n")?;
        f.sync_all()?;
        fs::rename(&tmp, path).with_context(|| format!("replacing {}", path.display()))?;
        fs::File::open(dir)?.sync_all()?;
        Ok(())
    })();
    if result.is_err() {
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp);
    }
    result
}

/// For `acd init`: tightens a loose mode on a regular file the user owns.
/// Refuses symlinks and foreign owners. Returns whether it changed.
pub fn repair_mode(path: &Path, mode: u32) -> Result<bool> {
    use std::os::unix::fs::MetadataExt;
    let meta = fs::symlink_metadata(path).with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display()))?;
    if meta.file_type().is_symlink() {
        bail!("{} is a symlink; replace it with a regular file", path.display());
    }
    if !meta.is_file() {
        bail!("{} is not a regular file", path.display());
    }
    if meta.uid() != acd_securefs::euid() {
        bail!("{} is not owned by you", path.display());
    }
    if meta.mode() & 0o777 & !mode == 0 {
        return Ok(false);
    }
    fs::set_permissions(path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(mode))?;
    Ok(true)
}
```

written atomically is the transaction). This task creates the store,

store-only step; Tasks 24 and 25 add retention and the header rewrite.

- Create: `crates/acd/src/hook_token.rs`

```rust
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn dir() -> tempfile::TempDir {
        let d = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        crate::secrets::ensure_private_dir(d.path()).unwrap();
        d

    #[test]
    fn ensure_creates_generation_one_without_a_previous_token() {
        let d = dir();
        assert!(ensure(d.path(), false).unwrap());
        let s = load(d.path()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((s.generation, s.previous.is_none(), s.headers.state), (1, true, HeaderState::Done));
        crate::secrets::parse_token(&s.current).unwrap();
        assert!(!ensure(d.path(), false).unwrap(), "idempotent");
        assert_eq!(load(d.path()).unwrap(), s);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_legacy_hook_token_is_migrated_and_removed() {
        let d = dir();
        let legacy = crate::secrets::new_token();
        crate::secrets::write_secret(&d.path().join(LEGACY_FILE), &legacy).unwrap();
        let s = load(d.path()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(s.current, legacy);
        assert!(!d.path().join(LEGACY_FILE).exists());
        assert!(d.path().join(STORE_FILE).exists());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rotation_keeps_the_previous_token_for_at_most_30_days() {
        let d = dir();
        ensure(d.path(), false).unwrap();
        let before = load(d.path()).unwrap();
        let after = rotate_store(d.path(), 1_000).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(after.generation, before.generation + 1);
        let prev = after.previous.clone().unwrap();
        assert_eq!((prev.token, prev.generation), (before.current, before.generation));
        assert_eq!(prev.expires_at_ms, 1_000 + MAX_RETENTION_MS);
        assert_eq!(after.headers.state, HeaderState::Pending);
        assert_eq!(load(d.path()).unwrap(), after, "committed");
    }

        let e = format!("{:#}", load(d.path()).unwrap_err());
        assert!(e.contains(STORE_FILE), "{e}");
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the store**

```rust
//! The hook token store (contract T1 to T5): `hook-tokens.json`, written
//! atomically at 0600, is the single source of truth and the rotation
//! transaction. Tasks 24 and 25 add retention and header rewrites.
use std::path::Path;

use anyhow::{Context, Result, bail};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::secrets;

pub const STORE_FILE: &str = "hook-tokens.json";
pub const LEGACY_FILE: &str = "hook.token";
pub const MAX_RETENTION_MS: i64 = 30 * 86_400_000;

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Previous {
    pub token: String,
    pub generation: u64,
    pub rotated_at_ms: i64,
    pub expires_at_ms: i64,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum HeaderState {
    Pending,
    Done,
    Failed,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Headers {
    /// The generation the managed Claude headers carry once `state` is `done`.
    pub generation: u64,
    pub state: HeaderState,
    /// When the headers last became `done` (T4 counts sessions older than this).
    pub updated_at_ms: i64,
    pub failed: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Store {
    pub version: u32,
    pub generation: u64,
    pub current: String,
    pub previous: Option<Previous>,
    pub headers: Headers,
}

/// Debug-only failure injection at a named stage (`ACD_TEST_FAIL_AT`).
pub fn fail_at(stage: &str) -> Result<()> {
    #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    if std::env::var("ACD_TEST_FAIL_AT").is_ok_and(|s| s == stage) {
        bail!("injected failure at {stage}");
    }
    let _ = stage;
    Ok(())
}

fn now_ms() -> i64 {
    chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis()
}

pub fn save(dir: &Path, s: &Store) -> Result<()> {
    secrets::parse_token(&s.current).context("refusing to save an invalid hook token")?;
    fail_at("store-write")?;
    secrets::write_secret(&dir.join(STORE_FILE), &serde_json::to_string_pretty(s)?)
}

fn fresh(token: String, generation: u64) -> Store {
    Store {
        version: 1,
        generation,
        current: token,
        previous: None,
        headers: Headers { generation, state: HeaderState::Done, updated_at_ms: now_ms(), failed: vec![] },
    }
}

pub fn load(dir: &Path) -> Result<Store> {
    let path = dir.join(STORE_FILE);
    if path.symlink_metadata().is_err() {
        let legacy = dir.join(LEGACY_FILE);
        if legacy.symlink_metadata().is_ok() {
            let token = secrets::parse_token(&secrets::read_secret(&legacy)?)
                .context("hook.token is invalid; run `acd init --force`")?;
            let s = fresh(token, 1);
            save(dir, &s)?;
            std::fs::remove_file(&legacy).with_context(|| format!("removing {}", legacy.display()))?;
            return Ok(s);
        }
        bail!("{} is missing; run `acd init`", path.display());
    }
    let s: Store = serde_json::from_str(&secrets::read_secret(&path)?)
        .with_context(|| format!("{} is corrupt; run `acd init --force`", path.display()))?;
    secrets::parse_token(&s.current).with_context(|| format!("{} holds an invalid token", path.display()))?;
    Ok(s)
}

/// For `acd init`. `force` replaces the store with a fresh generation.
pub fn ensure(dir: &Path, force: bool) -> Result<bool> {
    let exists = dir.join(STORE_FILE).symlink_metadata().is_ok() || dir.join(LEGACY_FILE).symlink_metadata().is_ok();
    if exists && !force {
        load(dir)?; // migrates a legacy file
        return Ok(false);
    }
    let generation = if exists { load(dir).map(|s| s.generation + 1).unwrap_or(1) } else { 1 };
    save(dir, &fresh(secrets::new_token(), generation))?;
    Ok(true)
}

/// T3 step 1: commit the new generation; the old token stays accepted
/// until retired (Task 24), never beyond `MAX_RETENTION_MS`. Task 25's
/// `rotate` calls it under the writer lock after its prechecks.
pub fn rotate_store(dir: &Path, now_ms: i64) -> Result<Store> {
    let old = load(dir)?;
    let generation = old.generation + 1;
    let s = Store {
        version: 1,
        generation,
        current: secrets::new_token(),
        previous: Some(Previous {
            token: old.current,
            generation: old.generation,
            rotated_at_ms: now_ms,
            expires_at_ms: now_ms + MAX_RETENTION_MS,
        }),
        headers: Headers { generation, state: HeaderState::Pending, updated_at_ms: old.headers.updated_at_ms, failed: vec![] },
    };
    save(dir, &s)?;
    Ok(s)
}
```

- `init`: drop `hook.token` from the token loop and from the repair

```rust
use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::net::TcpListener;
use std::process::Stdio;
use std::sync::mpsc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// A one-shot HTTP server: records the request, replies with `status` and
/// `body`, or never replies when `stall`.
fn one_shot(status: u16, body: &'static str, stall: bool) -> (u16, mpsc::Receiver<String>) {
    let l = TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap();
    let port = l.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel();
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let Ok((mut s, _)) = l.accept() else { return };
        s.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(2))).unwrap();
        let mut req = Vec::new();
        let mut buf = [0u8; 65536];
        loop {
            match s.read(&mut buf) {
                Ok(0) | Err(_) => break,
                Ok(n) => {
                    req.extend_from_slice(&buf[..n]);
                    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&req);
                    if let Some((head, rest)) = text.split_once("\r\n\r\n") {
                        let len = head.lines().find_map(|l| l.to_ascii_lowercase().strip_prefix("content-length: ").map(|v| v.trim().parse::<usize>().unwrap())).unwrap_or(0);
                        if rest.len() >= len { break; }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        let _ = tx.send(String::from_utf8_lossy(&req).into_owned());
        if stall {
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(5));
            return;
        }
        let _ = write!(s, "HTTP/1.1 {status} X\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len());
    });
    (port, rx)
}

fn shim_dir(port: u16) -> (tempfile::TempDir, std::path::PathBuf) {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let dir = init_dir(tmp.path());
    std::fs::write(dir.join("config.toml"), format!("hook_port = {port}\n")).unwrap();
    (tmp, dir)
}

fn run_shim(dir: &std::path::Path, event: &str, stdin: &[u8], env: &[(&str, &str)]) -> (std::process::Output, Duration) {
    let mut cmd = acd(dir);
    for (k, v) in env { cmd.env(k, v); }
    let mut child = cmd.args(["hook", "codex", event, "acd-managed:v1"])
        .stdin(Stdio::piped()).stdout(Stdio::piped()).stderr(Stdio::piped()).spawn().unwrap();
    let t = Instant::now();
    child.stdin.take().unwrap().write_all(stdin).ok();
    let out = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
    (out, t.elapsed())
}

fn codex_fixture(name: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
    std::fs::read(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/hooks/codex/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_forwards_the_hook_and_prints_the_reply() {
    let (port, rx) = one_shot(200, "{\"ok\":1}", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let token = hook_token_of(&dir);
    let body = codex_fixture("payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
    let (out, _) = run_shim(&dir, "PermissionRequest", &body, &[("HERDR_PANE_ID", "w1:p2"), ("HERDR_SOCKET_PATH", "")]);
    assert!(out.status.success());
    assert_eq!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout), "{\"ok\":1}");
    let req = rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(2)).unwrap();
    assert!(req.starts_with("POST /hook/codex/PermissionRequest HTTP/1.1\r\n"), "{req}");
    assert!(req.contains(&format!("X-Acd-Token: {token}\r\n")));
    assert!(req.contains("X-Herdr-Pane: w1:p2\r\n"));
    assert!(!req.contains("X-Herdr-Socket"), "empty env values are not sent");
    assert!(req.ends_with(std::str::from_utf8(&body).unwrap()));
    assert!(!String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).contains(&token) && !String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains(&token));
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_fails_open_when_acd_is_down() {
    let port = free_port(); // nothing listens
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let (out, took) = run_shim(&dir, "PermissionRequest", &codex_fixture("payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success());
    assert!(out.stdout.is_empty() && out.stderr.is_empty());
    assert!(took < Duration::from_secs(1), "{took:?}");
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_fails_open_when_acd_stalls() {
    let (port, _rx) = one_shot(200, "", true);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let (out, took) = run_shim(&dir, "PermissionRequest", &codex_fixture("payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success() && out.stdout.is_empty());
    assert!(took < Duration::from_millis(1200), "inside Codex's 1 s budget plus process start: {took:?}");
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_prints_nothing_for_a_non_2xx_reply() {
    let (port, _rx) = one_shot(401, "{\"no\":1}", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let (out, _) = run_shim(&dir, "Stop", b"{\"session_id\":\"s\"}", &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success() && out.stdout.is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_refuses_oversized_input_without_sending() {
    let (port, rx) = one_shot(200, "x", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let big = vec![b'a'; (1 << 20) + 1];
    let (out, _) = run_shim(&dir, "Stop", &big, &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success() && out.stdout.is_empty());
    assert!(rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_millis(300)).is_err(), "nothing was sent");
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_without_a_token_prints_nothing() {
    let (port, rx) = one_shot(200, "x", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    std::fs::remove_file(dir.join("hook-tokens.json")).unwrap();
    let (out, _) = run_shim(&dir, "Stop", b"{}", &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success() && out.stdout.is_empty() && out.stderr.is_empty());
    assert!(rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_millis(300)).is_err());
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_honours_an_explicit_config_dir() {
    let (port, rx) = one_shot(200, "ok", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let other_home = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let mut cmd = acd_home(other_home.path(), other_home.path());
    cmd.env_remove("ACD_CONFIG_DIR");
    let out = cmd.args(["hook", "codex", "Stop", "--config-dir"]).arg(&dir).arg("acd-managed:v1")
        .stdin(Stdio::null()).output().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout), "ok");
    assert!(rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(1)).is_ok());
}
```

`hook_shim.rs`:

```rust
//! `acd hook codex <event>` (spec §6.1): Codex's command hook. Reads the
//! payload on stdin, forwards it to the local hook endpoint, prints the
//! reply body and exits 0. Any failure prints nothing and still exits 0, so
//! Codex shows its own dialog. It never logs and never prints the token.
use std::ffi::OsString;
use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::net::{SocketAddr, TcpStream};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

pub const MAX_BODY: usize = 1 << 20;
const CONNECT_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_millis(500);

pub fn main(args: &[OsString]) -> i32 {
    if let Some(body) = run(args) {
        let mut out = std::io::stdout().lock();
        let _ = out.write_all(body.as_bytes());
        let _ = out.flush();
    }
    0
}

/// Inside Codex's own timeouts (§6.3: 1 s for PermissionRequest, 2 s else).
fn budget(event: &str) -> Duration {
    if event == "PermissionRequest" { Duration::from_millis(800) } else { Duration::from_millis(1500) }
}

fn header_safe(v: &str) -> bool {
    !v.is_empty() && v.len() <= 1024 && !v.chars().any(char::is_control)
}

fn run(args: &[OsString]) -> Option<String> {
    let mut it = args.iter().map(|a| a.to_str());
    let agent = it.next()??;
    let event = it.next()??;
    if agent != "codex" || event.is_empty() || event.len() > 64 || !event.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric()) {
        return None;
    }
    let mut config_dir: Option<PathBuf> = None;
    while let Some(a) = it.next() {
        match a? {
            "--config-dir" => config_dir = Some(PathBuf::from(it.next()??)),
            "acd-managed:v1" => {}
            _ => return None,
        }
    }
    let deadline = Instant::now() + budget(event);
    let mut body = Vec::new();
    std::io::stdin().lock().take(MAX_BODY as u64 + 1).read_to_end(&mut body).ok()?;
    if body.len() > MAX_BODY {
        return None;
    }
    let dir = match config_dir {
        Some(d) => d,
        None => crate::paths::config_dir().ok()?,
    };
    let token = crate::hook_token::load(&dir).ok()?.current;
    let port = crate::config::Config::load(&dir).ok()?.hook_port;
    let mut headers = vec![("X-Acd-Token".to_string(), token)];
    // Hints only (D17): acd never binds Codex with them.
    for (env, header) in [("HERDR_PANE_ID", "X-Herdr-Pane"), ("HERDR_SOCKET_PATH", "X-Herdr-Socket")] {
        if let Ok(v) = std::env::var(env)
            && header_safe(&v)
        {
            headers.push((header.to_string(), v));
        }
    }
    post(port, &format!("/hook/codex/{event}"), &headers, &body, deadline)
}

fn post(port: u16, path: &str, headers: &[(String, String)], body: &[u8], deadline: Instant) -> Option<String> {
    let addr = SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], port));
    let mut s = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&addr, CONNECT_TIMEOUT).ok()?;
    let left = deadline.checked_duration_since(Instant::now())?;
    s.set_write_timeout(Some(left)).ok()?;
    let mut req = format!(
        "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n",
        body.len()
    );
    for (k, v) in headers {
        req.push_str(&format!("{k}: {v}\r\n"));
    }
    req.push_str("\r\n");
    s.write_all(req.as_bytes()).ok()?;
    s.write_all(body).ok()?;
    let mut resp = Vec::new();
    let mut buf = [0u8; 8192];
    loop {
        let left = deadline.checked_duration_since(Instant::now())?;
        s.set_read_timeout(Some(left)).ok()?;
        match s.read(&mut buf) {
            Ok(0) => break,
            Ok(n) => {
                resp.extend_from_slice(&buf[..n]);
                if resp.len() > MAX_BODY + 8192 {
                    return None;
                }
            }
            Err(_) => return None,
        }
    }
    parse_response(&resp)
}

/// The body of a 2xx reply with a plain (non-chunked) body.
fn parse_response(resp: &[u8]) -> Option<String> {
    let text = std::str::from_utf8(resp).ok()?;
    let (head, body) = text.split_once("\r\n\r\n")?;
    let status: u16 = head.split_whitespace().nth(1)?.parse().ok()?;
    if !(200..300).contains(&status) {
        return None;
    }
    if head.lines().any(|l| l.to_ascii_lowercase().starts_with("transfer-encoding:")) {
        return None;
    }
    Some(body.to_owned())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn only_plain_2xx_bodies_are_printed() {
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: 2\r\n\r\n{}").as_deref(), Some("{}"));
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\ncontent-length: 0\r\n\r\n").as_deref(), Some(""));
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized\r\n\r\nno"), None);
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nTransfer-Encoding: chunked\r\n\r\n2\r\n{}\r\n0\r\n\r\n"), None);
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"garbage"), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn unexpected_arguments_send_nothing() {
        let a = |v: &[&str]| v.iter().map(OsString::from).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        assert!(run(&a(&["claude", "Stop"])).is_none());
        assert!(run(&a(&["codex", "Stop;rm"])).is_none());
        assert!(run(&a(&["codex", "Stop", "--surprise"])).is_none());
    }
}
```

### Task 22: Settings editing primitives

The building blocks of the installer: order-preserving JSON edits (D16),
the owned entries with exact ownership (D22), raw-preserving group edits
(D16), and atomic writes with a chosen mode. GATE 2: the Claude entries
carry `$VAR` header values (2a: interpolated when listed in
`allowedEnvVars`; unlisted ones become `""`) and omit `SessionStart`
(Claude never sends it over http).

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd/Cargo.toml` (`serde_json` with `features = ["raw_value"]`), `Cargo.lock`
- Create: `crates/acd/src/hooks_install/mod.rs` (`MARKER`, `Target`, `Ctx`)
- Create: `crates/acd/src/hooks_install/json.rs`
- Create: `crates/acd/src/hooks_install/entries.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/secrets.rs` (`write_atomic`)
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/config.rs` (`[claude] permission_hold`)
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/main.rs` (`mod hooks_install;` only)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 19 (`write_secret`), Task 21 (`hook_port`, the shim
  command line).
- Produces:
  - `config::ClaudeSection { permission_hold: u64 }` (seconds, default
    3600) as `Config::claude`.
  - `secrets::write_atomic(path: &Path, bytes: &[u8], mode: u32) -> Result<()>`
    (`write_secret` calls it with `value + "\n"` and 0600).
  - `hooks_install::MARKER = "acd-managed:v1"`, `hooks_install::Target { Claude, Codex }` (clap `ValueEnum`),
    `hooks_install::Ctx { home, claude_dir, codex_dir, config_dir, exe, hook_port, permission_hold }`,
    `Ctx::from_env(config_dir: &Path) -> Result<Ctx>`, `Ctx::settings_path(Target) -> PathBuf`.
  - `json::Obj` (`parse`, `get`, `set`, `remove`, `is_empty`, `render`), `json::raw`, `json::raw_text`.
  - `entries::claude_events`, `CODEX_EVENTS`, `claude_group`, `codex_group`, `sh_quote`,
    `sh_words`, `entries::Ownership { Owned, Suspicious, Foreign }`, `entries::classify(event, entry) -> Ownership`.
  - `json::GroupEdit { Keep, Drop, Rewrite(Box<RawValue>) }`, `json::edit_group`, `json::render_array`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the JSON layer**

`crates/acd/Cargo.toml`: `serde_json = { workspace = true, features = ["raw_value"] }`.

`hooks_install/json.rs`:

```rust
//! Order-preserving edits of a JSON object (D16): only the keys acd edits
//! are re-serialized; every other value keeps its original bytes.
use serde::de::{Deserializer, MapAccess, Visitor};
use serde_json::value::RawValue;

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct Obj(pub Vec<(String, Box<RawValue>)>);

impl<'de> serde::Deserialize<'de> for Obj {
    fn deserialize<D: Deserializer<'de>>(d: D) -> Result<Obj, D::Error> {
        struct V;
        impl<'de> Visitor<'de> for V {
            type Value = Obj;
            fn expecting(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter) -> std::fmt::Result {
                f.write_str("a JSON object")
            }
            fn visit_map<A: MapAccess<'de>>(self, mut m: A) -> Result<Obj, A::Error> {
                let mut out = Vec::new();
                while let Some((k, v)) = m.next_entry::<String, Box<RawValue>>()? {
                    out.push((k, v));
                }
                Ok(Obj(out))
            }
        }
        d.deserialize_map(V)
    }
}

impl Obj {
    pub fn parse(text: &str) -> anyhow::Result<Obj> {
        if text.trim().is_empty() {
            return Ok(Obj::default());
        }
        Ok(serde_json::from_str(text)?)
    }

    pub fn get(&self, k: &str) -> Option<&RawValue> {
        self.0.iter().find(|(key, _)| key == k).map(|(_, v)| v.as_ref())
    }

    pub fn set(&mut self, k: &str, v: Box<RawValue>) {
        match self.0.iter_mut().find(|(key, _)| key == k) {
            Some(e) => e.1 = v,
            None => self.0.push((k.to_owned(), v)),
        }
    }

    pub fn remove(&mut self, k: &str) {
        self.0.retain(|(key, _)| key != k)
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.0.is_empty()
    }

    /// Two-space indent at this level; nested values keep their bytes.
    pub fn render(&self) -> String {
        if self.0.is_empty() {
            return "{}".into();
        }
        let mut s = String::from("{\n");
        for (i, (k, v)) in self.0.iter().enumerate() {
            s.push_str("  ");
            s.push_str(&serde_json::to_string(k).expect("a string serializes"));
            s.push_str(": ");
            s.push_str(v.get());
            s.push_str(if i + 1 < self.0.len() { ",\n" } else { "\n" });
        }
        s.push('}');
        s
    }
}

pub fn raw(v: &serde_json::Value) -> Box<RawValue> {
    RawValue::from_string(serde_json::to_string_pretty(v).expect("json")).expect("valid json")
}

pub fn raw_text(s: String) -> anyhow::Result<Box<RawValue>> {
    Ok(RawValue::from_string(s)?)
}
```

`hooks_install/entries.rs`:

```rust
//! The hook entries acd owns (spec §6.2, §6.3 tables). Every entry carries
//! `acd-managed:v1`; ownership is matched by that marker only.
use serde_json::{Value, json};

use super::{Ctx, MARKER};

/// Claude: `(event, matcher, timeout seconds)`. No SessionStart: Claude
/// never sends it over http (gate 2 draft).
pub fn claude_events(permission_hold: u64) -> Vec<(&'static str, Option<&'static str>, u64)> {
    vec![
        ("UserPromptSubmit", None, 1),
        ("PermissionRequest", None, permission_hold + 10),
        ("PreToolUse", Some("AskUserQuestion"), 1),
        ("PostToolUse", None, 1),
        ("Stop", None, 1),
        ("SessionEnd", None, 1),
    ]
}

pub const CODEX_EVENTS: [(&str, u64); 5] =
    [("SessionStart", 2), ("UserPromptSubmit", 2), ("PermissionRequest", 1), ("PostToolUse", 2), ("Stop", 2)];

pub fn claude_group(ctx: &Ctx, token: &str, event: &str, matcher: Option<&str>, timeout: u64) -> Value {
    let entry = json!({
        "type": "http",
        "url": format!("http://127.0.0.1:{}/hook/claude/{event}?marker={MARKER}", ctx.hook_port),
        "timeout": timeout,
        "headers": {
            "X-Acd-Token": token,
            "X-Herdr-Pane": "$HERDR_PANE_ID",
            "X-Herdr-Socket": "$HERDR_SOCKET_PATH",
            "X-Claude-Entrypoint": "$CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT"
        },
        "allowedEnvVars": ["HERDR_PANE_ID", "HERDR_SOCKET_PATH", "CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT"]
    });
    match matcher {
        Some(m) => json!({"matcher": m, "hooks": [entry]}),
        None => json!({"hooks": [entry]}),
    }
}

/// POSIX single-quote quoting.
pub fn sh_quote(s: &str) -> String {
    format!("'{}'", s.replace('\'', "'\\''"))
}

pub fn codex_group(ctx: &Ctx, event: &str, timeout: u64) -> Value {
    let command = format!(
        "{} hook codex {event} --config-dir {} {MARKER}",
        sh_quote(&ctx.exe.to_string_lossy()),
        sh_quote(&ctx.config_dir.to_string_lossy())
    );
    json!({"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": command, "timeout": timeout}]})
}

// Ownership (`classify`) is added in Step 6.
```

`hooks_install/mod.rs`:

```rust
//! `acd hooks install|uninstall claude|codex` (spec §6.1 Installation).
// Task 23 uses everything here; until then nothing calls it.
#![allow(dead_code)]
mod entries;
mod json;

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use anyhow::{Context, Result};

pub const MARKER: &str = "acd-managed:v1";

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, clap::ValueEnum)]
pub enum Target {
    Claude,
    Codex,
}

pub struct Ctx {
    pub home: PathBuf,
    pub claude_dir: PathBuf,
    pub codex_dir: PathBuf,
    pub config_dir: PathBuf,
    pub exe: PathBuf,
    pub hook_port: u16,
    pub permission_hold: u64,
}

impl Ctx {
    pub fn from_env(config_dir: &Path) -> Result<Ctx> {
        let home = PathBuf::from(std::env::var_os("HOME").context("HOME is not set")?);
        let cfg = crate::config::Config::load(config_dir)?;
        let env_dir = |k: &str| std::env::var_os(k).filter(|v| !v.is_empty()).map(PathBuf::from);
        Ok(Ctx {
            claude_dir: env_dir("CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR").unwrap_or_else(|| home.join(".claude")),
            codex_dir: env_dir("CODEX_HOME").unwrap_or_else(|| home.join(".codex")),
            home,
            config_dir: std::fs::canonicalize(config_dir)?,
            exe: std::fs::canonicalize(std::env::current_exe()?)?,
            hook_port: cfg.hook_port,
            permission_hold: cfg.claude.permission_hold,
        })
    }

    pub fn settings_path(&self, t: Target) -> PathBuf {
        match t {
            Target::Claude => self.claude_dir.join("settings.json"),
            Target::Codex => self.codex_dir.join("hooks.json"),
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Exact ownership and raw group edits (D16, D22; Sol I5)**

Revision 1 called any entry whose URL or command contained the marker
"owned", so a foreign hook that merely mentioned it would be removed or
get the token. Ownership is now exact. Add to `entries.rs`:

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Ownership {
    /// Exactly acd's entry for this event.
    Owned,
    /// Mentions the marker but is not exactly acd's entry: never edited,
    /// never given a token; install and uninstall warn, rotation refuses.
    Suspicious,
    Foreign,
}

/// Splits a command into words: bare words without shell metacharacters
/// and single-quoted words (`'\''` escapes). Anything else is `None`.
pub fn sh_words(cmd: &str) -> Option<Vec<String>> {
    let mut words = Vec::new();
    let mut cur = String::new();
    let mut in_word = false;
    let mut chars = cmd.chars().peekable();
    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
        match c {
            ' ' => {
                if in_word {
                    words.push(std::mem::take(&mut cur));
                    in_word = false;
                }
            }
            '\'' => {
                in_word = true;
                loop {
                    match chars.next()? {
                        '\'' => break,
                        ch => cur.push(ch),
                    }
                }
                // `'\''`: a closing quote, an escaped quote, a new quote.
                if chars.peek() == Some(&'\\') {
                    chars.next();
                    if chars.next()? != '\'' {
                        return None;
                    }
                    cur.push('\'');
                    if chars.next()? != '\'' {
                        return None;
                    }
                    loop {
                        match chars.next()? {
                            '\'' => break,
                            ch => cur.push(ch),
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            c if c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || "-_./:".contains(c) => {
                in_word = true;
                cur.push(c);
            }
            _ => return None, // ; $ ` " \ | & ( ) < > and anything else
        }
    }
    if in_word {
        words.push(cur);
    }
    Some(words)
}

fn claude_owned(event: &str, e: &serde_json::Value) -> bool {
    if e.get("type").and_then(Value::as_str) != Some("http") {
        return false;
    }
    let Some(url) = e.get("url").and_then(Value::as_str) else { return false };
    let Some(rest) = url.strip_prefix("http://127.0.0.1:") else { return false };
    let Some((port, path)) = rest.split_once('/') else { return false };
    port.parse::<u16>().is_ok_and(|p| p > 0)
        && !port.starts_with('0')
        && path == format!("hook/claude/{event}?marker={MARKER}")
}

fn codex_owned(event: &str, e: &serde_json::Value) -> bool {
    if e.get("type").and_then(Value::as_str) != Some("command") {
        return false;
    }
    let Some(words) = e.get("command").and_then(Value::as_str).and_then(sh_words) else { return false };
    matches!(words.as_slice(),
        [exe, h, c, ev, flag, dir, marker]
            if exe.starts_with('/') && h == "hook" && c == "codex" && ev == event
               && flag == "--config-dir" && dir.starts_with('/') && marker == MARKER)
}

pub fn classify(event: &str, e: &serde_json::Value) -> Ownership {
    if claude_owned(event, e) || codex_owned(event, e) {
        Ownership::Owned
    } else if e.to_string().contains(MARKER) {
        Ownership::Suspicious
    } else {
        Ownership::Foreign
    }
}
```

Add to `json.rs` a group editor that keeps foreign entries as raw bytes:

```rust
pub enum GroupEdit {
    /// No owned entry: keep the group's bytes.
    Keep,
    /// Only owned entries: drop the group.
    Drop,
    /// Owned entries removed; every other key and entry keeps its bytes.
    Rewrite(Box<RawValue>),
}

/// `classify` is `entries::classify` bound to the event.
pub fn edit_group(group: &RawValue, classify: impl Fn(&serde_json::Value) -> bool) -> anyhow::Result<GroupEdit> {
    let mut obj = Obj::parse(group.get())?;
    let Some(list) = obj.get("hooks") else { return Ok(GroupEdit::Keep) };
    let items: Vec<Box<RawValue>> = serde_json::from_str(list.get())?;
    let mut kept = Vec::new();
    let mut dropped = 0;
    for item in items {
        let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(item.get())?;
        if classify(&v) {
            dropped += 1;
        } else {
            kept.push(item);
        }
    }
    Ok(match (dropped, kept.is_empty()) {
        (0, _) => GroupEdit::Keep,
        (_, true) => GroupEdit::Drop,
        _ => {
            obj.set("hooks", raw_text(render_array(&kept))?);
            GroupEdit::Rewrite(raw_text(obj.render())?)
        }
    })
}

pub fn render_array(items: &[Box<RawValue>]) -> String {
    if items.is_empty() {
        return "[]".into();
    }
    format!("[\n{}\n]", items.iter().map(|i| i.get().to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(",\n"))
}
```

Tests, in `entries.rs`:

```rust
    #[test]
    fn ownership_is_exact() {
        let c = ctx();
        let owned_c = claude_group(&c, "tok", "Stop", None, 1)["hooks"][0].clone();
        let owned_x = codex_group(&c, "Stop", 2)["hooks"][0].clone();
        assert_eq!(classify("Stop", &owned_c), Ownership::Owned);
        assert_eq!(classify("Stop", &owned_x), Ownership::Owned);
        assert_eq!(classify("PostToolUse", &owned_c), Ownership::Suspicious, "wrong event");
        for v in [
            serde_json::json!({"type": "command", "command": "echo acd-managed:v1 >> log"}),
            serde_json::json!({"type": "http", "url": "https://example.com/x?note=acd-managed:v1"}),
            serde_json::json!({"type": "http", "url": "http://10.0.0.1:47801/hook/claude/Stop?marker=acd-managed:v1"}),
            serde_json::json!({"type": "http", "url": "http://127.0.0.1:47801/hook/claude/Stop?marker=acd-managed:v1&x=1"}),
        ] {
            assert_eq!(classify("Stop", &v), Ownership::Suspicious, "{v}");
        }
        assert_eq!(classify("Stop", &serde_json::json!({"type": "command", "command": "say done"})), Ownership::Foreign);
    }

    #[test]
    fn sh_words_round_trips_the_quoting() {
        assert_eq!(sh_words(&format!("{} hook", sh_quote("/opt/my tools/a'b"))).unwrap(), vec!["/opt/my tools/a'b", "hook"]);
        for bad in ["a;b", "a $(x)", "a \"b\"", "a `b`", "a\\b", "'open"] {
            assert!(sh_words(bad).is_none(), "{bad}");
        }
    }
```

```rust
    #[test]
    fn a_mixed_group_keeps_foreign_entries_byte_exact() {
        let group = r#"{"matcher": "Bash",  "hooks": [{"type":"command",   "command":"guard.sh"}, {"type": "http", "url": "OWNED"}]}"#;
        let raw = RawValue::from_string(group.into()).unwrap();
        let GroupEdit::Rewrite(out) = edit_group(&raw, |v| v["url"] == "OWNED").unwrap() else { panic!() };
        assert!(out.get().contains(r#"{"type":"command",   "command":"guard.sh"}"#), "{}", out.get());
        assert!(out.get().contains(r#""matcher": "Bash""#) || out.get().contains(r#""matcher":"Bash""#));
        assert!(!out.get().contains("OWNED"));
    }
```

### Task 23: `acd hooks install|uninstall claude|codex`

Spec §6.1 "Installation" and "Token storage", §14 item 9, and Sol I4 to
I6: one acd writer at a time, change detection before the rename, a
journal for settings plus record, records keyed by canonical
destination, Git tracked/untracked/unknown, and a Git recheck before
every token-bearing write.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/acd/src/hooks_install/git.rs`, `crates/acd/src/hooks_install/journal.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/hooks_install/mod.rs` (lock, records, install, uninstall, token rewrite)
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/main.rs` (`Hooks` subcommand)
- Test: `crates/acd/tests/hooks_cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 20, 22.
- Produces:
  - `hooks_install::Flags { allow_tracked, allow_unknown_git }`.
  - `hooks_install::install(ctx: &Ctx, target: Target, flags: Flags) -> Result<Report>`,
    `hooks_install::uninstall(ctx: &Ctx, target: Target) -> Result<Report>`,
    `hooks_install::rewrite_claude_token(ctx: &Ctx, held: &WriterLock, token: &str, flags: Flags) -> Result<RewriteReport>`
    (the caller holds the writer lock; a second `flock` on a new
    descriptor in the same process would block)
    (`RewriteReport { updated: Vec<PathBuf>, failed: Vec<(PathBuf, String)> }`; Task 25 calls it).
  - `hooks_install::Record { version, files: BTreeMap<PathBuf, FileRecord> }` at
    `<config_dir>/hooks-installed.json` (0600), keyed by canonical destination;
    `hooks_install::recorded_claude_roots(config_dir) -> Vec<PathBuf>` (Task 28).
  - `hooks_install::lock_writers(config_dir) -> Result<WriterLock>` (`flock` on
    `<config_dir>/hooks.lock`).
  - `git::{GitStatus, status, check}`, `journal::{Journal, recover}`.
  - CLI: `acd hooks install claude|codex [--allow-tracked] [--allow-unknown-git]`,
    `acd hooks uninstall claude|codex`.

```rust
fn install(dir: &std::path::Path, home: &std::path::Path, target: &str, extra: &[&str]) -> std::process::Output {
    acd_home(dir, home).args(["hooks", "install", target]).args(extra).output().unwrap()
}

fn uninstall(dir: &std::path::Path, home: &std::path::Path, target: &str) -> std::process::Output {
    acd_home(dir, home).args(["hooks", "uninstall", target]).output().unwrap()
}

fn json_of(p: &std::path::Path) -> serde_json::Value {
    serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(p).unwrap()).unwrap()
}

fn owned_count(v: &serde_json::Value) -> usize {
    v["hooks"].as_object().map_or(0, |events| events.values()
        .flat_map(|groups| groups.as_array().into_iter().flatten())
        .flat_map(|g| g["hooks"].as_array().into_iter().flatten())
        .filter(|e| e.to_string().contains("acd-managed:v1"))
        .count())
}

const FOREIGN_CLAUDE: &str = r#"{
 "permissions": {"allow": ["Bash(npm test)"]},
 "hooks": {
  "Stop": [{"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "say done"}]}],
  "PreToolUse": [{"matcher": "Bash", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "guard.sh"}]}]
 },
 "model": "opus"
}"#;

#[test]
fn claude_install_writes_owned_entries_with_the_token_at_mode_0600() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    std::fs::write(dir.join("config.toml"), "hook_port = 47999\n").unwrap();
    let out = install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
    let settings = home.path().join(".claude/settings.json");
    assert_eq!(mode(&settings), 0o600);
    let v = json_of(&settings);
    let token = hook_token_of(&dir);
    for (event, timeout) in [("UserPromptSubmit", 1), ("PermissionRequest", 3610), ("PreToolUse", 1), ("PostToolUse", 1), ("Stop", 1), ("SessionEnd", 1)] {
        let e = &v["hooks"][event][0]["hooks"][0];
        assert_eq!(e["type"], "http", "{event}");
        assert_eq!(e["timeout"], timeout, "{event}");
        assert_eq!(e["url"], format!("http://127.0.0.1:47999/hook/claude/{event}?marker=acd-managed:v1"));
        assert_eq!(e["headers"]["X-Acd-Token"], token.as_str());
        assert_eq!(e["headers"]["X-Herdr-Pane"], "$HERDR_PANE_ID");
        assert_eq!(e["allowedEnvVars"], serde_json::json!(["HERDR_PANE_ID", "HERDR_SOCKET_PATH", "CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT"]));
    }
    assert_eq!(v["hooks"]["PreToolUse"][0]["matcher"], "AskUserQuestion");
    assert!(v["hooks"].get("SessionStart").is_none(), "Claude sends no http SessionStart (gate 2)");
    assert_eq!(mode(&dir.join("hooks-installed.json")), 0o600);
    for o in [&out.stdout, &out.stderr] {
        assert!(!String::from_utf8_lossy(o).contains(&token), "the token is never printed");
    }
}

#[test]
fn install_twice_then_uninstall_is_identity() {
    for (target, rel, original) in [
        ("claude", ".claude/settings.json", FOREIGN_CLAUDE),
        ("codex", ".codex/hooks.json", r#"{"hooks": {"Stop": [{"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "notify"}]}]}}"#),
    ] {
        let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
        let path = home.path().join(rel);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(&path, original).unwrap();
        assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), target, &[]).status.success());
        assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), target, &[]).status.success());
        let v = json_of(&path);
        assert_eq!(owned_count(&v), if target == "claude" { 6 } else { 5 }, "{target}: one entry per event");
        let out = uninstall(&dir, home.path(), target);
        assert!(out.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
        assert_eq!(json_of(&path), serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(original).unwrap(), "{target}");
    }
}

fn foreign_entries_keep_their_order_and_bytes() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let path = home.path().join(".claude/settings.json");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(&path, FOREIGN_CLAUDE).unwrap();

    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();

    let v = json_of(&path);
    assert_eq!(v["hooks"]["Stop"][0]["hooks"][0]["command"], "say done", "foreign group first");
    assert_eq!(v["hooks"]["PreToolUse"][0]["matcher"], "Bash");
    assert!(text.find("\"permissions\"").unwrap() < text.find("\"hooks\"").unwrap());
    assert!(text.find("\"hooks\"").unwrap() < text.find("\"model\"").unwrap());
}

#[test]
fn refuses_symlink_and_tracked_settings() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let claude = home.path().join(".claude");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&claude).unwrap();
    let real = home.path().join("dotfiles-settings.json");
    std::fs::write(&real, "{}").unwrap();
    std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&real, claude.join("settings.json")).unwrap();
    let out = install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    assert!(!out.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("symlink"));
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(&real).unwrap(), "{}", "untouched");

    std::fs::remove_file(claude.join("settings.json")).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(claude.join("settings.json"), "{}").unwrap();
    let git = |args: &[&str]| std::process::Command::new("git").current_dir(&claude).args(args)
        .env("HOME", home.path()).env("GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM", "1").env("GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL", "/dev/null")
        .env("GIT_AUTHOR_NAME", "t").env("GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL", "t@t").env("GIT_COMMITTER_NAME", "t").env("GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL", "t@t")
        .output().unwrap();
    git(&["init", "-q"]);
    git(&["add", "settings.json"]);
    let out = install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    assert!(!out.status.success());
    let err = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr);
    assert!(err.contains("tracked by Git") && err.contains("--allow-tracked"), "{err}");
    assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &["--allow-tracked"]).status.success());
}

#[test]
fn uninstall_survives_claude_reformatting() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let path = home.path().join(".claude/settings.json");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(&path, FOREIGN_CLAUDE).unwrap();
    install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    // Claude rewrites the whole file (indent 2) when it persists a rule (gate 2e).
    let mut v = json_of(&path);
    v["permissions"]["allow"].as_array_mut().unwrap().push("Bash(touch /tmp/*)".into());
    std::fs::write(&path, serde_json::to_string_pretty(&v).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert!(uninstall(&dir, home.path(), "claude").status.success());
    let mut want: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(FOREIGN_CLAUDE).unwrap();
    want["permissions"]["allow"].as_array_mut().unwrap().push("Bash(touch /tmp/*)".into());
    assert_eq!(json_of(&path), want);
}

#[test]
fn a_changed_config_root_gets_its_own_record() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let a = home.path().join("claude-a");
    let b = home.path().join("claude-b");
    for root in [&a, &b] {
        assert!(acd_home(&dir, home.path()).env("CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR", root).args(["hooks", "install", "claude"]).status().unwrap().success());
    }
    let record: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("hooks-installed.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(record["files"].as_object().unwrap().len(), 2);
    assert!(acd_home(&dir, home.path()).env("CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR", &b).args(["hooks", "uninstall", "claude"]).status().unwrap().success());
    assert!(!b.join("settings.json").exists(), "acd created it");
    assert_eq!(owned_count(&json_of(&a.join("settings.json"))), 6, "the other root is untouched");
}

#[cfg(debug_assertions)]
#[test]
fn a_record_write_failure_is_repaired_by_the_journal() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let path = home.path().join(".claude/settings.json");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(&path, FOREIGN_CLAUDE).unwrap();
    let out = acd_home(&dir, home.path()).env("ACD_TEST_FAIL_AT", "record-write").args(["hooks", "install", "claude"]).output().unwrap();
    assert!(!out.status.success());
    assert!(dir.join("hooks-journal.json").exists());
    assert_eq!(owned_count(&json_of(&path)), 6, "settings were written");
    // The next command recovers the record, so uninstall restores the original.
    assert!(uninstall(&dir, home.path(), "claude").status.success());
    assert_eq!(json_of(&path), serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(FOREIGN_CLAUDE).unwrap());
    assert!(!dir.join("hooks-journal.json").exists());
}

#[cfg(debug_assertions)]
#[test]
fn an_unknown_git_status_is_refused_without_the_flag() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let run = |extra: &[&str]| acd_home(&dir, home.path())
        .env("ACD_TEST_GIT", "/nonexistent/git").args(["hooks", "install", "claude"]).args(extra).output().unwrap();
    let out = run(&[]);
    assert!(!out.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("--allow-unknown-git"));
    assert!(run(&["--allow-unknown-git"]).status.success());
}

#[test]
fn a_deleted_but_still_tracked_settings_file_is_refused() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let claude = home.path().join(".claude");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&claude).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(claude.join("settings.json"), "{}").unwrap();
    let git = |args: &[&str]| std::process::Command::new("git").current_dir(&claude).args(args)
        .env("HOME", home.path()).env("GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM", "1").env("GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL", "/dev/null")
        .output().unwrap();
    git(&["init", "-q"]);
    git(&["add", "settings.json"]);
    std::fs::remove_file(claude.join("settings.json")).unwrap();
    let out = install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    assert!(!out.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("tracked by Git"));
}

#[test]
fn entries_that_only_mention_the_marker_are_left_alone() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let path = home.path().join(".claude/settings.json");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
    let foreign = r#"{"hooks": {"Stop": [{"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "echo acd-managed:v1 >> /tmp/log"}]}]}}"#;
    std::fs::write(&path, foreign).unwrap();
    let out = install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("unexpected"), "warned");
    assert!(uninstall(&dir, home.path(), "claude").status.success());
    assert_eq!(json_of(&path), serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(foreign).unwrap(), "never removed");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Git status (`hooks_install/git.rs`, D23)**

```rust
//! Whether Git tracks a settings file: tracked, untracked or unknown.
//! Unknown (git missing or failing) is refused unless the user says
//! otherwise (Sol I6).
use std::path::Path;
use std::process::{Command, Stdio};

use anyhow::{Result, bail};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum GitStatus {
    Tracked,
    Untracked,
    Unknown(String),
}

fn git_program() -> String {
    #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    if let Ok(p) = std::env::var("ACD_TEST_GIT") {
        return p;
    }
    "git".into()
}

/// Works for an absent file too: a deleted file that is still in the
/// index counts as tracked.
pub fn status(path: &Path) -> GitStatus {
    let Some(name) = path.file_name() else { return GitStatus::Unknown("no file name".into()) };
    let mut dir = path.parent().map(Path::to_path_buf).unwrap_or_default();
    let mut rel = std::path::PathBuf::from(name);
    while !dir.is_dir() {
        let Some(parent) = dir.parent() else { return GitStatus::Untracked };
        rel = std::path::PathBuf::from(dir.file_name().unwrap_or_default()).join(rel);
        dir = parent.to_path_buf();
    }
    let out = Command::new(git_program())
        .arg("-C").arg(&dir)
        .args(["ls-files", "--error-unmatch", "--"]).arg(&rel)
        .env("GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT", "0")
        .stdin(Stdio::null())
        .output();
    match out {
        Err(e) => GitStatus::Unknown(format!("git could not run: {e}")),
        Ok(o) if o.status.success() => GitStatus::Tracked,
        Ok(o) => {
            let err = String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stderr);
            match o.status.code() {
                Some(1) => GitStatus::Untracked,
                Some(128) if err.contains("not a git repository") => GitStatus::Untracked,
                _ => GitStatus::Unknown(err.lines().next().unwrap_or("git failed").to_owned()),
            }
        }
    }
}

pub fn check(path: &Path, allow_tracked: bool, allow_unknown: bool) -> Result<()> {
    match status(path) {
        GitStatus::Untracked => Ok(()),
        GitStatus::Tracked if allow_tracked => Ok(()),
        GitStatus::Tracked => bail!(
            "{} is tracked by Git, and the hook token would be committed. Untrack it, or pass --allow-tracked",
            path.display()
        ),
        GitStatus::Unknown(_) if allow_unknown => Ok(()),
        GitStatus::Unknown(why) => bail!(
            "cannot tell whether {} is tracked by Git ({why}). Fix Git, or pass --allow-unknown-git",
            path.display()
        ),
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The journal (`hooks_install/journal.rs`)**

```rust
//! Settings and record updates are journaled (Sol I4): the journal names
//! the destination, its content hash before and after, and the record
//! entry that belongs to "after". Recovery applies the record only if the
//! settings file is in the "after" state.
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use anyhow::Result;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};

use super::{FileRecord, Record};

pub const JOURNAL_FILE: &str = "hooks-journal.json";

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Journal {
    pub destination: PathBuf,
    pub before: Option<String>,
    pub after: Option<String>,
    /// `None` means the record entry is removed (uninstall).
    pub record_after: Option<FileRecord>,
}

pub fn digest(text: Option<&str>) -> Option<String> {
    text.map(|t| Sha256::digest(t.as_bytes()).iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect())
}

pub fn begin(config_dir: &Path, j: &Journal) -> Result<()> {
    crate::secrets::write_secret(&config_dir.join(JOURNAL_FILE), &serde_json::to_string_pretty(j)?)
}

pub fn finish(config_dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {
    match std::fs::remove_file(config_dir.join(JOURNAL_FILE)) {
        Err(e) if e.kind() != std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Err(e.into()),
        _ => Ok(()),
    }
}

/// Run at the start of every installer command, under the writer lock.
pub fn recover(config_dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {
    let path = config_dir.join(JOURNAL_FILE);
    if path.symlink_metadata().is_err() {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let j: Journal = serde_json::from_str(&crate::secrets::read_secret(&path)?)?;
    let now = digest(super::read_settings(&j.destination)?.as_deref());
    if now == j.after {
        let mut record = Record::load(config_dir)?;
        match j.record_after {
            Some(r) => record.files.insert(j.destination.clone(), r),
            None => record.files.remove(&j.destination),
        };
        record.save(config_dir)?;
    } else if now != j.before {
        eprintln!(
            "warning: {} changed since an interrupted acd hooks command; its install record was left as it was",
            j.destination.display()
        );
    }
    finish(config_dir)
}
```
